# 4.1.4 Model

- [Würstchen: An Efficient Architecture for Large-Scale Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.00637): Compresses generation into a two-stage latent hierarchy, using a compact Stage C prior over low-dimensional image tokens and a separate decoder stack to cut text-to-image training and sampling cost.
- [Synthbuster: Towards Detection of Diffusion Model Generated Images](https://doi.org/10.1109/ojsp.2023.3337714): Builds a generated-image detector for diffusion outputs, reporting IEEE OJSP evidence on handcrafted forensic cues and classifier behavior across modern diffusion sources.
- [Stochastic Latent Talking Face Generation Toward Emotional Expressions and Head Poses](https://doi.org/10.1109/tcsvt.2023.3311039): Models talking-face generation as a stochastic latent process, separating emotional expression and head-pose variation so audio-driven faces can sample diverse plausible motions.
- [Autoencoder-Based Collaborative Attention GAN for Multi-Modal Image Synthesis](https://doi.org/10.1109/tmm.2023.3274990): Uses autoencoder representations and collaborative attention inside a GAN to fuse multimodal inputs for image synthesis, with IEEE TMM experiments over paired synthesis settings.
- [Multi-Sentence Complementarily Generation for Text-to-Image Synthesis](https://doi.org/10.1109/tmm.2023.3297769): Conditions text-to-image GAN generation on complementary multi-sentence descriptions rather than a single caption, using sentence-level semantic aggregation to improve fine-grained synthesis on captioned image benchmarks.
- [New Job, New Gender? Measuring the Social Bias in Image Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.00763): Proposes BiasPainter, which edits seed portraits with gender-, race-, and age-neutral prompts across professions, activities, objects, and traits, then measures demographic changes in six image generators with 90.8% human-validated bias-detection accuracy.
- [Q-Refine: A Perceptual Quality Refiner for AI-Generated Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.01117): Adds an IQA-guided post-refiner for AI-generated images, routing low-, medium-, and high-quality samples through separate perceptual pipelines to improve fidelity and aesthetics without degrading already strong outputs.
- [Instruct-Imagen: Image Generation with Multi-modal Instruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.01952): Fine-tunes a text-to-image diffusion model with retrieval-augmented multimodal context and task instructions that unify text, edge, style, and subject cues, matching or exceeding task-specific generators and generalizing to unseen tasks.
- [Improving Diffusion-Based Image Synthesis with Context Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.02015): Introduces ConPreDiff, which attaches a context decoder during training so denoising blocks predict neighborhood tokens, features, or pixels; the decoder is removed at inference and improves unconditional generation, text-to-image generation, and inpainting, including MS-COCO zero-shot FID 6.21.
- [Bring Metric Functions into Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.02414): Proposes Cas-DM, a cascaded DDPM backbone whose second module predicts clean images so LPIPS-style metric losses can train diffusion models effectively, improving FID, sFID, and IS on standard image-generation benchmarks.
- [Progressive Knowledge Distillation Of Stable Diffusion XL Using Layer Level Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.02677): Progressively distills Stable Diffusion XL with layer-level losses, transferring intermediate denoising representations rather than only matching final images.
- [Attack-Resilient Image Watermarking Using Stable Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.04247): Embeds robust image watermarks through a Stable Diffusion based generation pipeline, targeting resistance to common image transformations and removal attacks.
- [PIXART-δ: Fast and Controllable Image Generation with Latent Consistency Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.05252): Combines PixArt-alpha with latent consistency distillation and a ControlNet-Transformer, generating 1024px images in 2-4 steps while retaining fine-grained conditioning and low-memory inference.
- [Parrot: Pareto-optimal Multi-Reward Reinforcement Learning Framework for Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.05675): Uses batch-wise Pareto selection to jointly optimize a T2I model and prompt-expansion network across aesthetics, preference, text-image alignment, and sentiment rewards, avoiding manual reward-weight tuning.
- [EraseDiff: Erasing Data Influence in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.05779): Provides a diffusion unlearning method that suppresses selected training-data influence while preserving the generator's remaining image synthesis behavior.
- [PALP: Prompt Aligned Personalization of Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.06105): Personalizes a T2I model for a single complex prompt using an added score-distillation sampling term, improving subject fidelity while preserving prompt alignment in single-shot, multi-shot, and multi-subject settings.
- [ViSAGe: A Global-Scale Analysis of Visual Stereotypes in Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.06310): Audits nationality-based visual stereotypes across 135 identities in T2I outputs, using ViSAGe to measure global stereotype behavior in generated images.
- [Seek for Incantations: Towards Accurate Text-to-Image Diffusion Synthesis through Prompt Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.06345): Studies prompt-engineering strategies for text-to-image diffusion and identifies prompt forms that improve semantic accuracy without changing model weights.
- [HieraFashDiff: Hierarchical Fashion Design with Multi-stage Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.07450): Uses a multi-stage hierarchical diffusion pipeline for fashion design, decomposing garment generation into coarse layout and finer visual-detail stages.
- [EmoTalker: Emotionally Editable Talking Face Generation via Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.08049): Conditions a diffusion talking-face generator on editable emotion controls, separating identity, lip motion, and affect so generated faces can be steered after input selection.
- [SCoFT: Self-Contrastive Fine-Tuning for Equitable Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.08053): Fine-tunes image generators with self-contrastive objectives that counter demographic imbalance, targeting more equitable text-to-image outputs without requiring new labeled fairness datasets.
- [Adversarial Supervision Makes Layout-to-Image Diffusion Models Thrive](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.08815): Adds adversarial supervision to layout-conditioned diffusion so generated objects follow specified spatial layouts more faithfully than reconstruction-only training.
- [MM-Interleaved: Interleaved Image-Text Generative Modeling via Multi-modal Feature Synchronizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.10208): Trains interleaved image-text generation with a multimodal feature synchronizer, aligning visual and textual streams so the model can produce coherent mixed-modality sequences.
- [A Simple Latent Diffusion Approach for Panoptic Segmentation and Mask Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.10227): Recasts panoptic segmentation and mask inpainting as latent diffusion tasks, using a simple shared formulation for dense masks and missing-region synthesis.
- [On Mitigating Stability-Plasticity Dilemma in CLIP-guided Image Morphing via Geodesic Distillation Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.10526): Adds a geodesic distillation loss to CLIP-guided image morphing, preserving source-target stability while allowing semantic plasticity during the morph trajectory.
- [Sat2Scene: 3D Urban Scene Generation from Satellite Images with Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.10786): Uses diffusion to synthesize 3D urban scenes from satellite imagery, translating overhead observations into structured scene content rather than generic 2D images.
- [Mastering Text-to-Image Diffusion: Recaptioning, Planning, and Generating with Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.11708): Uses multimodal LLMs to recaption prompts, plan visual content, and guide generation, improving text-to-image composition through explicit planning before diffusion sampling.
- [EmerDiff: Emerging Pixel-level Semantic Knowledge in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.11739): Analyzes pixel-level semantic knowledge inside diffusion models and uses that internal signal to explain or steer generation without external segmentation labels.
- [Large-scale Reinforcement Learning for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.12244): Scales reinforcement learning for diffusion generators, optimizing image samples with learned or human-aligned rewards while retaining the pretrained diffusion backbone.
- [CCA: collaborative competitive agents for image editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.13011): Frames image editing as collaboration and competition between editing agents, using iterative proposals and critiques to improve localized edits.
- [Scaling Up to Excellence: Practicing Model Scaling for Photo-Realistic Image Restoration In the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.13627): Studies scaling laws for real-world image restoration models, showing how larger restoration backbones and data improve photorealistic recovery in uncontrolled images.
- [Deconstructing Denoising Diffusion Models for Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.14404): Dissects which denoising-diffusion components produce useful self-supervised visual representations, making it adjacent analysis of diffusion backbones rather than a new T2I model.
- [Taiyi-Diffusion-XL: Advancing Bilingual Text-to-Image Generation with Large Vision-Language Model Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.14688): Extends SDXL-style text-to-image generation for bilingual Chinese-English prompts with large vision-language model support for captioning and alignment data.
- [IntentTuner: An Interactive Framework for Integrating Human Intentions in Fine-tuning Text-to-Image Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.15559): Fine-tunes T2I generators with interactive human-intent feedback, turning user preference signals into model updates for more controllable visual outputs.
- [FreeStyle: Free Lunch for Text-guided Style Transfer using Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.15636): Performs text-guided style transfer by reusing pretrained diffusion features and guidance, avoiding a separate style-transfer training stage.
- [StableIdentity: Inserting Anybody Into Anywhere at First Sight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.15975): Personalizes identity insertion from a first-sight reference, adapting a diffusion generator so a target person can be placed into new scenes while preserving identity.
- [Spatial-Aware Latent Initialization for Controllable Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.16157): Initializes diffusion latents with spatial structure so object positions and layouts are better preserved during controllable image generation.
- [Pick-and-Draw: Training-free Semantic Guidance for Text-to-Image Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.16762): Provides training-free semantic guidance for personalized T2I generation, selecting and steering subject-relevant signals at inference rather than fine-tuning the generator.
- [StrokeNUWA: Tokenizing Strokes for Vector Graphic Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.17093): Tokenizes strokes for vector-graphics synthesis.
- [Motion Guidance: Diffusion-Based Image Editing with Differentiable Motion Estimators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.18085): Steers diffusion editing with gradients from an off-the-shelf optical-flow estimator, letting users specify dense pixel motion fields for layout, pose, shape, and object-position edits without training.
- [A Single Simple Patch is All You Need for AI-generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.01123): Tests whether a simple learned patch cue can expose AI-generated images, contributing a lightweight generated-image detection baseline.
- [Cross-view Masked Diffusion Transformers for Person Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.01516): Uses cross-view masked diffusion transformers for controllable person image synthesis.
- [Plug-and-Play image restoration with Stochastic deNOising REgularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.01779): Adds stochastic denoising regularization to plug-and-play restoration, using a pretrained denoiser as an image prior for inverse problems.
- [Improving Diffusion Models for Inverse Problems Using Optimal Posterior Covariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.02149): Refines diffusion posterior sampling for inverse problems by estimating an optimal posterior covariance, improving restoration and reconstruction quality.
- [DiffEditor: Boosting Accuracy and Flexibility on Diffusion-Based Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.02583): Improves diffusion image editing with a more flexible editing formulation that targets higher edit accuracy while preserving unrelated regions.
- [Training-Free Consistent Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.03286): Enforces consistency across training-free T2I generations, using inference-time controls rather than model fine-tuning to keep subjects or layouts stable.
- [InstanceDiffusion: Instance-Level Control for Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.03290): Adds instance-level conditioning to diffusion generation so each object can be controlled by its own spatial and semantic signal within one image.
- [Constrained Synthesis with Projected Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.03559): Uses projection steps during diffusion sampling to satisfy explicit constraints, turning pretrained generative models into constrained image synthesizers.
- [QuEST: Low-Bit Diffusion Model Quantization via Efficient Selective Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.03666): Quantizes diffusion models with selective fine-tuning, reducing bit width while preserving image-generation quality better than uniform low-bit conversion.
- [Noise Map Guidance: Inversion with Spatial Context for Real Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.04625): Guides DDIM inversion with spatial noise maps so real-image edits keep background context while changing intended regions.
- [Improved off-policy training of diffusion samplers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.05098): Improves diffusion sampler training with off-policy data, making learned samplers reuse trajectories beyond the current policy distribution.
- [Get What You Want, Not What You Don't: Image Content Suppression for Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.05375): Suppresses unwanted concepts in T2I diffusion outputs through negative content control, separating desired prompt content from explicitly excluded visual elements.
- [MIGC: Multi-Instance Generation Controller for Text-to-Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.05408): Controls multiple objects in one T2I image with instance-specific boxes, attributes, and attention modulation so generated scenes follow multi-instance layouts.
- [Scalable Diffusion Models with State Space Backbone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.05608): Replaces attention-heavy diffusion backbones with state-space components to scale visual generation with lower sequence-processing cost.
- [Particle Denoising Diffusion Sampler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.06320): Uses multiple particles during denoising to better approximate posterior sampling and diversify diffusion trajectories.
- [PRDP: Proximal Reward Difference Prediction for Large-Scale Reward Finetuning of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.08714): Fine-tunes diffusion models at scale with proximal reward-difference prediction, using reward comparisons to improve generated-image preference alignment.
- [Self-Play Fine-Tuning of Diffusion Models for Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.10210): Uses self-play between generated candidates to construct preference signals for fine-tuning T2I diffusion models without relying only on static human labels.
- [Make a Cheap Scaling: A Self-Cascade Diffusion Model for Higher-Resolution Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.10491): Adapts generators to higher resolution with a self-cascade diffusion design, reusing a base model to reduce the cost of scaling image size.
- [Universal Prompt Optimizer for Safe Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.10882): Optimizes prompts for safety before T2I generation, steering the model away from unsafe visual concepts while preserving benign intent.
- [DiLightNet: Fine-grained Lighting Control for Diffusion-based Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.11929): Adds fine-grained lighting controls to diffusion generation, conditioning images on illumination direction and appearance rather than only text.
- [Direct Consistency Optimization for Robust Customization of Text-to-Image Diffusion models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.12004): Optimizes customized T2I diffusion models with a direct consistency objective so personalized subjects remain robust across prompts and sampling settings.
- [Groot: Adversarial Testing for Generative Text-to-Image Models with Tree-based Semantic Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.12100): Generates tree-structured semantic prompt transformations to adversarially test T2I generators for compositional and safety failures.
- [FiT: Flexible Vision Transformer for Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.12376): Uses a flexible ViT diffusion backbone that treats images as variable-resolution and variable-aspect token grids, enabling generation beyond the training resolution layout.
- [Robust-Wide: Robust Watermarking against Instruction-driven Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.12688): Designs watermarks that survive instruction-driven image editing, evaluating robustness against edits that normally erase provenance signals.
- [Diffusion Posterior Sampling is Computationally Intractable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.12727): Gives a computational hardness analysis of exact diffusion posterior sampling, clarifying limits of diffusion-based inverse problem solvers.
- [A User-Friendly Framework for Generating Model-Preferred Prompts in Text-to-Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.12760): Introduces the CFP coarse-to-fine prompt dataset and UF-FGTG prompt refiner, training a text generator with T2I image-related losses so novice prompts are rewritten into fine-grained prompts preferred by image generators.
- [RealCompo: Dynamic Equilibrium between Realism and Compositionality Improves Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.12908): Balances realism and compositionality during T2I diffusion training, dynamically adjusting supervision so object layouts and photorealistic appearance improve together instead of trading off.
- [ToDo: Token Downsampling for Efficient Generation of High-Resolution Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.13573): Uses token downsampling for efficient high-resolution image generation.
- [SDXL-Lightning: Progressive Adversarial Diffusion Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.13929): Distills SDXL into a few-step generator with progressive adversarial diffusion distillation, preserving high-resolution text-to-image quality at 1, 2, 4, and 8 inference steps.
- [D-Flow: Differentiating through Flows for Controlled Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.14017): Backpropagates control losses through normalizing-flow or continuous-flow generators, enabling gradient-based optimization of generated samples for controllable image synthesis.
- [T-Stitch: Accelerating Sampling in Pre-Trained Diffusion Models with Trajectory Stitching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.14167): Speeds pretrained diffusion sampling by stitching together denoising trajectory segments from different timestep schedules, reducing steps without retraining the base model.
- [Fine-Tuning of Continuous-Time Diffusion Models as Entropy-Regularized Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.15194): Formulates continuous-time diffusion fine-tuning as entropy-regularized stochastic control, connecting reward-guided generator adaptation with principled trajectory optimization.
- [Don't Start From Scratch: Behavioral Refinement via Interpolant-based Policy Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.16075): Refines an existing generative policy through interpolant-based diffusion, using behavior-preserving trajectories to adapt model outputs without retraining from scratch.
- [Stochastic Conditional Diffusion Models for Robust Semantic Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.16506): Makes semantic image synthesis robust to noisy label maps by diffusing discrete labels, using a class-wise noise schedule, and simulating human input errors during evaluation.
- [Cross-Modal Contextualized Diffusion Models for Text-Guided Visual Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.16627): Conditions diffusion on cross-modal contextual tokens, letting text-guided generation and editing reuse surrounding visual context rather than treating the prompt as the only control signal.
- [Preserving Fairness Generalization in Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.17229): Studies fairness generalization in deepfake detectors and trains detection models to reduce demographic performance gaps when forged-image distributions shift.
- [SDDGR: Stable Diffusion-Based Deep Generative Replay for Class Incremental Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.17323): Uses Stable Diffusion to synthesize replay images for class-incremental object detection, replacing stored old data with generated examples that preserve earlier categories.
- [DiffuseKronA: A Parameter Efficient Fine-tuning Method for Personalized Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.17412): Personalizes diffusion models with Kronecker-product adaptation matrices, reducing fine-tuning parameters while retaining subject identity across prompts.
- [Label-Noise Robust Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.17517): Trains diffusion generators under noisy class or caption labels by modifying the learning objective so corrupted supervision has less influence on the denoising model.
- [Structure-Guided Adversarial Training of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.17563): Adds structure-aware adversarial perturbations during diffusion training, improving robustness of generated images to layout and semantic structure shifts.
- [Leveraging Representations from Intermediate Encoder-blocks for Synthetic Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.19091): Detects synthetic images by using intermediate encoder-block features, capturing generator artifacts that are weaker in final-layer representations.
- [DistriFusion: Distributed Parallel Inference for High-Resolution Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.19481): Parallelizes high-resolution diffusion inference across devices by reusing stale patch activations, accelerating large-image generation while keeping cross-patch consistency.
- [RealCustom: Narrowing Real Text Word for Real-Time Open-Domain Text-to-Image Customization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.00483): Performs real-time open-domain customization by narrowing reference-image semantics into compact text-word embeddings, avoiding heavy per-subject fine-tuning.
- [Learning a Physical-Aware Diffusion Model Based on Transformer for Underwater Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.01497): Combines a transformer diffusion backbone with underwater imaging priors so enhancement respects color attenuation, scattering, and physical degradation patterns.
- [Theoretical Insights for Diffusion Guidance: A Case Study for Gaussian Mixture Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.01639): Analyzes classifier-free and related diffusion guidance in Gaussian-mixture settings, explaining when guidance improves or distorts sample distributions.
- [OOTDiffusion: Outfitting Fusion based Latent Diffusion for Controllable Virtual Try-on](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.01779): Uses outfitting-fusion latent diffusion to preserve garment texture and body pose in controllable virtual try-on, separating clothing condition from person identity.
- [ViewDiff: 3D-Consistent Image Generation with Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.01807): Adds multi-view consistency to text-to-image diffusion, aligning generated views so outputs behave like one coherent 3D object or scene.
- [Stable Diffusion 3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.03206): Introduces rectified-flow transformers for high-resolution text-to-image generation, combining scalable architecture, flow matching, and multimodal conditioning.
- [Towards Understanding Cross and Self-Attention in Stable Diffusion for Text-Guided Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.03431): Dissects cross-attention and self-attention roles in Stable Diffusion editing, identifying which maps control text alignment, structure preservation, and local edit leakage.
- [FLAME Diffuser: Wildfire Image Synthesis using Mask Guided Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.03463): Generates wildfire imagery with mask-guided diffusion, using spatial fire masks to control where flames and smoke appear for synthetic emergency-vision data.
- [NoiseCollage: A Layout-Aware Text-to-Image Diffusion Model Based on Noise Cropping and Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.03485): Controls layout by cropping and merging noise regions before denoising, letting a pretrained T2I diffusion model place multiple objects according to spatial boxes.
- [Unifying Generation and Compression: Ultra-low bitrate Image Coding Via Multi-stage Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.03736): Treats ultra-low-bitrate image coding as generative reconstruction, using a multi-stage transformer to transmit compact semantics and synthesize perceptually faithful images at decode time.
- [PixArt-Σ: Weak-to-Strong Training of Diffusion Transformer for 4K Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.04692): Scales diffusion transformers for high-resolution text-to-image generation through weak-to-strong training.
- [Face2Diffusion for Fast and Editable Face Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.05094): Maps a face reference into diffusion personalization tokens, enabling fast identity-preserving face generation that remains editable by text.
- [CogView3: Finer and Faster Text-to-Image Generation via Relay Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.05121): Introduces relay diffusion for text-to-image generation, using staged denoising relays to improve detail and speed over earlier CogView models.
- [ELLA: Equip Diffusion Models with LLM for Enhanced Semantic Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.05135): Adds an LLM-based semantic adapter to diffusion models, converting long or compositional prompts into conditioning signals that improve image-text alignment.
- [Improving Diffusion Models for Authentic Virtual Try-on in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.05139): Adapts diffusion virtual try-on for unconstrained photos, improving garment warping, person preservation, and in-the-wild realism.
- [Towards Effective Usage of Human-Centric Priors in Diffusion Models for Text-based Human Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.05239): Injects pose, shape, and other human-centric priors into text-based human image diffusion so generated bodies follow plausible anatomy and clothing structure.
- [Diffusion Lens: Interpreting Text Encoders in Text-to-Image Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.05846): Probes T2I text encoders through diffusion outputs, revealing how prompt tokens, layers, and embeddings steer visual attributes during generation.
- [Decoupled Data Consistency with Diffusion Purification for Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.06054): Separates measurement consistency from diffusion purification in restoration, combining inverse-problem constraints with a generative prior while avoiding over-correction.
- [DivCon: Divide and Conquer for Complex Numerical and Spatial Reasoning in Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.06400): Decomposes prompts with numerical and spatial relations into smaller generation constraints, improving T2I handling of counts, positions, and object relations.
- [Distribution-Aware Data Expansion with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.06741): Expands training data with diffusion samples chosen to match target distribution gaps, using generative augmentation to improve downstream visual model coverage.
- [SELMA: Learning and Merging Skill-Specific Text-to-Image Experts with Auto-Generated Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.06952): Trains skill-specific T2I experts on automatically generated data and merges them, letting one model combine specialized capabilities without manual expert datasets.
- [BrushNet: A Plug-and-Play Image Inpainting Model with Decomposed Dual-Branch Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.06976): Adds a decomposed dual-branch diffusion module for inpainting, separately modeling masked content and preserved context as a plug-and-play component.
- [Text-to-Image Diffusion Models are Great Sketch-Photo Matchmakers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.07214): Uses T2I diffusion priors for sketch-photo matching, exploiting generated visual correspondences to bridge sparse sketches and real images.
- [Frequency-Aware Deepfake Detection: Improving Generalizability through Frequency Space Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.07240): Improves deepfake detector transfer by learning frequency-domain artifacts, reducing reliance on source-specific pixel cues.
- [Efficient Diffusion Model for Image Restoration by Residual Shifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.07319): Restores degraded images by shifting residuals through a compact diffusion process, reducing restoration cost while keeping generative detail recovery.
- [Genuine Knowledge from Practice: Diffusion Test-Time Adaptation for Video Adverse Weather Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.07684): Performs diffusion test-time adaptation for adverse-weather removal, updating restoration behavior on each video using practice-time signals from the observed sequence.
- [Bridging Different Language Models and Generative Vision Models for Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.07860): Aligns external language-model representations with generative vision models so richer prompt understanding can condition text-to-image diffusion.
- [Ambient Diffusion Posterior Sampling: Solving Inverse Problems with Diffusion Models trained on Corrupted Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.08728): Extends posterior sampling to ambient diffusion models trained on corrupted observations, solving inverse problems without requiring clean-image pretraining data.
- [NoiseDiffusion: Correcting Noise for Image Interpolation with Diffusion Models beyond Spherical Linear Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.08840): Corrects latent noise trajectories for image interpolation, producing smoother semantic transitions than spherical linear interpolation in diffusion space.
- [DialogGen: Multi-modal Interactive Dialogue System for Multi-turn Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.08857): Supports multi-turn T2I generation by updating diffusion conditioning from dialogue history and multimodal user feedback, enabling iterative image refinement.
- [SemanticDraw: Towards Real-Time Interactive Content Creation from Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.09055): Turns diffusion generation into an interactive drawing system, updating semantic content in real time from user strokes and text controls.
- [Switch Diffusion Transformer: Synergizing Denoising Tasks with Sparse Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.09176): Uses sparse mixture-of-experts inside a diffusion transformer so different denoising tasks route to specialized experts while sharing one backbone.
- [PrompTHis: Visualizing the Process and Influence of Prompt Editing During Text-to-Image Creation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.09615): Analyzes how prompt edits change T2I outputs over time, exposing prompt-history effects that influence model behavior during image creation.
- [Glyph-ByT5: A Customized Text Encoder for Accurate Visual Text Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.09622): Replaces the T2I text encoder with a ByT5-based glyph encoder, improving accurate rendering of letters and words inside generated images.
- [SCP-Diff: Photo-Realistic Semantic Image Synthesis with Spatial-Categorical Joint Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.09638): Replaces the standard Gaussian inference prior in diffusion-based semantic image synthesis with spatial, categorical, and joint priors to reduce mask misalignment and malformed substructures.
- [SphereDiffusion: Spherical Geometry-Aware Distortion Resilient Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.10044): Builds diffusion on spherical geometry so panorama and omnidirectional images preserve global structure under projection distortion.
- [IMPRINT: Generative Object Compositing by Learning Identity-Preserving Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.10701): Learns identity-preserving object representations for compositing, inserting generated objects into scenes while maintaining the source object's appearance.
- [StableGarment: Garment-Centric Generation via Stable Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.10783): Centers Stable Diffusion conditioning on garment features, generating fashion images that preserve clothing texture, shape, and identity across poses.
- [OMG: Occlusion-friendly Personalized Multi-concept Generation in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.10983): Personalizes multiple concepts under occlusion by modeling concept interactions, reducing identity mixing when several learned subjects appear in one image.
- [Divide-and-Conquer Posterior Sampling for Denoising Diffusion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.11407): Splits posterior sampling with diffusion priors into smaller subproblems, improving inverse-problem reconstruction when global optimization is unstable.
- [CRS-Diff: Controllable Remote Sensing Image Generation With Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.11614): Generates remote-sensing images with controllable conditions such as land-cover semantics and spatial structure, adapting diffusion to overhead imagery.
- [Fast High-Resolution Image Synthesis with Latent Adversarial Diffusion Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.12015): Distills latent diffusion with adversarial objectives for fast high-resolution synthesis, reducing denoising steps while preserving fine image detail.
- [Consistency Model is an Effective Posterior Sample Approximation for Diffusion Inverse Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.12063): Uses consistency models distilled from probability-flow ODEs as posterior sample approximators for diffusion inverse solvers, improving neural-operator inverse tasks such as semantic-segmentation conditioned reconstruction.
- [Understanding and Improving Training-free Loss-based Diffusion Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.12404): Analyzes training-free loss guidance as an optimization process, identifies adversarial-gradient and slow-convergence failure modes, and adds corrective techniques validated on image and motion generation.
- [SC-Diff: 3D Shape Completion with Latent Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.12470): Encodes TSDF shapes into a discrete latent space jointly supervised by 2D images and partial 3D scans, enabling multimodal latent-diffusion shape completion with lower GPU memory use.
- [Tuning-Free Image Customization with Image and Text Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.12658): Edits specified image regions within seconds by blending UNet decoder self-attention, combining reference-image semantics with text-described attribute changes without fine-tuning.
- [AnimateDiff-Lightning: Cross-Model Diffusion Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.12706): Distills motion modules with progressive adversarial diffusion distillation across multiple base diffusion models, targeting few-step video generation with broader style compatibility.
- [DreamDA: Generative Data Augmentation with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.12803): Perturbs reverse diffusion trajectories from real training images to synthesize diverse classification data, then uses self-training to assign pseudo-labels for the generated samples.
- [You Only Sample Once: Taming One-Step Text-To-Image Synthesis by Self-Cooperative Diffusion GANs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.12931): Trains YOSO, a one-step diffusion-GAN generator, with self-cooperative adversarial smoothing, latent perceptual loss, a latent discriminator, and prior initialization for fast T2I synthesis.
- [FouriScale: A Frequency Perspective on Training-Free High-Resolution Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.12963): Replaces pretrained diffusion convolutions with dilation and low-pass operations plus padding-then-crop sampling to produce arbitrary-aspect high-resolution images without retraining.
- [When Do We Not Need Larger Vision Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.13043): Tests Scaling on Scales, running a frozen smaller ViT over multiple image scales, and shows this multi-scale inference can outperform larger ViTs on classification, segmentation, depth, MLLM, and robotics benchmarks.
- [Magic Fixup: Streamlining Photo Editing by Watching Dynamic Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.13044): Trains a diffusion editor from video frame pairs by warping a source frame toward a target with motion models that mimic user edits, preserving fine details while adapting lighting, context, and object interactions to a coarse edited layout.
- [Diffusion Model for Data-Driven Black-Box Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.13219): Reformulates structured black-box design optimization as conditional diffusion sampling, using abundant unlabeled designs plus small noisy-reward or pairwise-preference labels to generate near-optimal structured candidates.
- [DetDiffusion: Synergizing Generative and Perceptive Models for Enhanced Data Generation and Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.13304): Combines generative and perceptive models to produce data that improves downstream perception performance.
- [AGFSync: Leveraging AI-Generated Feedback for Preference Optimization in Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.13352): Runs an AI-only DPO loop where VLMs score generated images for style, coherence, and aesthetics, producing feedback data that improves SD and SDXL prompt-following and preference metrics.
- [ZigMa: A DiT-style Zigzag Mamba Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.13802): Inserts a zero-parameter Zigzag Mamba scan into stochastic-interpolant visual generators, preserving spatial continuity while improving speed and memory use over Transformer baselines.
- [Editing Massive Concepts in Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.13807): Proposes EMCID, a two-stage concept-editing method with dual self-distillation and closed-form multi-layer model editing, plus ICEB for testing up to 1,000 T2I concept edits.
- [DesignEdit: Multi-Layered Latent Decomposition and Fusion for Unified & Accurate Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.14487): Decomposes source-image latents into object and background layers, uses key-masking self-attention for inpainting, and fuses layers under instructions for multiple spatial editing tasks.
- [ReNoise: Real Image Inversion Through Iterative Noising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.14602): Improves real-image diffusion inversion by iteratively re-noising each inversion step and averaging model predictions, raising reconstruction accuracy while preserving text-driven editability.
- [Long-CLIP: Unlocking the Long-Text Capability of CLIP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.15378): Extends CLIP for long prompts with position-embedding stretching and primary-component feature matching, making detailed text descriptions usable in retrieval and T2I generation pipelines.
- [Finding needles in a haystack: A Black-Box Approach to Invisible Watermark Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.15955): Proposes WMD, a black-box and annotation-free detector that uses offset learning between a clean dataset and a reference set to expose arbitrary invisible watermarks without a watermark decoder.
- [Distilling Semantic Priors from SAM to Efficient Image Restoration Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.16368): Transfers SAM-derived semantic masks into small restoration networks through semantic-prior fusion, self-distillation, and a relation module, avoiding SAM at inference.
- [SDXS: Real-Time One-Step Latent Diffusion Models with Image Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.16627): Combines U-Net and decoder distillation with one-step feature matching and score distillation to reduce image-conditioned latent diffusion latency.
- [Be Yourself: Bounded Attention for Multi-Subject Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.16990): Introduces a training-free bounded-attention sampler that limits semantic leakage across subjects, improving multi-subject prompt and layout faithfulness.
- [SD-DiT: Unleashing the Power of Self-Supervised Discrimination in Diffusion Transformer*](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.17004): Splits DiT training into teacher-student discriminative encoding and generative decoding, using PF-ODE noise pairs to improve convergence without mask-reconstruction mismatch.
- [DreamLIP: Language-Image Pre-training with Long Captions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.17007): Recaptions 30M images with detailed MLLM descriptions, samples sub-captions as positives, and aligns them with local patches through a grouping loss for fine-grained vision-language pretraining.
- [Continuous, Subject-Specific Attribute Control in T2I Models by Identifying Semantic Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.17064): Finds token-level directions in CLIP text embeddings that modulate subject-specific attributes, enabling continuous multi-attribute T2I control without changing the diffusion model.
- [Improving Text-to-Image Consistency via Automatic Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.17804): Uses OPT2I, an LLM-driven iterative prompt optimizer, to maximize prompt-image consistency scores while preserving image quality on MSCOCO and PartiPrompts.
- [AID: Attention Interpolation of Text-to-Image Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.17924): Interpolates inner and outer attention layers, fuses them with self-attention, and uses beta-distributed selection to make condition interpolation smoother and more faithful.
- [Bidirectional Consistency Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.18035): Trains one network to traverse probability-flow ODEs forward and backward, unifying fast generation, inversion, interpolation, and inpainting in consistency models.
- [Don't Look into the Dark: Latent Codes for Pluralistic Image Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.18186): Predicts discrete latent tokens only from visible regions with a restrictive partial encoder, infers missing tokens with a bidirectional Transformer, and synthesizes diverse large-mask inpaintings.
- [VersaT2I: Improving Text-to-Image Models with Versatile Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.18493): Decomposes T2I quality into aesthetics, alignment, geometry, and low-level rewards, fine-tunes LoRA specialists from high-quality generated samples, and gates them to avoid reward conflicts.
- [ObjectDrop: Bootstrapping Counterfactuals for Photorealistic Object Removal and Insertion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.18818): Captures before and after scenes for single-object removal, fine-tunes a diffusion editor to remove both objects and physical effects, then bootstraps synthetic counterfactual data for photorealistic object insertion.
- [MagicLens: Self-Supervised Image Retrieval with Open-Ended Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.19651): Trains open-ended instruction image retrieval from 36.7M web-mined image-instruction-image triplets, where foundation models verbalize implicit relations between images appearing on the same pages.
- [Detecting Origin Attribution for Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.19653): Attributes images to 12 T2I generators and studies which seeds, inference hyperparameters, image modifications, style cues, and high-frequency traces remain detectable.
- [Structure Matters: Tackling the Semantic Discrepancy in Diffusion Models for Image Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.19898): Introduces StrDiffusion, which uses time-dependent structure guidance and adaptive resampling to reduce semantic mismatch between masked and unmasked inpainting regions.
- [Relation Rectification in Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.20249): Adds a lightweight HGCN over relation words and object order to adjust text embeddings, correcting visual relations without updating the text encoder or diffusion model.
- [Latent Watermark: Inject and Detect Watermarks in Latent Diffusion Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.00230): Injects and detects watermarks in latent diffusion space with progressive training, weakening the image-quality versus robustness tradeoff under compression, blur, and other attacks.
- [Drag Your Noise: Interactive Point-based Editing via Diffusion Semantic Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.01050): Treats predicted U-Net noise as a semantic editor, applies point-based changes in one denoising step, and propagates them for faster, more stable diffusion dragging.
- [Uncovering the Text Embedding in Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.01154): Studies per-word embeddings, contextual correlations, and SVD directions in T2I text embeddings, exposing learning-free controls for semantic editing and attribute discovery.
- [Measuring Style Similarity in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.01292): Builds a style-descriptor dataset and retrieval framework to attribute generated-image style to training images, separating style similarity from semantic-content retrieval.
- [CosmicMan: A Text-to-Image Foundation Model for Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.01294): Trains a human-focused T2I model with the CosmicMan-HQ dataset, Annotate Anyone data flywheel, and decomposed-attention-refocusing to align dense body-structured descriptions with pixels.
- [Bigger is not Always Better: Scaling Properties of Latent Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.01367): Empirically studies text-to-image LDM size versus sampling budget, showing smaller models can outperform larger ones under fixed inference steps.
- [Linear Combination of Saved Checkpoints Makes Consistency and Diffusion Models Better](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.02241): Uses evolutionary-search coefficients to average saved checkpoints, cutting DM and CM training cost or improving generation quality and NFE at inference.
- [InstantStyle: Free Lunch towards Style-Preserving in Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.02733): Decouples reference-image style from content in diffusion feature space, giving tuning-free style-preserving T2I generation without per-image adapter weight tuning.
- [On the Scalability of Diffusion-based Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.02883): Ablates UNet and Transformer T2I backbones from 0.4B to 4B parameters and up to 600M images, finding cross-attention placement, transformer-block scaling, caption density, and data quality dominate efficient scaling.
- [Visual Autoregressive Modeling: Scalable Image Generation via Next-Scale Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.02905): Recasts image autoregression as coarse-to-fine next-scale prediction, letting GPT-style visual AR transformers beat DiT quality and speed on ImageNet while supporting inpainting, outpainting, and editing.
- [AIGIQA-20K: A Large Database for AI-Generated Image Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.03407): Provides 20,000 AI-generated images with 420,000 subjective ratings from 15 generators and benchmarks 16 AIGI quality models against human perceptual and alignment scores.
- [LCM-Lookahead for Encoder-based Text-to-Image Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.03620): Uses few-step latent consistency previews to backpropagate identity losses into encoder-based T2I personalization, improving face fidelity without sacrificing layout diversity or prompt alignment.
- [Reference-Based 3D-Aware Image Editing with Triplanes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.03632): Edits 3D-aware GAN triplane features through encoding, automatic localization, spatial disentanglement, and fusion learning, enabling reference-guided face, animal, clothing, and stylized edits.
- [CoMat: Aligning Text-to-Image Diffusion Model with Image-to-Text Concept Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.03653): Fine-tunes SDXL with image-to-text concept matching and attribute-concentration modules so ignored prompt tokens are revisited without human preference or image supervision.
- [Score identity Distillation: Exponentially Fast Distillation of Pretrained Diffusion Models for One-Step Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.04057): Distills pretrained diffusion models into one-step generators with score-identity losses over self-synthesized images, removing real-data and reverse-sampling requirements during distillation.
- [Dynamic Prompt Optimizing for Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.04095): Introduces Prompt Auto-Editing, using online reinforcement learning to choose per-word weights and injection timesteps that balance aesthetics, semantic consistency, and user preference.
- [Watermark-based Attribution of AI-Generated Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.04254): Studies user-level attribution for generated content by assigning distinct watermarks per service user and optimizing watermark sets with probabilistic detection and attribution bounds.
- [Diffusion-RWKV: Scaling RWKV-Like Architectures for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.04478): Adapts RWKV-style sequence modeling to conditional and unconditional diffusion, reducing spatial aggregation cost for high-resolution image generation without windowed attention.
- [Initno: Boosting Text-to-Image Diffusion Models via Initial Noise Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.04650): Optimizes initial diffusion noise using cross-attention response and self-attention conflict scores, steering T2I sampling toward prompt-faithful latent regions.
- [Gaussian Shading: Provable Performance-Lossless Image Watermarking for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.04956): Embeds training-free diffusion watermarks by mapping watermark codes to Gaussian latents, preserving generation quality while supporting semantic and attack-resistant extraction.
- [Towards More General Video-based Deepfake Detection through Facial Component Guided Adaptation for Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.05583): Adds a CLIP-encoder side-network decoder and facial-component guidance so video deepfake detection captures spatial-temporal and local facial forgery cues with parameter-efficient adaptation.
- [UniFL: Improve Latent Diffusion Model via Unified Feedback Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.05595): Combines perceptual, decoupled aesthetic, and adversarial feedback learning to improve LDM visual quality, preference appeal, and inference speed across SD1.5 and SDXL.
- [MarkPlugger: Generalizable Watermark Framework for Latent Diffusion Models Without Retraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.05607): Adds plug-and-play watermarking to latent diffusion models by identifying semantic-preserving watermark representations, avoiding model retraining across fast-changing LDM variants.
- [MoMA: Multimodal LLM Adapter for Fast Personalized Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.05674): Uses a multimodal LLM adapter to encode reference identity and prompt context for fast personalized T2I generation, reducing reliance on per-subject fine-tuning.
- [SwapAnything: Enabling Arbitrary Object Swapping in Personalized Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.05717): Enables arbitrary object replacement in personalized edits by disentangling source identity, target object cues, and background preservation inside a diffusion editing pipeline.
- [GeoSynth: Contextually-Aware High-Resolution Satellite Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.06637): Generates high-resolution satellite imagery with contextual conditioning, targeting geospatial scenes where surrounding land-use structure constrains plausible synthesis.
- [SafeGen: Mitigating Sexually Explicit Content Generation in Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.06666): Suppresses explicit T2I outputs by locating and editing safety-relevant model components, aiming to reduce unsafe generations while preserving benign prompt utility.
- [Disguised Copyright Infringement of Latent Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.06737): Shows latent diffusion can reproduce protected content through disguised prompts and analyzes copyright risk beyond direct memorization or exact prompt matching.
- [GoodDrag: Towards Good Practices for Drag Editing with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.07206): Distills practical design choices for point-based diffusion dragging, clarifying how guidance, masks, and optimization settings affect localized edit quality.
- [Object-Conditioned Energy-Based Attention Map Alignment in Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.07389): Aligns object-specific attention maps with energy-based guidance, improving object placement and relation faithfulness in T2I diffusion without broad retraining.
- [Taming Stable Diffusion for Text to 360{\deg} Panorama Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.07949): Adapts Stable Diffusion to text-conditioned 360-degree panorama synthesis, addressing wraparound consistency and wide-field composition beyond ordinary perspective images.
- [ControlNet++: Improving Conditional Controls with Efficient Consistency Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.07987): Fine-tunes controllable diffusion with pixel-level cycle-consistency feedback from discriminative condition extractors, using single-step noisy rewards to avoid full-sampling memory cost.
- [OpenBias: Open-Set Bias Detection in Text-to-Image Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.07990): Detects open-set social bias in T2I outputs rather than fixed protected-attribute lists, giving model auditors a way to surface unexpected stereotype patterns.
- [MoE-FFD: Mixture of Experts for Generalized and Parameter-Efficient Face Forgery Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.08452): Freezes a ViT backbone and trains LoRA, adapters, and MoE modules to combine global transformer features with local CNN priors for generalized face-forgery detection.
- [Watermark-embedded Adversarial Examples for Copyright Protection against Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.09401): Trains a conditional adversarial generator that turns a few protected samples into perturbations causing diffusion models to output visible owner watermarks.
- [FSRT: Facial Scene Representation Transformer for Face Reenactment from Factorized Appearance, Head-Pose, and Facial Expression Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.09736): Learns factorized set-latent face representations with transformer encoders and decoders, separating appearance, head pose, and expression for cross-person reenactment.
- [Digging into Contrastive Learning for Robust Depth Estimation with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.09831): Adds trinity contrastive learning across noise, feature, and image levels so diffusion-based depth estimators remain stable under adverse weather and degraded scenes.
- [Ctrl-Adapter: An Efficient and Versatile Framework for Adapting Diverse Controls to Any Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.09967): Reuses pretrained ControlNets through lightweight adapters and MoE routing, adding image, video, sparse-frame, and multi-condition controls to U-Net and DiT diffusion backbones.
- [HQ-Edit: A High-Quality Dataset for Instruction-based Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.09990): Builds about 200,000 GPT-4V and DALL-E 3 curated edit pairs with alignment and coherence metrics, improving instruction image editors after fine-tuning.
- [Consistent Diffusion Meets Tweedie: Training Exact Ambient Diffusion Models with Noisy Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.10177): Uses a double Tweedie formula and consistency loss to train diffusion models from noisy data while reducing memorization during SDXL fine-tuning.
- [SparseDM: Toward Sparse Efficient Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.10445): Adds trainable sparse masks to convolutional and linear layers in pretrained diffusion models, cutting MACs by about half while preserving FID in UNet and Transformer generators.
- [LaDiC: Are Diffusion Models Really Inferior to Autoregressive Counterparts for Image-to-Text Generation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.10763): Tests diffusion for image-to-text generation, challenging the assumption that autoregressive decoders are inherently better for caption-like outputs.
- [Towards Highly Realistic Artistic Style Transfer via Stable Diffusion with Step-aware and Layer-aware Prompt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.11474): Learns step-aware and layer-aware prompts in Stable Diffusion so artistic style transfer preserves content structure while avoiding artifacts and disharmonious patterns.
- [MoA: Mixture-of-Attention for Subject-Context Disentanglement in Personalized Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.11565): Splits diffusion attention into personalized and frozen-prior branches with routing, preserving subject identity while keeping layouts and context from the base T2I model.
- [CU-Mamba: Selective State Space Models with Channel Learning for Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.11778): Inserts spatial and channel state-space modules into a U-shaped restoration network, modeling global context and channel correlations with linear feature-map complexity.
- [TextCenGen: Attention-Guided Text-Centric Background Adaptation for Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.11824): Uses cross-attention maps and force-directed relocation to create text-friendly T2I backgrounds, keeping generated objects away from intended typography regions without training.
- [EdgeFusion: On-Device Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.11925): Starts from compact BK-SDM, adds high-quality synthetic image-text data and LCM-tailored distillation, then quantizes and deploys two-step T2I generation under one second on edge devices.
- [StyleBooth: Image Style Editing with Multimodal Instruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.12154): Conditions diffusion style editing on unified text and exemplar-image instructions and builds style or destyle edit pairs through iterative tuning, editing, and usability filtering.
- [Lazy Diffusion Transformer for Interactive Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.12382): Generates only masked regions with a context encoder and diffusion transformer decoder, making prompt-guided local edits scale with mask size rather than full-canvas cost.
- [GenVideo: One-shot target-image and shape aware video editing using T2I diffusion models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.12541): Edits videos with a target-image-aware T2I model, target-and-shape-aware InvEdit masks, and latent noise correction so replacement objects can change shape or size while staying temporally consistent.
- [Robust CLIP-Based Detector for Exposing Diffusion Model-Generated Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.12908): Combines CLIP image-text features with an MLP, robustness-oriented loss, and loss-landscape flattening to generalize diffusion-image detection across imbalanced synthetic-source datasets.
- [Neural Flow Diffusion Models: Learnable Forward Process for Improved Diffusion Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.12940): Learns the forward process in diffusion models to improve generative modeling.
- [Motion-aware Latent Diffusion Models for Video Frame Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.13534): Builds MADiff for video frame interpolation, injecting motion priors between neighboring frames during latent diffusion sampling to reduce blur and improve coherent intermediate frames.
- [Hyper-SD: Trajectory Segmented Consistency Model for Efficient Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.13686): Compresses diffusion inference with trajectory-segmented consistency distillation, human feedback learning, and score distillation to keep near-original image quality in very few steps.
- [RingID: Rethinking Tree-Ring Watermarking for Enhanced Multi-Key Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.14055): Revisits Tree-Ring watermark robustness, identifies distribution shift as a hidden factor, and adds multi-channel heterogeneous watermarks for stronger multi-key diffusion-image identification.
- [MultiBooth: Towards Generating All Your Concepts in an Image from Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.14239): Splits multi-concept customization into single-concept encoding with a multimodal image encoder and box-guided cross-attention integration, improving fidelity while reducing inference cost.
- [Gradient Guidance for Diffusion Models: An Optimization Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.14743): Formalizes gradient-guided diffusion as regularized optimization and proposes forward-prediction-loss guidance that preserves pretrained score-model structure under external objectives.
- [Perturbing Attention Gives You More Bang for the Buck: Subtle Imaging Perturbations That Efficiently Fool Customized Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.15081): Introduces CAAT, a training-free attack that perturbs published images through cross-attention sensitivity so customized latent diffusion fine-tuning maps text to corrupted outputs.
- [Multimodal Large Language Model is a Human-Aligned Annotator for Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.15100): Uses multimodal LLM annotators to build VisionPrefer across prompt-following, aesthetics, fidelity, and harmlessness, then trains VP-Score rewards for T2I preference tuning.
- [From Parts to Whole: A Unified Reference Framework for Controllable Human Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.15267): Parts2Whole combines a semantic-aware appearance encoder, multiple reference images, shared self-attention, and mask-aware attention to generate controllable human portraits from separate part references.
- [ID-Aligner: Enhancing Identity-Preserving Text-to-Image Generation with Reward Feedback Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.15449): Adds a general feedback-learning framework for ID-T2I, using identity-consistency rewards and identity-aesthetic rewards from face models and preference data to improve portrait fidelity.
- [CharacterFactory: Sampling Consistent Characters With GANs for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.15677): Trains a GAN to map latent samples into celebrity-name embedding space, producing reusable identity embeddings for consistent new characters across image, video, and 3D diffusion models.
- [PuLID: Pure and Lightning ID Customization via Contrastive Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.16022): Adds tuning-free identity customization by pairing a Lightning T2I branch with the standard diffusion branch and optimizing contrastive alignment plus accurate-ID losses.
- [MuseumMaker: Continual Style Customization Without Catastrophic Forgetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.16612): Supports continual style customization with style distillation for new few-shot styles and dual shared-LoRA regularization to reduce forgetting of previously learned styles.
- [ObjectAdd: Adding Objects into Image via a Training-Free Diffusion Modification Fashion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.17230): Adds boxed object insertion without training, combining embedding-level concatenation, latent and attention injection, and attention-refocused inpainting to preserve the rest of the image.
- [PromptCIR: Blind Compressed Image Restoration with Prompt Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.17433): Restores JPEG-compressed images with lightweight prompts that encode compression information and interact with image-derived soft weights for content- and distortion-aware restoration.
- [Exposing Text-Image Inconsistency Using Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.18033): Uses T2I diffusion as D-TIIL to localize word-region inconsistencies in misleading text-image pairs, backed by a 14K-pair TIIL dataset with word and image-region annotations.
- [Paint by Inpaint: Learning to Add Image Objects by Removing Them First](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.18212): Builds object-addition training pairs by first removing objects with inpainting, then trains a diffusion editor to invert that process and add objects from natural-language instructions.
- [Explicit Correlation Learning for Generalizable Cross-Modal Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.19171): Trains cross-modal deepfake detectors with correlation distillation over content information and evaluates them on CMDFD, a four-generation-method dataset for audio-visual fakes.
- [Espresso: Robust Concept Filtering in Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.19227): Filters unacceptable T2I concepts through CLIP image-text embedding distances and contrastive fine-tuning that separates unacceptable and acceptable concepts while preserving utility.
- [InstantFamily: Masked Attention for Zero-shot Multi-ID Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.19427): Enables zero-shot multi-ID generation with a multimodal embedding stack and masked cross-attention that controls each identity and composition region.
- [TwinDiffusion: Enhancing Coherence and Efficiency in Panoramic Image Generation with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.19475): Improves training-free panorama generation through Crop Fusion for adjacent-region coherence and Cross Sampling for efficient dynamic patch generation.
- [Streamlining Image Editing with Layered Diffusion Brushes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.00313): Defines diffusion editing layers as reusable generation-parameter sets and caches intermediate latents, enabling non-destructive overlapping local edits in about 140 ms per edit.
- [Deep Reward Supervisions for Tuning Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.00760): Introduces DRTune, which back-propagates reward supervision through the iterative T2I sampling process to tune early denoising steps and produce FDXL from SDXL.
- [Customizing Text-to-Image Models with a Single Image Pair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.01536): Learns stylistic differences from one paired artwork by separating style and content into orthogonal LoRA weight spaces, then applies the style through diffusion-time guidance.
- [DiffuseTrace: A Transparent and Flexible Watermarking Scheme for Latent Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.02696): Embeds multi-bit semantic watermarks as a plug-in for latent diffusion models, maintaining 99% detection under image-processing attacks and over 94% attribution under generative attacks.
- [U-DiTs: Downsample Tokens in U-Shaped Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.02730): Introduces U-shaped diffusion transformers that downsample tokens for more efficient generation.
- [ImageInWords: Unlocking Hyper-Detailed Image Descriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.02793): Curates human-in-the-loop hyper-detailed image descriptions and shows that IIW captions improve T2I fidelity and compositional reasoning despite being an image-to-text data method.
- [MVIP-NeRF: Multi-View 3D Inpainting on NeRF Scenes via Diffusion Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.02859): Applies diffusion priors to NeRF inpainting with multi-view SDS and normal-map SDS losses so RGB appearance and geometry are completed consistently across views.
- [VectorPainter: Advanced Stylized Vector Graphics Synthesis Using Stroke-Style Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.02962): Uses stroke-style priors for stylized vector graphics synthesis.
- [Imagine Flash: Accelerating Emu Diffusion Models with Backward Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.05224): Distills Emu diffusion models to one to three denoising steps with backward distillation, shifted reconstruction loss, and inference-time noise correction.
- [Attention-Driven Training-Free Efficiency Enhancement of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.05252): Speeds diffusion inference without retraining by ranking redundant attention tokens with Generalized Weighted PageRank and varying pruning budgets across denoising steps.
- [MasterWeaver: Taming Editability and Identity for Personalized Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.05806): Uses an identity encoder, added cross-attention, editing-direction loss, and a face-augmented dataset to balance face identity fidelity with prompt editability.
- [Frame Interpolation with Consecutive Brownian Bridge Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.05953): Replaces stochastic latent diffusion trajectories for video frame interpolation with consecutive Brownian Bridge diffusion, starting from deterministic latent values to reduce cumulative variance and improve deterministic intermediate-frame synthesis.
- [Distilling Diffusion Models into Conditional GANs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.05967): Treats diffusion distillation as paired noise-to-image translation, training a one-step conditional GAN with E-LatentLPIPS and a diffusion-derived multiscale discriminator with text-alignment loss.
- [UnMarker: A Universal Attack on Defensive Image Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.08363): Attacks defensive image watermarking without detector feedback or watermark-scheme knowledge, using an analysis of robust watermark structure to remove provenance fingerprints from generated images.
- [IRSRMamba: Infrared Image Super-Resolution via Mamba-Based Wavelet Transform Feature Modulation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.09873): Combines Mamba state-space modeling with wavelet-transform feature modulation and a semantic-consistency loss for infrared super-resolution under weak edges, sparse textures, and fragmented context.
- [AquaLoRA: Toward White-box Protection for Customized Stable Diffusion Models via Watermark LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.11135): Embeds watermark behavior into customized Stable Diffusion through LoRA weights so protected models can emit traceable images even when attackers have white-box access to the checkpoint.
- [UPAM: Unified Prompt Attack in Text-to-Image Generation Models Against Both Textual Filters and Visual Checkers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.11336): Uses gradient-based prompt optimization and Sphere-Probing Learning to attack both textual filters and visual checkers in defended text-to-image systems.
- [Evolving Storytelling: Benchmarks and Methods for New Character Customization with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.11852): Introduces NewEpisode for adding unseen characters to story visualization and studies methods that separate new and old characters under limited data to preserve narrative and identity consistency.
- [Bridging the Intent Gap: Knowledge-Enhanced Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.12538): Injects external knowledge into the visual-generation pipeline so prompts can be grounded beyond surface text, targeting better intent preservation in knowledge-heavy image synthesis.
- [EmoEdit: Evoking Emotions through Image Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.12661): Builds EmoEditSet with 40,120 emotion-attributed edit pairs and edits image content as well as color or style so affective manipulation changes emotional response while preserving composition.
- [Face Adapter for Pre-Trained Diffusion Models with Fine-Grained ID and Attribute Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.12970): Adds an adapter to pretrained diffusion models that decouples target structure, identity, and attributes for high-fidelity face reenactment and swapping without full generator retraining.
- [Personalized Residuals for Concept-Driven Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.12978): Learns low-rank residuals on selected layers of a frozen text-to-image diffusion model and applies them only in cross-attention-localized regions during sampling.
- [Computational Tradeoffs in Image Synthesis: Diffusion, Masked-Token, and Next-Token Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.13218): Compares diffusion, masked-token, and next-token image synthesis under controlled FLOP budgets, finding token prediction stronger for prompt following while diffusion remains competitive as quality scales.
- [Class-Conditional self-reward mechanism for improved Text-to-Image models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.13473): Fine-tunes a text-to-image diffusion model on self-generated, self-judged class-conditional samples, using the model's own rewards to automate preference data selection.
- [MetaEarth: A Generative Foundation Model for Global-Scale Remote Sensing Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.13570): Scales remote-sensing generation with a resolution-guided self-cascading generator for worldwide, multi-resolution, unbounded satellite-image synthesis.
- [Learning Diffusion Priors from Observations by Expectation Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.13712): Learns diffusion priors from noisy or indirect observations with expectation maximization and an improved posterior sampling scheme for unconditional diffusion models.
- [LightningDrag: Lightning Fast and Accurate Drag-based Image Editing Emerging from Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.13722): Reframes drag editing as conditional generation learned from videos, cutting diffusion-based point-drag edits from minute-scale runtimes to about one second.
- [Conditioning diffusion models by explicit forward-backward bridging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.13794): Casts conditional simulation in an unconditional diffusion model as inference on a partial SDE bridge, enabling particle Gibbs and pseudo-marginal samplers for constrained generation.
- [FreeCustom: Tuning-Free Customized Image Generation for Multi-Concept Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.13870): Performs multi-concept customized generation without fine-tuning, composing several user-provided concepts at inference instead of training separate subject adapters.
- [FreeTuner: Any Subject in Any Style with Training-free Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.14201): Enables compositional personalization without training, disentangling subject and style references so a supplied subject can be rendered in a supplied style.
- [RectifID: Personalizing Rectified Flow with Anchored Classifier Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.14677): Uses anchored classifier guidance in rectified-flow generation for identity-preserving personalization from reference images without domain-specific retraining.
- [Learning Multi-Dimensional Human Preference for Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.14705): Trains Multi-dimensional Preference Score to evaluate text-to-image outputs across separate human-preference aspects rather than a single overall scalar.
- [EditWorld: Simulating World Dynamics for Instruction-Following Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.14785): Extends instruction-based editing beyond add, replace, and delete operations by simulating physical world dynamics needed for realistic scene changes.
- [Good Seed Makes a Good Crop: Discovering Secret Seeds in Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.14828): Studies how random seeds shape diffusion outputs and identifies high-performing golden seeds that improve fixed-prompt image quality during inference.
- [TerDiT: Ternary Diffusion Models with Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.14854): Compresses diffusion transformers through ternary quantization, targeting lower deployment cost for large DiT text-to-image generators.
- [ODGEN: Domain-specific Object Detection Data Generation with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.15199): Generates domain-specific object-detection training images conditioned on bounding boxes, improving controllability for dense multi-class scenes with occlusions.
- [NIVeL: Neural Implicit Vector Layers for Text-to-Vector Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.15217): Generates text-to-vector graphics with neural implicit vector layers.
- [Defensive Unlearning with Adversarial Training for Robust Concept Erasure in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.15234): Introduces AdvUnlearn, combining adversarial training, retain-set regularization, and text-encoder robustification so erased T2I concepts resist adversarial prompts without collapsing image utility.
- [StyleMaster: Towards Flexible Stylized Image Generation with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.15287): Targets flexible stylized diffusion generation, separating style control from base image synthesis so users can vary stylization without retraining a full model.
- [Unlearning Concepts in Diffusion Model via Concept Domain Correction and Concept Preserving Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.15304): Uses DoCo domain correction plus concept-preserving gradient surgery to erase sensitive concepts from T2I diffusion while reducing collateral damage to neighboring concepts.
- [Towards Understanding the Working Mechanism of Text-to-Image Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.15330): Analyzes how text-to-image diffusion models work internally, adding diagnostic evidence for prompt conditioning and generation behavior.
- [Accelerating Diffusion Models with Parallel Sampling: Inference at Sub-Linear Time Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.15986): Introduces a parallel sampler for diffusion inference that reduces sampling time below linear dependence on step count while preserving image-generation quality.
- [R.A.C.E.: Robust Adversarial Concept Erasure for Secure Text-to-Image Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.16341): Strengthens concept erasure in text-to-image diffusion with adversarial training, making removed concepts harder to recover through attack prompts.
- [Pruning for Robust Concept Erasing in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.16534): Removes concept-specific behavior by pruning diffusion-model components, aiming for concept erasure that remains robust while preserving unrelated generation ability.
- [DMPlug: A Plug-in Method for Solving Inverse Problems with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.16749): Uses pretrained diffusion models as plug-in priors for inverse problems, avoiding task-specific retraining while guiding reconstruction through diffusion sampling.
- [PromptFix: You Prompt and We Fix the Photo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.16785): Edits user photos from natural-language prompts, treating photo correction as prompt-conditioned image editing rather than manual retouching.
- [EM Distillation for One-step Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.16852): Distills diffusion generators into one-step samplers with an expectation-maximization objective, targeting fast image synthesis without the usual multistep denoising loop.
- [PatchScaler: An Efficient Patch-Independent Diffusion Model for Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.17158): Performs image super-resolution with patch-independent diffusion, reducing dependence between large image patches for more efficient high-resolution restoration.
- [RB-Modulation: Training-Free Personalization of Diffusion Models using Stochastic Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.17401): Uses reference-based stochastic optimal control to extract style attributes as a terminal cost, separating style and content through cross-attention feature aggregation without adapters or ControlNets.
