- [A Closer Look at Time Steps is Worthy of Triple Speed-Up for Diffusion Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.17403): Divides diffusion timesteps into acceleration, deceleration, and convergence regions, then samples fewer low-benefit convergence steps to cut training cost by roughly threefold.
- [Little Data, Big Impact: Privacy-Aware Visual Language Models via Minimal Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.17423): Off-axis for image generation, but contributes PrivBench, PrivBench-H, and PrivTune for minimally tuning VLMs to recognize GDPR-aligned privacy-sensitive visual content.
- [ClavaDDPM: Multi-relational Data Synthesis with Cluster-guided Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.17724): Off-axis for visual generation, but extends diffusion synthesis to multi-table relational data with cluster latent variables that capture long-range cross-table dependencies.
- [MixDQ: Memory-Efficient Few-Step Text-to-Image Diffusion Models with Metric-Decoupled Mixed Precision Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.17873): Applies metric-decoupled mixed-precision quantization to few-step text-to-image diffusion, reducing memory while protecting perceptual and alignment quality.
- [DiG: Scalable and Efficient Diffusion Models with Gated Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.18428): Replaces expensive attention in diffusion backbones with gated linear attention, improving scalability and efficiency for image-generation diffusion models.
- [Principled Probabilistic Imaging using Diffusion Models as Plug-and-Play Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.18782): Turns diffusion priors into a principled MCMC posterior sampler for Bayesian inverse imaging, reducing general inverse problems to Gaussian denoising posterior sampling.
- [ConceptPrune: Concept Editing in Diffusion Models via Skilled Neuron Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.19237): Edits concepts in diffusion models by pruning specialized neurons, offering a targeted model-side concept-removal method.
- [ART: Automatic Red-teaming for Text-to-Image Models to Protect Benign Users](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.19360): Uses VLM-driven automatic red teaming to find benign prompts that unintentionally trigger unsafe T2I outputs, expanding safety testing beyond malicious jailbreak prompts.
- [Promptus: Can Prompts Streaming Replace Video Streaming with Stable Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.20032): Replaces transmitted video frames with prompt streams, then reconstructs pixel-aligned frames at the receiver using Stable Diffusion plus prompt fitting and bitrate control.
- [RIGID: A Training-free and Model-Agnostic Framework for Robust AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.20112): Detects AI-generated images without training by measuring how real and generated images differ under tiny perturbations in vision-foundation-model representation space.
- [Jina CLIP: Your CLIP Model Is Also Your Text Retriever](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.20204): Off-axis for image generation, but trains Jina CLIP with multi-task contrastive learning so one CLIP model handles both text-image and text-text retrieval.
- [Boost Your Human Image Generation Model via Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.20216): Adapts DPO for human image synthesis by using high-quality real images as preferred samples, pushing generated bodies, poses, and details toward realistic human references.
- [Slight Corruption in Pre-training Data Makes Better Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.20494): Studies controlled corruption of image-condition pairs in diffusion pretraining and shows that slight condition noise can improve robustness and generation quality.
- [Diffusion Models Are Innate One-Step Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.20750): Argues that pretrained diffusion models already contain one-step generation ability, then distills sampling by matching the teacher's trajectory rather than only final images.
- [Faster Diffusion Sampling with Randomized Midpoints: Sequential and Parallel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.00924): Introduces randomized-midpoint diffusion samplers with sequential and parallel variants, improving theoretical dimension dependence for score-based sampling.
- [Δ-DiT: A Training-Free Acceleration Method Tailored for Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.01125): Speeds DiT inference without retraining by exploiting block roles: early blocks shape outlines while later blocks refine details, allowing selective computation.
- [Dimba: Transformer-Mamba Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.01159): Builds a text-to-image diffusion backbone that alternates Transformer and Mamba layers, using cross-attention conditioning plus quality tuning and resolution adaptation.
- [AutoStudio: Crafting Consistent Subjects in Multi-turn Interactive Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.01388): Uses a training-free multi-agent LLM framework to manage multi-turn image generation, keeping subjects consistent when users switch or refine subjects over turns.
- [Guided Score identity Distillation for Data-Free One-Step Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.01561): Distills Stable Diffusion into a data-free one-step generator by augmenting Score identity Distillation with long and short classifier-free guidance.
- [DiffUHaul: A Training-Free Method for Object Dragging in Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.01594): Performs training-free object dragging by using localized text-to-image diffusion spatial understanding while avoiding layout manipulation that entangles object identity.
- [Learning-to-Cache: Accelerating Diffusion Transformer via Layer Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.01733): Removes much of DiT inference compute by caching layer outputs across denoising steps, reporting large cache-step savings with negligible FID loss.
- [DEFT: Efficient Fine-tuning of Diffusion Models by Learning the Generalised $h$-transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.01781): Unifies conditional diffusion training and sampling for inverse problems by learning a generalized h-transform, reducing brittle heuristic guidance.
- [Cross-Domain Graph Data Scaling: A Showcase with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.01899): Off-axis for visual generation, but uses a pretrained discrete diffusion graph augmentor, UniAug, to scale heterogeneous graph data for downstream tasks.
- [Enhance Image-to-Image Generation with LLaVA-generated Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.01956): Has LLaVA describe the input image, then feeds that prompt with the original image into image-to-image generation to improve visual coherence and resemblance.
- [Flash Diffusion: Accelerating Any Conditional Diffusion Model for Few Steps Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.02347): Distills conditional diffusion models into few-step generators across text-to-image, inpainting, face swapping, and super-resolution while supporting UNet and DiT backbones.
- [RoomTex: Texturing Compositional Indoor Scenes via Iterative Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.02461): Textures untextured 3D indoor scene meshes through coarse panoramic ControlNet generation followed by iterative object-level inpainting from selected camera views.
- [Guiding a Diffusion Model with a Bad Version of Itself](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.02507): Guides image generation with a smaller or less-trained copy of the model to improve quality separately from diversity, instead of using unconditional classifier-free guidance.
- [DiffCut: Catalyzing Zero-Shot Semantic Segmentation with Diffusion Features and Recursive Normalized Cut](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.02842): Off-axis as segmentation, but shows diffusion UNet self-attention features can drive recursive normalized-cut zero-shot semantic segmentation.
- [PosterLLaVa: Constructing a Unified Multi-modal Layout Generator with LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.02884): Fine-tunes an MLLM layout generator on JSON-style layout text and visual instructions, adds user-constrained generation and a complicated-poster dataset, and outputs editable SVG posters.
- [Text-to-Image Rectified Flow as Plug-and-Play Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.03293): Shows rectified-flow T2I models can act as plug-and-play priors like diffusion models, with time-symmetry properties that support inverse and editing uses.
- [Tackling copyright issues in AI image generation through originality estimation and genericization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.03341): Adds originality estimation and PREGen prompt-rewriting genericization to reduce imitation of copyrighted visual characters in generated images.
- [JIGMARK: A Black-Box Approach for Enhancing Image Watermarks against Diffusion Model Edits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.03720): Strengthens image watermarks against diffusion-model edits using contrastive learning over edited and unedited image pairs without backpropagating through the editor.
- [Your Absorbing Discrete Diffusion Secretly Models the Conditional Distributions of Clean Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.03736): Reparameterizes absorbing discrete diffusion as time-independent clean-data conditional probabilities, enabling cached network evaluations during sampling.
- [Multistep Distillation of Diffusion Models via Moment Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.04103): Distills diffusion models through multistep moment matching so shortened samplers preserve trajectory statistics rather than only one-step outputs.
- [ReNO: Enhancing One-step Text-to-Image Models through Reward-based Noise Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.04312): Improves one-step T2I models at inference by optimizing initial noise with gradients from human-preference reward models, avoiding full reward fine-tuning.
- [MELFuSION: Synthesizing Music from Image and Language Cues Using Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.04673): Off-axis for image generation, but adds a visual-synapse module to a text-to-music diffusion model so image cues influence generated music.
- [Fast Policy Synthesis with Variable Noise Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.04806): Off-axis for image generation, but accelerates robotic diffusion policies by streaming partially denoised action trajectories with variable noise levels.
- [AttnDreamBooth: Towards Text-Aligned Personalized Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.05000): Personalizes T2I generation by separately learning concept embedding alignment, cross-attention maps, and subject identity, improving identity preservation and text alignment.
- [Layered Image Vectorization via Semantic Simplification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.05404): Converts raster images into layered vectors by using SDS-driven semantic simplification followed by structural buildup and visual refinement.
- [Binarized Diffusion Model for Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.05723): Compresses diffusion super-resolution with BI-DiffSR, combining a binarization-friendly UNet with consistent pixel downsampling and upsampling to reduce compute.
- [TIGeR: Unifying Text-to-Image Generation and Retrieval with Large Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.05814): Unifies T2I generation and retrieval inside one LMM, adding an autonomous choice between generated and retrieved images plus TIGeR-Bench for creative and knowledge-intensive prompts.
- [ProcessPainter: Learn Painting Process from Sequence Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.06062): Learns stepwise painting processes from sequence data, generating text-to-video painting trajectories rather than only final diffusion images.
- [ExtraNeRF: Visibility-Aware View Extrapolation of Neural Radiance Fields with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.06133): Extends NeRF view coverage by tracking unobserved regions and using diffusion inpainting to extrapolate consistent scene content beyond captured views.
- [Diffusion-RPO: Aligning Diffusion Models through Relative Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.06382): Aligns T2I diffusion models with human preferences through relative preference optimization over same-prompt and semantically related prompt-image pairs.
- [Margin-aware Preference Optimization for Aligning Diffusion Models without Reference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.06424): Removes DPO's reference-model constraint for T2I alignment by adding margin-aware preference optimization, reducing reference mismatch during style or object adaptation.
- [Cometh: A continuous-time discrete-state graph diffusion model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.06449): Off-axis for visual generation, but introduces a continuous-time discrete-state graph diffusion model with random-walk structural encodings for graph synthesis.
- [Autoregressive Model Beats Diffusion: Llama for Scalable Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.06525): Introduces LlamaGen, applying vanilla next-token autoregression to image tokens with a high-usage tokenizer and scalable class-conditional image generation.
- [Evolving from Single-modal to Multi-modal Facial Deepfake Detection: Progress and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.06965): Off-axis as a survey, but maps facial deepfake detection from single-modal detectors to audio-visual and text-visual multimodal methods.
- [Is One GPU Enough? Pushing Image Generation at Higher-Resolutions with Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.07251): Introduces Pixelsmith, a zero-shot single-GPU high-resolution generation framework that cascades a low-resolution reference and patchwise denoising with Slider guidance.
- [Towards Realistic Data Generation for Real-World Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.07255): Builds RealDGen, an unsupervised data generator for real-world super-resolution that decouples content and degradation extraction to produce realistic training pairs.
- [Flow map matching with stochastic interpolants: A mathematical framework for consistency models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.07507): Provides a stochastic-interpolant framework for learning two-time flow maps, giving consistency models a principled one-step or few-step generation foundation.
- [Instant 3D Human Avatar Generation using Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.07516): Generates 3D human avatars by combining specialized diffusion image generators for views and back-view prediction with a separate 3D lifting network.
- [Neural Gaffer: Relighting Any Object via Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.07520): Trains an end-to-end 2D diffusion relighting model that edits a single object image under arbitrary novel environment lighting.
- [Unveiling the Power of Wavelets: A Wavelet-based Kolmogorov-Arnold Network for Hyperspectral Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.07869): Off-axis for image generation, but evaluates wavelet-based KAN activations for hyperspectral image classification across spatial-spectral patterns.
- [One-Step Effective Diffusion Network for Real-World Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.08177): Replaces multi-step random-noise real-world super-resolution with OSediff, a one-step diffusion network for deterministic high-quality restoration.
- [WMAdapter: Adding WaterMark Control to Latent Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.08337): Adds a lightweight contextual adapter and hybrid fine-tuning so latent diffusion models can imprint user-specified watermarks during generation.
- [OmniCorpus: A Unified Multimodal Corpus of 10 Billion-Level Images Interleaved with Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.08418): Off-axis as a dataset, but releases a 10-billion-scale interleaved image-text corpus for multimodal in-context learning and model fine-tuning.
- [Diffusion Soup: Model Merging for Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.08431): Averages T2I diffusion models trained on data shards, enabling training-free continual learning, unlearning, style mixing, and anti-memorization behavior.
- [DiTFastAttn: Attention Compression for Diffusion Transformer Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.08552): Compresses DiT attention after training by exploiting spatial, temporal, and conditional redundancy during image and video generation inference.
- [FakeInversion: Learning to Detect Images from Unseen Text-to-Image Models by Inverting Stable Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.08603): Detects synthetic images from unseen T2I generators by inverting them through Stable Diffusion and training on inversion-derived features.
- [Suitability of KANs for Computer Vision: A preliminary investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.09087): Off-axis for image generation, but compares Kolmogorov-Arnold Network visual backbones against conventional convolutional and linear layers on recognition and segmentation.
- [EMMA: Your Text-to-Image Diffusion Model Can Secretly Accept Multi-Modal Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.09162): Extends ELLA-style T2I diffusion with a multimodal feature connector so text plus reference appearances can jointly condition image generation.
- [CLIPAway: Harmonizing Focused Embeddings for Removing Objects via Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.09368): Removes objects with diffusion inpainting by using CLIP embeddings that emphasize background regions while excluding the object to avoid hallucinated remnants.
- [OmniTokenizer: A Joint Image-Video Tokenizer for Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.09399): Builds a transformer tokenizer for both images and videos with decoupled spatial-temporal attention and progressive image-to-video training.
- [Instruct 4D-to-4D: Editing 4D Scenes as Pseudo-3D Scenes Using 2D Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.09402): Edits dynamic 4D scenes by treating them as pseudo-3D, adding anchor-aware attention and optical-flow appearance propagation to 2D diffusion editing.
- [Data Attribution for Text-to-Image Models by Unlearning Synthesized Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.09408): Identifies influential T2I training images by simulating unlearning of a generated output and measuring loss deviations over candidate training samples.
- [Alleviating Distortion in Image Generation via Multi-Resolution Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.09416): Adds a multi-resolution network and time-dependent layer normalization to reduce patchification-induced distortion in transformer-based diffusion generators.
- [ControlVAR: Exploring Controllable Visual Autoregressive Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.09750): Introduces ControlVAR, jointly modeling images and pixel-level controls with next-scale autoregression for efficient controllable visual generation.
- [SuperSVG: Superpixel-Based Scalable Vector Graphics Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.09794): Synthesizes scalable vector graphics through superpixel-based generation.
- [Make It Count: Text-to-Image Generation with an Accurate Number of Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.10210): Finds diffusion features that track object identity during denoising, then uses them to detect and repair over-counting or under-counting in T2I outputs.
- [Consistency-diversity-realism Pareto fronts of conditional image generative models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.10429): Studies inference-time control knobs by drawing Pareto fronts over prompt consistency, output diversity, and realism for conditional image generators.
- [STAR: Scale-wise Text-conditioned AutoRegressive image generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.10797): Generates 1024px text-conditioned images with scale-wise autoregression, a pretrained text encoder, normalized 2D RoPE, and stabilized within-scale token sampling.
- [Improving Probabilistic Diffusion Models With Optimal Diagonal Covariance Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.10808): Learns optimal diagonal analytic covariance with an unbiased OCM objective, improving diffusion sampling efficiency, recall, and likelihood.
- [HAIChart: Human and AI Paired Visualization System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.11033): Off-axis for image generation, but uses reinforcement learning and user feedback to iteratively recommend dataset visualizations.
- [Exploring the Role of Large Language Models in Prompt Encoding for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.11831): Analyzes why decoder-only LLM prompt encoders hurt T2I prompt following, then adds usage guidance to align LLM features with diffusion conditioning.
- [Scaling the Codebook Size of VQGAN to 100,000 with a Utilization Rate of 99%](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.11837): Introduces VQGAN-LC, scaling image-token codebooks to 100,000 entries with over 99% utilization for autoregressive visual generation.
- [Autoregressive Image Generation without Vector Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.11838): Removes vector quantization from autoregressive image generation by modeling continuous per-token distributions with a diffusion loss.
- [Not All Prompts Are Made Equal: Prompt-based Pruning of Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.12042): Adds Adaptive Prompt-Tailored Pruning, where a prompt router selects the needed T2I diffusion capacity while preserving GPU batch parallelism.
- [Neural Residual Diffusion Models for Deep Scalable Vision Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.13215): Connects residual network dynamics to reverse diffusion and proposes a scalable neural residual diffusion design for deeper vision generators.
- [AniFaceDiff: High-Fidelity Face Reenactment via Facial Parametric Conditioned Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.13272): Animates stylized avatars with Stable Diffusion plus facial-alignment spatial conditioning and parametric expression controls that avoid target-motion identity leakage.
- [Invertible Consistency Distillation for Text-Guided Image Editing in Around 7 Steps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.14539): Adds invertible Consistency Distillation so distilled T2I models can both synthesize images and invert real images for editing in a few steps.
- [Holistic Evaluation for Interleaved Text-and-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.14643): Off-axis as evaluation, but introduces InterleavedBench and InterleavedEval for arbitrary-order text-and-image generation tasks.
- [Towards trustworthy management of AIGC copyright: blockchain-enabled full lifecycle recording and multi-party auditing approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.14966): Off-axis for model methods, but proposes blockchain-based full-lifecycle recording and multi-party auditing for AIGC copyright management.
- [MVOC: a training-free multiple video object composition method with diffusion models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.15829): Composes multiple video objects by DDIM-inverting each object, editing the first composited frame, then injecting features and attention in image-to-video generation.
- [Repairing Catastrophic-Neglect in Text-to-Image Diffusion Models via Attention-Guided Feature Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.16272): Repairs missing prompt objects by detecting catastrophic neglect and applying attention-guided feature enhancement during T2I generation.
- [Prompt-Consistency Image Generation (PCIG): A Unified Framework Integrating LLMs, Knowledge Graphs, and Controllable Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.16333): Extracts objects with an LLM, builds a knowledge graph for likely object locations, and feeds controllable diffusion to improve prompt-image consistency.
- [ResMaster: Mastering High-Resolution Image Generation via Structural and Fine-Grained Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.16476): Upscales beyond a model's native resolution by using a low-resolution reference for low-frequency structure and image-prompt guidance for patchwise high-resolution details.
- [Repulsive Latent Score Distillation for Solving Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.16683): Adds repulsive latent score distillation for inverse problems, using particle diversity regularization and latent-data disentanglement to reduce SDS mode collapse.
- [Recite, Reconstruct, Recollect: Memorization in LMs as a Multifaceted Phenomenon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.17746): Off-axis for image generation; its useful contribution is a memorization taxonomy splitting language-model memorization into recitation, reconstruction, and recollection with different predictive factors.
- [DiffuseHigh: Training-free Progressive High-Resolution Image Synthesis through Structure Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.18459): Uses low-resolution diffusion outputs as structural guides for progressive high-resolution sampling, reducing object repetition and shape distortion without training or fine-tuning a larger model.
- [Lumina-Next: Making Lumina-T2X Stronger and Faster with Next-DiT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.18583): Reworks Lumina-T2X with Next-DiT, 3D RoPE, sandwich normalization, scaled RoPE extrapolation, sigmoid time discretization, and context-drop token merging for faster multilingual text-to-image and cross-modal generation.
- [Rethinking and Red-Teaming Protective Perturbation in Personalized Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.18944): Explains protective perturbations as CLIP-space shortcut misalignment during personalized diffusion fine-tuning, then red-teams them with restoration-based purification and contrastive decoupling from noise tokens.
- [GM-DF: Generalized Multi-Scenario Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.20078): Builds a multi-dataset face-forgery detector with hybrid domain experts, CLIP commonality features, masked image reconstruction, and domain-aware meta-learning to generalize across unseen forgery scenarios.
- [Prompt Refinement with Image Pivot for Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.00247): Trains PRIP to translate user prompts into generator-preferred prompts by using the latent representation of a user-preferred image as a pivot between user language and system language.
- [LLM4GEN: Leveraging Semantic Representation of LLMs for Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.00737): Adds a plug-and-play cross-adapter that fuses LLM semantic representations with native T2I text features and an entity-guided regularizer to improve dense prompt and attribute binding.
- [InstantStyle-Plus: Style Transfer with Content-Preserving in Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.00788): Preserves style-transfer content by starting from inverted content latent noise, using tile ControlNet for spatial layout, adding a semantic adapter, and applying a style extractor as discriminator guidance.
- [An Expectation-Maximization Algorithm for Training Clean Diffusion Models from Corrupted Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.01014): Trains diffusion priors from corrupted observations by alternating E-step clean-image reconstruction with M-step weight refinement, targeting imaging tasks such as inpainting, denoising, and deblurring when clean data are scarce.
- [On Statistical Rates and Provably Efficient Criteria of Latent Diffusion Transformers (DiTs)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.01079): Provides theory for latent DiTs, deriving sample-complexity and approximation rates under low-dimensional latent assumptions and identifying almost-linear inference and training criteria via low-rank gradient structure.
- [StyleShot: A Snapshot on Any Style](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.01414): Learns a tuning-free style representation with a style-aware encoder, the StyleGallery dataset, decoupled style learning, and a content-fusion encoder for generalized image-driven style transfer.
- [FORA: Fast-Forward Caching in Diffusion Transformer Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.01425): Speeds DiT inference by caching and reusing attention and MLP intermediate outputs across denoising steps, requiring no retraining and only modestly affecting FID and IS.
- [On Discrete Prompt Optimization for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.01606): Formulates T2I prompt engineering as discrete optimization, using compact word subspaces and a shortcut text gradient to either improve prompt faithfulness or construct adversarial prompts.
- [GlyphDraw2: Automatic Generation of Complex Glyph Posters with Diffusion Models and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.02252): Uses LLM-assisted poster planning and triple-cross-attention alignment so diffusion generation can render complex English and Chinese glyph posters with controllable fonts and resolution.
- [MIGC++: Advanced Multi-Instance Generation Controller for Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.02329): Extends multi-instance image generation with text or image attributes, box or mask position controls, and a Shading Aggregation Controller for consistent iterative generation.
- [Image-to-Text Logic Jailbreak: Your Imagination can Help You Do Anything](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.02534): Off-axis for this model section; it introduces Flow-JD, a flowchart-based VLM jailbreak dataset that tests whether visual logic reasoning can bypass safety policies.
- [InternLM-XComposer-2.5: A Versatile Large Vision Language Model Supporting Long-Contextual Input and Output](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.03320): Mostly a VLM entry rather than a generator; its relevant artifact is a 7B long-context image-text model with LoRA applications for web-page composition and text-image article creation.
- [Learning Action and Reasoning-Centric Image Editing from Videos and Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.03471): Curates AURORA triplets from videos and simulation engines so instruction editors learn action, movement, spatial reasoning, and physical-dynamics edits beyond static object or style changes.
- [T2IShield: Defending Against Backdoors on Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.04215): Detects, localizes, and mitigates T2I backdoors by exploiting trigger-induced cross-attention assimilation, using norm truncation, covariance discrimination, and binary-search trigger localization.
- [UltraEdit: Instruction-based Fine-Grained Image Editing at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.05282): Releases about four million instruction-editing samples grounded in real photos and artworks, with LLM-generated instructions and region annotations that improve MagicBrush and Emu-Edit fine-tuning.
- [JeDi: Joint-Image Diffusion Models for Finetuning-Free Personalized Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.06187): Learns a joint distribution over related text-image pairs sharing a subject, enabling finetuning-free personalization from one or many reference images during sampling.
- [Powerful and Flexible: Personalized Text-to-Image Generation via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.06642): Uses deterministic policy gradient reinforcement learning to optimize personalized T2I diffusion with structural-consistency and other differentiable or non-differentiable rewards.
- [RodinHD: High-Fidelity 3D Avatar Generation with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.06938): Generates detailed 3D avatars from a portrait by combining triplane diffusion, a data-scheduling fix for decoder forgetting, weight consolidation, and multi-layer cross-attention to fine-grained portrait features.
- [MARS: Mixture of Auto-Regressive Models for Fine-grained Text-to-image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.07614): Builds bilingual English-Chinese T2I generation around a SemVIE expert that preserves a pretrained LLM text branch while fine-tuning visual integration through staged image-text alignment.
- [Controlling Space and Time with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.07860): Introduces 4DiM, a cascaded diffusion model for novel-view synthesis that mixes 3D, 4D, and video data and supports metric camera-pose plus timestamp control.
- [DMM: Disparity-Guided Multispectral Mamba for Oriented Object Detection in Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.08132): Off-axis for generation; it contributes a multispectral remote-sensing detector with disparity-guided Mamba fusion, target-aware attention, and a target-prior auxiliary task.
- [Diff-Tracker: Text-to-Image Diffusion Models are Unsupervised Trackers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.08394): Reuses a pretrained T2I diffusion model for unsupervised visual tracking through a learned target prompt and an online prompt updater that follows target motion.
- [LightenDiffusion: Unsupervised Low-Light Image Enhancement with Latent-Retinex Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.08939): Performs low-light restoration by decomposing latent features into reflectance and illumination maps, then using diffusion with self-constrained consistency to avoid normal-light content leakage.
- [TCAN: Animating Human Images with Temporally Consistent Pose Guidance using Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.09012): Animates human images by freezing ControlNet, adding LoRA to U-Net layers, inserting a temporal layer for pose-detector robustness, and using pose-derived temperature maps for static backgrounds.
- [ContextualStory: Consistent Visual Storytelling with Spatially-Enhanced and Storyline Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.09774): Generates consistent story frames with Spatially-Enhanced Temporal Attention, a Storyline Contextualizer, and a StoryFlow Adapter that guides scene changes across visual storytelling sequences.
- [DiffStega: Towards Universal Training-Free Coverless Image Steganography with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.10459): Implements training-free coverless steganography by combining password-dependent reference-image prompts with text prompts and a Noise Flip mechanism for authorized recovery.
- [When Synthetic Traces Hide Real Content: Analysis of Stable Diffusion Image Laundering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.10736): Shows that Stable Diffusion image-to-image laundering can make real content look synthetic, then proposes a two-stage detector for pristine, laundered, and fully generated images.
- [AccDiffusion: An Accurate Method for Higher-Resolution Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.10738): Reduces repeated objects in training-free high-resolution generation by replacing one global prompt with patch-content-aware prompts and adding dilated sampling with window interaction.
- [Model Inversion Attacks Through Target-Specific Conditional Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.11424): Uses a target-specific conditional diffusion model and iterative reconstruction to recover private classifier training images, making this a diffusion-based privacy attack rather than a generator improvement.
- [How Control Information Influences Multilingual Text Image Generation and Editing?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.11502): Studies how control images affect text rendering across denoising stages and introduces TextGen, which uses Fourier-adjusted control features plus two-stage generation for Chinese and English visual text.
- [Scaling Diffusion Transformers to 16 Billion Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.11633): Scales DiT with sparse MoE routing, shared experts, and expert-level balance loss, reaching 16.5B parameters while reducing inference cost through expert specialization.
- [Subject-driven Text-to-Image Generation via Preference-based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.12164): Fine-tunes only the U-Net for subject-driven T2I using a lambda-Harmonic reward and Bradley-Terry preference labels, with early stopping to reduce overfitting and negative samples.
- [Reliable and Efficient Concept Erasure of Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.12383): Erases unsafe or copyrighted concepts in seconds with closed-form target-embedding derivation and iterative cross-attention alignment to harmless concepts, preserving unrelated generation ability.
- [SlimFlow: Training Smaller One-Step Diffusion Models with Rectified Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.12718): Compresses diffusion along both step count and model size by combining annealing reflow with flow-guided distillation for small one-step rectified-flow generators.
- [SMooDi: Stylized Motion Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.12783): Off-axis for image editing; it adapts a text-to-motion diffusion model with style guidance and a lightweight style adapter for stylized motion generation.
- [DreamStory: Open-Domain Story Visualization by LLM-Guided Multi-Subject Consistent Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.12899): Uses an LLM story director plus a multi-subject diffusion model with masked mutual self- and cross-attention so story frames keep character identity and semantics consistent.
- [Image Inpainting Models are Effective Tools for Instruction-guided Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.13139): Wins instruction-guided editing by decomposing edits into category classification, object identification, mask acquisition, and inpainting, avoiding joint LLM-diffusion fine-tuning.
- [Safe-SD: Safe and Traceable Stable Diffusion with Text Prompt Trigger for Invisible Generative Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.13188): Trains a unified latent diffusion wrapper to inject and detect imperceptible QR-style watermarks during generation using text prompt triggers, lambda sampling, and encryption.
- [TextureCrop: Enhancing Synthetic Image Detection Through Texture-Based Cropping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.15500): Improves synthetic-image detection by cropping high-frequency texture regions where generator artifacts concentrate, working as a plug-in preprocessor for existing detectors.
- [Learning to Manipulate Anywhere: A Visual Generalizable Framework For Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.15815): Off-axis robotics work; Maniwhere combines multi-view representation learning, spatial transformer modules, and curriculum randomization for visually generalizable manipulation policies.
- [CatVTON: Concatenation Is All You Need for Virtual Try-On with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.15886): Simplifies virtual try-on by concatenating person and garment images as diffusion inputs, dropping extra encoders and pose, parsing, and caption preprocessing while training mainly self-attention parameters.
- [Diffusion Models for Monocular Depth Estimation: Overcoming Challenging Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.16698): Uses depth-aware T2I diffusion to synthesize challenging scenes and self-distill monocular depth networks, improving depth estimation under adverse out-of-distribution conditions.
- [FlexiEdit: Frequency-Aware Latent Refinement for Enhanced Non-Rigid Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.17850): Improves non-rigid image edits by reducing high-frequency DDIM latent components in targeted regions and re-inverting to recover prompt fidelity after layout changes.
- [BetterDepth: Plug-and-Play Diffusion Refiner for Zero-Shot Monocular Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.17952): Refines zero-shot monocular depth maps with a conditional diffusion model that uses pretrained MDE predictions as global layout conditioning and adds local detail through patch masking.
- [SWIFT: Semantic Watermarking for Image Forgery Thwarting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.18995): Embeds caption-vector semantics through a modified HiDDeN watermarking channel and adds a local confidence metric tied to message recovery for tamper localization.
- [Temporal Feature Matters: A Framework for Diffusion Model Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.19547): Quantizes diffusion models by preserving timestep features with temporal-information reconstruction, cached quantized temporal features, and disturbance-aware strategy selection.
- [Mixture of Nested Experts: Adaptive Processing of Visual Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.19985): Off-axis for generation, but relevant to visual backbone efficiency; MoNE routes redundant image and video tokens through cheaper nested experts to reduce inference compute.
- [Diffusion Feedback Helps CLIP See Better](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.20171): Uses a text-to-image diffusion model as a self-supervised visual assistant, giving CLIP generative feedback that improves fine-grained visual attributes while preserving zero-shot ability.
- [Contrasting Deepfakes Diffusion via Contrastive Learning and Global-Local Similarities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.20337): Trains CoDE, a CLIP-derived contrastive embedding for diffusion-image detection, enforcing global-local similarities on 9.2M generated images from four generators.
- [Matting by Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.21017): Recasts image matting as latent-diffusion generation, adding architecture changes for high-resolution detailed alpha mattes with optional guidance cues.
- [Safeguard Text-to-Image Diffusion Models with Human Feedback Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.21032): Converts human feedback on generated images into learned textual tokens for concept removal, then uses self-distillation to align erasure with human judgment while preserving quality.
- [Direct Unlearning Optimization for Robust and Safe Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.21035): Uses preference optimization on paired image data plus output-preserving regularization to remove unsafe T2I concepts while resisting red-team prompts.
- [Informed Correctors for Discrete Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.21243): Adds a diffusion-informed predictor-corrector sampler, hollow transformers, and a tailored objective so discrete diffusion can sample tokenized images with fewer errors or better FID.
- [Detecting, Explaining, and Mitigating Memorization in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.21720): Detects memorized prompts from text-conditional prediction magnitudes, attributes memorization to individual tokens, and mitigates it through inference minimization or training-time filtering.
- [TurboEdit: Text-Based Image Editing Using Few-Step Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.00735): Adapts DDPM-noise inversion editing to few-step samplers by shifting the noise schedule to fix artifacts and using pseudo-guidance to strengthen edits in as few as three steps.
- [VAR-CLIP: Text-to-Image Generator with Visual Auto-Regressive Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.01181): Extends VAR next-scale autoregressive generation to text-to-image by encoding captions with CLIP text embeddings and training on BLIP2-captioned image-text data.
- [A Sharp Convergence Theory for The Probability Flow ODEs of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.02320): Proves non-asymptotic discrete-time convergence for probability-flow ODE samplers with score-estimation error, characterizing dimension and accuracy scaling for diffusion generation.
- [LaMamba-Diff: Linear-Time High-Fidelity Diffusion Models Based on Local Attention and Mamba](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.02615): Combines local attention with Mamba state-space blocks in a U-Net diffusion backbone, retaining global context and local details with lower GFLOPs than DiT on ImageNet.
- [Lumina-mGPT: Illuminate Flexible Photorealistic Text-to-Image Generation with Multimodal Generative Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.02657): Initializes decoder-only autoregressive image generation from multimodal generative pretraining, then uses FP-SFT and UniRep for variable-aspect photorealistic T2I and controllable generation.
- [Speculative Diffusion Decoding: Accelerating Language Generation through Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.05636): Classification concern: this is language generation rather than image editing; it uses a diffusion language model to draft multiple candidate tokens for speculative decoding by an autoregressive verifier.
- [LaWa: Using Latent Space for In-Generation Image Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.05868): Embeds watermarks in latent space during image generation, contributing an in-generation authenticity mechanism.
- [A Simple Early Exiting Framework for Accelerated Sampling in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.05927): Skips timestep-dependent subsets of score-network parameters during sampling, reducing diffusion inference cost while preserving image-synthesis quality and solver compatibility.
- [IDRetracor: Towards Visual Forensics against Malicious Face Swapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.06635): Trains a face-swap forensic pipeline to recover identity traces from manipulated faces, targeting attribution of malicious swaps rather than only binary fake detection.
- [Improving Synthetic Image Detection Towards Generalization: An Image Transformation Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.06741): Improves synthetic-image detector generalization by treating image transformations as distribution shifts and training detectors to retain fake cues after common post-processing.
- [ED4: Explicit Data-level Debiasing for Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.06779): Addresses content, forgery-source, and center-location bias in deepfake detectors with ClockMix data mixing and an adversarial spatial-consistency module.
- [Moderator: Moderating Text-to-Image Diffusion Models through Fine-grained Context-based Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.07728): Lets admins author fine-grained TTI moderation policies, self-generates violating images, computes task vectors by reverse fine-tuning, and subtracts them from the base model.
- [MVInpainter: Learning Multi-View Consistent Inpainting to Bridge 2D and 3D Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.08000): Recasts 3D editing as pose-free multi-view 2D inpainting, using video motion priors, reference key-value attention, and slot-attention flow aggregation.
- [TurboEdit: Instant text-based image editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.08332): Uses encoder-based iterative inversion for few-step diffusion and freezes noise maps while editing one prompt attribute, enabling 8-NFE inversion and 4-NFE text edits.
- [QMambaBSR: Burst Image Super-Resolution with Query State Space Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.08665): Fuses burst low-resolution frames with a Query State Space Model and adaptive upsampling to recover sub-pixel details while suppressing inconsistent high-frequency noise.
- [Ask, Attend, Attack: A Effective Decision-Based Black-Box Targeted Attack for Image-to-Text Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.08989): Classification concern: this attacks image-to-text models, not generators; it uses question-guided attention to craft decision-based black-box targeted adversarial images.
- [Timestep-Aware Diffusion Model for Extreme Image Rescaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.09151): Maps high-resolution and downscaled latent features with a pseudo-invertible module, then uses timestep alignment to recover structures and textures under extreme rescaling.
- [Detecting the Undetectable: Combining Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks and MLP for AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.09371): Pairs semantic image embeddings with an MLP-KAN hybrid classifier and evaluates against DALL-E 3, MidJourney, and Stable Diffusion 3 in out-of-distribution detection.
- [Photorealistic Object Insertion with Diffusion-Guided Inverse Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.09702): Uses a personalized diffusion model to guide physically based inverse rendering, recovering lighting, material, and tone-mapping parameters for plausible inserted objects.
- [FFAA: Multimodal Large Language Model based Explainable Open-World Face Forgery Analysis Assistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.10072): Fine-tunes an MLLM on OW-FFA-VQA face-forgery descriptions and reasoning, then uses a multi-answer decision system for explainable open-world forgery judgments.
- [Understanding Generative AI Content with Embedding Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.10437): Applies PCA-style reductions to foundation-model embeddings and shows real and AI-generated samples separate along interpretable directions useful for detector features.
- [A Gray-Box Attack Against Latent Diffusion Model-Based Image Editing by Posterior Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.10901): Protects images from unauthorized LDM editing by attacking only the VAE encoder and inducing diffusion or concentration collapse through a posterior-collapse loss.
- [MegaFusion: Extend Diffusion Models towards Higher-resolution Image Generation without Further Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.11001): Extends pretrained diffusion models to megapixel and varied-aspect generation without fine-tuning via truncate-and-relay denoising, dilated convolutions, and noise rescheduling.
- [Evolution of Detection Performance throughout the Online Lifespan of Synthetic Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.11541): Measures detector decay as synthetic images are reused online, then uses retrieval-assisted detection to preserve AUC and balanced accuracy across the image lifespan.
- [Scalable Autoregressive Image Generation with Mamba](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.12245): Builds AiM, a next-token autoregressive image generator that replaces Transformer blocks with Mamba state-space layers and scales to 1.3B parameters on ImageNet.
- [Open-Set Deepfake Detection: A Parameter-Efficient Adaptation Method with Forgery Style Mixture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.12791): Mixes forgery-style statistics across source domains and trains lightweight ViT modules for global and local cues, improving adaptation to unseen face-forgery domains.
- [Prompt-Softbox-Prompt: A Free-Text Embedding Control for Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.13623): Analyzes SDXL text embeddings and performs training-free edits by manipulating EOS and per-word embedding semantics while preserving global prompt information.
- [ForgeLens: Data-Efficient Forgery Focus for Generalizable Forgery Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.13697): Keeps the backbone frozen and adds WSGM guidance plus FAFormer feature integration so detectors focus on forgery-specific cues with little training data.
- [Draw Like an Artist: Complex Scene Generation With Diffusion Model via Composition, Painting, and Retouching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.13858): Defines complex-scene criteria and uses LLM decomposition, regional attention modulation, and retouching in a training-free composition-painting-retouching diffusion pipeline.
- [MagicMan: Generative Novel View Synthesis of Humans with 3D-Aware Diffusion and Iterative Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.14211): Combines a pretrained 2D diffusion prior with SMPL-X geometry, hybrid multi-view attention, RGB-normal dual branches, and iterative refinement for human novel-view synthesis.
- [OctFusion: Octree‐based Diffusion Models for 3D Shape Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.14732): Classification concern for this image page: it generates 3D shapes with an octree-based diffusion representation that allocates detail adaptively across sparse geometry.
- [Alfie: Democratising RGBA Image Generation With No $$$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.14826): Changes inference-time behavior of a pretrained Diffusion Transformer to generate complete RGBA illustration cutouts with usable alpha masks and no extra training.
- [Segmentation-guided Layer-wise Image Vectorization with Gradient Fills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.15741): Uses segmentation-guided layer-wise vectorization with gradient fills.
- [CoRe: Context-Regularized Text Embedding Learning for Text-to-Image Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.15914): Regularizes surrounding context-token outputs in the CLIP text encoder while learning a new concept embedding, improving personalized T2I identity preservation without generated-image supervision.
- [Distribution Backtracking Builds A Faster Convergence Trajectory for Diffusion Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.15991): Records a teacher diffusion model's degradation path and distills along the full convergence trajectory, reducing early score mismatch for one-step generator training.
- [Perceive-IR: Learning to Perceive Degradation Better for All-in-One Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.15994): Adds a backbone-agnostic quality perceiver and prompt-learning stage that estimates degradation type and severity before conditioning all-in-one restoration models.
- [Inversion Circle Interpolation: Diffusion-based Image Augmentation for Data-scarce Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.16266): Uses diffusion inversion-circle interpolation to synthesize class-preserving augmentations for data-scarce classifiers, making it a borderline augmentation entry rather than a core generator.
- [Self-Improving Diffusion Models with Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.16333): Uses self-synthesized data as negative guidance, steering diffusion generation away from synthetic-data manifolds toward real distributions to avoid model-autophagy degradation.
- [CSGO: Content-Style Composition in Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.16766): Builds IMAGStyle with 210K content-style-stylized triplets and trains independent content/style feature injection for image-driven, text-driven, and edited stylization.
- [STEREO: A Two-Stage Framework for Adversarially Robust Concept Erasing from Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.16807): Uses adversarial training to find embedding-space blind spots, then applies anchor-concept compositional fine-tuning to erase target concepts while preserving benign T2I utility.
- [AdaNAT: Exploring Adaptive Policy for Token-Based Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.00342): Treats token-generation scheduling as a Markov decision process and trains a lightweight policy network with adversarial rewards for per-sample non-autoregressive image-generation policies.
- [Accurate Compression of Text-to-Image Diffusion Models via Vector Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.00492): Tailors vector-quantization post-training compression to SDXL and SDXL-Turbo, pushing 2B-plus T2I diffusion weights to roughly 3 bits while preserving quality.
- [SPDiffusion: Semantic Protection Diffusion Models for Multi-concept Text-to-image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.01327): Extracts concept regions from cross-attention and protects them with SP-Attn so multi-concept prompts bind attributes to the right characters or objects.
- [LinFusion: 1 GPU, 1 Minute, 16K Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.02097): Distills Stable Diffusion into generalized linear-attention denoisers with normalization and non-causal inference, enabling zero-shot cross-resolution generation up to 16K on one GPU.
- [Exploring Low-Dimensional Subspaces in Diffusion Models for Controllable Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.02374): Uses low-rank Jacobian directions of the diffusion posterior mean predictor to derive LOCO Edit, a one-step training-free local editing method.
- [Diffusion Models Learn Low-Dimensional Distributions VIA Subspace Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.02426): Shows diffusion training with low-rank denoisers is equivalent to subspace clustering under mixture-of-low-rank-Gaussians assumptions, linking subspaces to semantic image editing.
- [Standing on the Shoulders of Giants: Reprogramming Visual-Language Model for General Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.02664): Reprograms CLIP for deepfake detection with learnable visual perturbations and sample-level adaptive text prompts, improving cross-dataset and cross-manipulation detection.
- [HiPrompt: Tuning-free Higher-Resolution Generation with Hierarchical MLLM Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.02919): Generates 4K-plus images by combining global prompts with MLLM patch-wise local prompts and frequency-decomposed denoising to reduce repetition and structural artifacts.
- [DKDM: Data-Free Knowledge Distillation for Diffusion Models with Any Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.03550): Distills new diffusion models of arbitrary architecture from existing diffusion models without real training data, using dynamic iterative time-domain knowledge extraction.
- [One-Shot Diffusion Mimicker for Handwritten Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.04004): Extracts high-frequency style cues from a single handwriting sample, fuses them with text-content conditions, and guides diffusion to synthesize matching multilingual handwriting.
- [SpotActor: Training-Free Layout-Controlled Consistent Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.04801): Defines layout-to-consistent-image generation and uses training-free dual energy guidance with layout-conditioned backward updates plus consistency-preserving forward sampling.
- [Elucidating Optimal Reward-Diversity Tradeoffs in Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.06493): Analyzes reward hacking in T2I preference optimization and proposes Annealed Importance Guidance as inference-time regularization for reward-diversity tradeoffs.
- [GigaGS: Scaling up Planar-Based 3D Gaussians for Large Scene Surface Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.06685): Scales 3D Gaussian surface reconstruction to large scenes with mutual-visibility partitioning and LoD-based multiview photometric and geometric consistency constraints.
- [Mamba Policy: Towards Efficient 3D Diffusion Policy with Hybrid Selective State Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.07163): Replaces heavy UNet diffusion-policy backbones for 3D manipulation with an XMamba block that mixes Mamba state models and attention while cutting parameters by over 80 percent.
- [Alignment of Diffusion Models: Fundamentals, Challenges, and Future](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.07253): Surveys diffusion-model alignment fundamentals, preference-tuning techniques, alignment benchmarks, and evaluation gaps for steering generated content toward human expectations.
- [LOCKEY: A Novel Approach to Model Authentication and Deepfake Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.07743): Combines key-conditioned model authentication with user-specific watermark embedding so invalid keys degrade generation and valid outputs can be traced to a user ID.
- [IFAdapter: Instance Feature Control for Grounded Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.08240): Introduces instance feature generation with appearance tokens and an instance semantic map, giving plug-and-play grounded T2I control over both bounding boxes and instance attributes.
- [GroundingBooth: Grounding Text-to-Image Customization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.08520): Adds a grounding module and subject-grounded cross-attention layer so zero-shot customized T2I can preserve identity while controlling object location and size.
- [DeCLIP: Decoding CLIP Representations for Deepfake Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.08849): Decodes pretrained CLIP features with a convolutional localization head to detect partially manipulated image regions and generalize across unseen generators.
- [InstantDrag: Improving Interactivity in Drag-based Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.08857): Builds an optimization-free drag editor with FlowGen for drag-conditioned optical flow and FlowDiffusion for flow-conditioned image updates from only an image and drag instruction.
- [Conditional sampling within generative diffusion models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.09650): Reviews conditional diffusion samplers that use joint distributions or pretrained marginals with explicit likelihoods for Bayesian inverse problems and other conditional generation tasks.
- [InteractPro: A Unified Framework for Motion-Aware Image Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.10090): Uses an LVLM planner to route image composition through physics simulation or video-diffusion motion modules, producing object-scene interactions rather than static insertions.
- [PSHuman: Photorealistic Single-image 3D Human Reconstruction using Cross-Scale Multiview Diffusion and Explicit Remeshing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.10141): Reconstructs 3D humans from one image by combining cross-scale multiview diffusion priors, identity-preserving facial generation, pose-consistent body views, and explicit remeshing.
- [Playground v3: Improving Text-to-Image Alignment with Deep-Fusion Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.10695): Integrates a decoder-only LLM as the text-conditioning backbone for PGv3 and adds an in-house captioner plus CapsBench to improve prompt adherence and text rendering.
- [Score Forgetting Distillation: A Swift, Data-Free Method for Machine Unlearning in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.11219): Aligns unsafe concept scores with safe ones inside score distillation, enabling data-free diffusion unlearning while preserving useful generation capability.
- [DroneDiffusion: Robust Quadrotor Dynamics Learning with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.11292): Models quadrotor dynamics as conditional diffusion sequence generation and plugs the learned multimodal uncertainty model into an adaptive controller with stability guarantees.
- [OmniGen: Unified Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.11340): Trains a single diffusion model for text-to-image, editing, subject-driven, and visual-conditional generation through instructions without task-specific plugins or intermediate pipelines.
- [StoryMaker: Towards Holistic Consistent Characters in Text-to-image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.12576): Preserves face, clothing, hairstyle, and body consistency for multi-character stories using cropped character conditions, face identities, PPR features, and separated cross-attention.
- [MaterialFusion: Enhancing Inverse Rendering with Material Diffusion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.15273): Adds StableMaterial, a 2D diffusion prior trained on BlenderVault albedo, material, and relighting data, to improve inverse rendering under novel illumination.
- [PixWizard: Versatile Image-to-Image Visual Assistant with Open-Language Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.15278): Unifies image manipulation and translation as image-text-to-image generation with free-form instructions and an Omni Pixel-to-Pixel training corpus.
- [TFG: Unified Training-Free Guidance for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.15761): Formalizes training-free diffusion guidance around a target-property predictor, testing how predictor-driven sampling steers unconditional generators without additional model training.
- [Zero-Shot Detection of AI-Generated Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.15875): Uses frozen vision-language representations and zero-shot distributional cues to detect AI-generated images without generator-specific training.
- [MaskBit: Embedding-free Image Generation via Bit Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.16211): Generates images directly from binary bit tokens instead of learned codebook embeddings, simplifying discrete-token visual generation.
- [GenCAD: Image-Conditioned Computer-Aided Design Generation with Transformer-Based Contrastive Representation and Diffusion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.16294): Conditions CAD generation on an input image using transformer contrastive representations and diffusion priors to synthesize structured design outputs.
- [Generative Object Insertion in Gaussian Splatting with a Multi-View Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.16938): Inserts objects into 3D Gaussian Splatting scenes by using a multiview diffusion model to generate view-consistent object appearances.
- [Revisiting Face Forgery Detection: From Facial Representation to Forgery Detection.](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.16945): Reframes face forgery detection around facial representations, using identity and representation cues rather than only low-level artifacts.
- [From geometric mimicry to comprehensive generation: a context-informed multimodal diffusion model for urban morphology synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.17049): Synthesizes urban morphology with a context-informed multimodal diffusion model that moves beyond geometric mimicry toward controllable city-form generation.
- [Degradation-Guided One-Step Image Super-Resolution with Diffusion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.17058): Performs one-step super-resolution by conditioning diffusion priors on degradation guidance, targeting fast restoration of real degraded inputs.
- [ID3: Identity-Preserving-yet-Diversified Diffusion Models for Synthetic Face Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.17576): Generates synthetic face-recognition data that preserves identity while diversifying nuisance factors, balancing recognition utility and sample variety.
- [Dark Miner: Defend against undesired generation for text-to-image diffusion models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.17682): Mines and suppresses dark prompt directions in T2I diffusion models to reduce undesired generation without retraining the full model.
- [Resolving Multi-Condition Confusion for Finetuning-Free Personalized Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.17920): Separates conflicting reference and condition signals at inference time to improve personalized image generation without finetuning.
- [FreeEdit: Mask-Free Reference-Based Image Editing With Multi-Modal Instruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.18071): Edits images from reference images and multimodal instructions without masks, using model-side reference grounding rather than manual region specification.
- [O(d/T) Convergence Theory for Diffusion Probabilistic Models under Minimal Assumptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.18959): Proves diffusion sampling convergence under minimal assumptions with an O(d/T) rate, clarifying iteration complexity for probabilistic diffusion models.
- [Multimodal Pragmatic Jailbreak on Text-to-image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.19149): Studies pragmatic multimodal jailbreaks against T2I systems, showing how image-text context can bypass generation safeguards.
- [Restore Anything with Masks: Leveraging Mask Image Modeling for Blind All-in-One Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.19403): Uses masked image modeling for blind all-in-one restoration, letting a single model infer and repair varied degradations through mask-guided reconstruction.
- [Magnet: We Never Know How Text-to-Image Diffusion Models Work, Until We Learn How Vision-Language Models Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.19967): Interprets T2I diffusion behavior through VLM function analysis, linking vision-language mechanisms to generator control and failure modes.
- [Conditional Diffusion Models are Minimax-Optimal and Manifold-Adaptive for Conditional Distribution Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.20124): Establishes minimax-optimal, manifold-adaptive guarantees for conditional diffusion models, formalizing when they estimate conditional image-like distributions efficiently.
- [EnzymeFlow: Generating Reaction-specific Enzyme Catalytic Pockets through Flow Matching and Co-Evolutionary Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.00327): Uses flow matching and co-evolutionary dynamics to generate reaction-specific enzyme catalytic pockets, an off-axis structural biology generation method.
- [Posterior-Mean Rectified Flow: Towards Minimum MSE Photo-Realistic Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.00418): Trains rectified-flow restoration around posterior-mean targets to reduce mean-square error while retaining photo-realistic image details.
- [Scene Graph Disentanglement and Composition for Generalizable Complex Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.00447): Disentangles scene-graph objects and relations, then composes them to improve generalization on complex text-to-image layouts.
- [A Hitchhikers Guide to Fine-Grained Face Forgery Detection Using Common Sense Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.00485): Adds commonsense reasoning cues to fine-grained face forgery detection so localized manipulation judgments can be explained beyond visual artifacts.
- [Aggregation of Multi Diffusion Models for Enhancing Learned Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.01262): Aggregates multiple pretrained diffusion models to improve learned visual representations rather than training a new generator from scratch.
- [KnobGen: Controlling the Sophistication of Artwork in Sketch-Based Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.01595): Adds a controllable sophistication knob to sketch-conditioned diffusion, letting users tune artwork complexity while preserving sketch guidance.
- [Accelerating Auto-regressive Text-to-Image Generation with Training-free Speculative Jacobi Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.01699): Speeds autoregressive T2I sampling with training-free speculative Jacobi decoding, parallelizing token proposals without changing model weights.
- [VitaGlyph: Vitalizing Artistic Typography with Flexible Dual-branch Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.01738): Uses a dual-branch diffusion design to generate artistic typography that balances glyph legibility with flexible visual style.
- [ImageFolder: Autoregressive Image Generation with Folded Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.01756): Folds visual tokens into an autoregressive sequence structure to improve image generation efficiency and spatial context modeling.
- [A Spark of Vision-Language Intelligence: 2-Dimensional Autoregressive Transformer for Efficient Finegrained Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.01912): Uses a two-dimensional autoregressive transformer to model fine-grained image tokens while preserving spatial structure more efficiently than raster-order decoding.
- [Discrete Copula Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.01949): Introduces copula-based discrete diffusion to model dependencies among categorical variables for discrete visual or structured generation.
- [Eliminating Oversaturation and Artifacts of High Guidance Scales in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.02416): Diagnoses high classifier-free guidance artifacts and modifies diffusion guidance behavior to reduce oversaturation while retaining prompt strength.
- [PnP-Flow: Plug-and-Play Image Restoration with Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.02423): Uses pretrained flow-matching generative priors as plug-and-play regularizers for image restoration without task-specific retraining.
- [ControlAR: Controllable Image Generation with Autoregressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.02705): Extends autoregressive image generators with lightweight encoders for Canny, depth, and other spatial controls, fusing control and image tokens during conditional decoding instead of prefilling control tokens.
- [NETS: A Non-Equilibrium Transport Sampler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.02711): Adds a non-equilibrium transport sampler that augments annealed-importance-sampling dynamics with a learned drift to reduce unbiasing-weight variance for generative sampling.
- [FakeShield: Explainable Image Forgery Detection and Localization via Multi-modal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.02761): Trains an MLLM-based forgery detector and localizer on GPT-4o-enriched tamper descriptions, combining explainable detection and multimodal localization modules for masks and textual evidence.
- [Tuning Timestep-Distilled Diffusion Model Using Pairwise Sample Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.03190): Fine-tunes few-step distilled diffusion models by increasing likelihood margins between training images and samples from the current distilled model, preserving low-latency generation during style adaptation.
- [Dynamic Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.03456): Builds DyDiT, a Diffusion Transformer variant that gates computation by timestep and spatial region so image generation avoids redundant static inference.
- [Not All Diffusion Model Activations Have Been Evaluated as Discriminative Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.03558): Audits underused diffusion activations such as attention queries, keys, and ViT-block features, then derives qualitative filters for selecting dense discriminative features from generative backbones.
- [Look Twice Before You Answer: Memory-Space Visual Retracing for Hallucination Mitigation in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.03577): Mitigates MLLM hallucination by retracing visual evidence in memory space before answering, an adjacent VLM reliability method rather than an image generator.
- [How Discrete and Continuous Diffusion Meet: Comprehensive Analysis of Discrete Diffusion Models via a Stochastic Integral Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.03601): Gives a stochastic-integral framework for discrete diffusion, unifying error analysis and tau-leaping bounds with continuous diffusion theory.
- [Chain-of-Jailbreak Attack for Image Generation Models via Editing Step by Step](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.03869): Decomposes malicious T2I requests into iterative editing sub-queries and introduces CoJ-Bench to measure step-by-step safety bypasses.
- [Epsilon-VAE: Denoising as Visual Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.04081): Replaces an autoencoder's single-step decoder with diffusion denoising conditioned on encoder latents, improving reconstruction rFID and downstream generation FID at comparable compression.
- [Distillation-Free One-Step Diffusion for Real-World Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.04224): Builds D3SR, a one-step real-world super-resolution diffusion model supervised by a large diffusion discriminator over noisy latent features and an edge-aware DISTS perceptual loss.
- [StreetSurfGS: Scalable Urban Street Surface Reconstruction With Planar-Based Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.04354): Tailors Gaussian Splatting to urban street reconstruction with planar octrees, segmented training, and guided smoothing for sparse long-trajectory scenes.
- [DiffusionFake: Enhancing Generalization in Deepfake Detection via Guided Stable Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.04372): Injects detector features into frozen Stable Diffusion and reconstructs source and target identities, forcing deepfake detectors to learn disentangled features that generalize to unseen manipulations.
- [CAR: Controllable Autoregressive Modeling for Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.04671): Injects progressively refined control representations into each step of a pretrained visual autoregressive model, giving ControlNet-like conditioning with fewer training resources than training from scratch.
- [ACDC: Autoregressive Coherent Multimodal Generation using Diffusion Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.04721): Combines autoregressive global token generation with memory-conditioned diffusion correction at inference, using an LLM memory module to reduce artifacts in story and video generation.
- [PostEdit: Posterior Sampling for Efficient Zero-Shot Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.04844): Uses posterior sampling with a measurement term tied to source features and Langevin dynamics, enabling inversion-free, training-free text image editing that preserves background regions.
- [Bridging SFT and DPO for Diffusion Model Alignment with Self-Sampling Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.05255): Aligns diffusion generators with self-sampling preference optimization, replaying historical checkpoints for preference pairs and switching between DPO and SFT according to sample quality.
- [Image Watermarks are Removable Using Controllable Regeneration from Clean Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.05470): Removes image watermarks through controllable regeneration, training semantic and spatial adapters to guide diffusion from clean or partially noised latents while preserving visible content.
- [DiffusionGuard: A Robust Defense Against Malicious Diffusion-based Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.05694): Protects images from unauthorized diffusion edits by optimizing adversarial noise for early denoising steps and augmenting masks to improve transfer and robustness against masked editing.
- [Manifolds, Random Matrices and Spectral Gaps: The geometric phases of generative diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.05898): Analyzes score-Jacobian spectra to identify diffusion phases for manifold coverage and consolidation during generation.
- [Sparse Repellency for Shielded Generation in Text-to-image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.06025): Steers pretrained diffusion trajectories away from protected or already generated images with sparse SDE repellency terms, increasing diversity and reducing training-set recreation.
- [X2-DFD: A Framework for Explainable and Extensible Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.06126): Builds an MLLM-based deepfake detector with model feature assessment, explainable dataset construction, strong-feature strengthening, weak-feature supplementing, and matched fine-tuning and inference for detection plus explanations.
- [InstantIR: Blind Image Restoration with Instant Generative Reference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.06551): Restores blindly degraded images by extracting compact vision-encoder references, decoding current diffusion latents at each step, and adapting sampling to degradation intensity.
- [Representation Alignment for Generation: Training Diffusion Transformers Is Easier Than You Think](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.06940): Uses representation alignment to simplify diffusion-transformer training for generation.
- [EvolveDirector: Approaching Advanced Text-to-Image Generation with Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.07133): Trains a public T2I base model from API-generated image pairs while VLMs iteratively evaluate, expand, delete, and mutate the training set to reduce closed-model distillation cost.
- [IterComp: Iterative Composition-Aware Feedback Learning from Model Gallery for Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.07171): Builds a six-model gallery for attribute binding and spatial composition, trains composition-aware reward models from ranked image pairs, and iteratively refines both rewards and generator.
- [An undetectable watermark for generative image models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.07369): Watermarks diffusion outputs by sampling initial latents from a pseudorandom error-correcting-code distribution, targeting provenance signals that remain computationally indistinguishable from ordinary generations.
- [I-Max: Maximize the Resolution Potential of Pre-trained Rectified Flow Transformers with Projected Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.07536): Extends pretrained rectified-flow transformers to higher resolutions without tuning through projected flow and an inference toolkit that stabilizes resolution extrapolation in Lumina-Next and Flux.
- [Steering Masked Discrete Diffusion Models via Discrete Denoising Posterior Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.08134): Casts masked discrete diffusion steering as posterior prediction, training simulation-free objectives that support class-conditioned pixel modeling and reward-guided generation.
- [DART: Denoising Autoregressive Transformer for Scalable Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.08159): Unifies diffusion and autoregressive modeling in a non-Markovian transformer that denoises image patches spatially and spectrally without image quantization.
- [Meissonic: Revitalizing Masked Generative Transformers for Efficient High-Resolution Text-to-Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.08261): Revives masked image modeling for 1024px T2I with architecture changes, positional encodings, optimized sampling, preference-informed micro-conditions, high-quality data, and feature compression.
- [Avoiding mode collapse in diffusion models fine-tuned with reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.08315): Stabilizes reward fine-tuning of diffusion models by selectively updating denoising steps and applying sliding-window parameter regularization to reduce mode collapse.
- [Quality Prediction of AI Generated Images and Videos: Emerging Trends and Opportunities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.08534): Reviews IQA and VQA methods for monitoring AI-generated and enhanced visual content quality, covering prompt fidelity and quality-of-experience concerns.
- [Losing dimensions: Geometric memorization in generative diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.08727): Measures score-field latent dimensionality to show memorization as a gradual collapse of independent variation directions, explaining how scarce data leads to local replication.
