- [CtrLoRA: An Extensible and Efficient Framework for Controllable Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.09400): Trains a shared Base ControlNet plus condition-specific LoRA modules, adapting T2I spatial control to new condition types with about 1,000 image-condition pairs.
- [Text4Seg: Reimagining Image Segmentation as Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.09855): Casts segmentation masks as semantic-descriptor text plus row-wise run-length encoding so MLLMs can output segmentation through autoregressive text generation.
- [MuseTalk: Real-Time High Quality Lip Synchronization with Latent Space Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10122): Uses facial abstract pretraining, lip-sync adversarial fine-tuning, informative frame sampling, and dynamic mouth-region sampling for real-time latent-space video dubbing.
- [GraphCLIP: Enhancing Transferability in Graph Foundation Models for Text-Attributed Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10329): Pretrains text-attributed graph encoders with LLM-generated graph-summary pairs and contrastive learning for cross-domain graph transfer.
- [FasterDiT: Towards Faster Diffusion Transformers Training without Architecture Modification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10356): Accelerates DiT training without architecture changes by analyzing SNR density, choosing robust timestep supervision, and adding a simple supervision scheme that reaches DiT-level FID faster.
- [DOME: Taming Diffusion Model into High-Fidelity Controllable Occupancy World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10429): Predicts future 3D occupancy frames with a spatial-temporal diffusion transformer, targeting controllable autonomous-driving world modeling.
- [Towards Reliable Verification of Unauthorized Data Usage in Personalized Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10437): Optimizes data-owner coatings so personalized T2I fine-tuning learns them as task-relevant features, improving black-box verification of unauthorized theme or style data use.
- [SANA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10629): Builds efficient high-resolution text-to-image synthesis with a 32x-compression autoencoder, linear diffusion transformers, and a decoder-only text encoder, making strong image-generation quality practical on smaller hardware.
- [TALK-Act: Enhance Textural-Awareness for 2D Speaking Avatar Reenactment with Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10696): Drives speaking avatars with explicit 2D and 3D motion guidance, motion-enhanced textural alignment, and memory-based hand recovery after brief person-specific footage.
- [HART: Efficient Visual Generation with Hybrid Autoregressive Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10812): Combines a discrete autoregressive model for coarse latent tokens with a small residual diffusion module for continuous details, improving 1024px generation throughput and reconstruction FID.
- [Ctrl-U: Robust Conditional Image Generation via Uncertainty-aware Reward Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11236): Regularizes conditional image generation with uncertainty-aware reward modeling, using reward variance to downweight unreliable feedback and improve fidelity to user conditions.
- [SGEdit: Bridging LLM with Text2Image Generative Model for Scene Graph-based Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11815): Parses source images into scene graphs with objects, relations, masks, and descriptions, then uses LLM-controlled attention modulation for object add, delete, replace, and adjust edits.
- [Improving Long-Text Alignment for Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11817): Handles long T2I prompts with segment-level encoding and decomposed CLIP-based preference optimization, reweighting text-relevant and visual-preference scores to reduce overfitting.
- [3DIS: Depth-Driven Decoupled Instance Synthesis for Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.12669): Decouples multi-instance T2I into coarse depth-map layout generation with an LDM3D adapter and training-free ControlNet rendering for fine-grained object attributes.
- [Meta-Unlearning on Diffusion Models: Preventing Relearning Unlearned Concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.12777): Adds a meta objective to diffusion concept unlearning so retained benign concepts self-destruct under malicious fine-tuning, preventing relearning of removed concepts.
- [TCP-Diffusion: A Multi-modal Diffusion Model for Global Tropical Cyclone Precipitation Forecasting with Change Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.13175): Forecasts tropical-cyclone precipitation with a multimodal diffusion model conditioned on past rainfall, environmental variables, and numerical weather predictions.
- [MagicTailor: Component-Controllable Personalization in Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.13370): Trains component-controllable personalization with dynamic masked degradation and dual-stream balancing so users can reconfigure individual concept parts without semantic pollution.
- [Solving Prior Distribution Mismatch in Diffusion Models via Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.13431): Matches the reverse initial distribution to the forward terminal distribution with an optimal-transport map, reducing prior error behind nonzero terminal SNR and denoising accumulation.
- [Probing the latent hierarchical structure of data via diffusion models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.13770): Uses forward-backward diffusion experiments to measure latent hierarchical structure, showing denoising changes occur in correlated chunks near phase transitions.
- [PUMA: Empowering Unified MLLM with Multi-Granular Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.13861): Treats multi-granular visual features as both MLLM inputs and outputs, then pretrains and instruction-tunes for T2I diversity and image-manipulation controllability.
- [Fluid: Scaling Autoregressive Text-to-image Generative Models with Continuous Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.13863): Trains a random-order autoregressive T2I model on continuous tokens, showing continuous latents and non-raster generation improve FID and GenEval relative to discrete-token designs.
- [FiTv2: Scalable and Improved Flexible Vision Transformer for Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.13925): Upgrades Flexible Vision Transformer with query-key normalization, AdaLN-LoRA, rectified-flow scheduling, and logit-normal sampling for arbitrary resolutions and aspect ratios.
- [Personalized Image Generation with Large Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.14170): Builds Pigeon for personalized image generation from noisy user-image histories and multimodal instructions, using masked preference reconstruction and pairwise preference alignment.
- [Unified Convergence Analysis for Score-Based Diffusion Models with Deterministic Samplers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.14237): Provides convergence guarantees for deterministic score-based diffusion samplers across general forward processes, extending analysis beyond stochastic sampler techniques.
- [Dynamic Negative Guidance of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.14398): Replaces constant negative prompting with time- and state-dependent guidance, estimating posterior class probabilities during denoising to remove unwanted content with less image damage.
- [BiGR: Harnessing Binary Latent Codes for Image Generation and Improved Visual Representation Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.14672): Uses binary latent codes, masked modeling, a binary transcoder, and entropy-ordered sampling to support generation, inpainting, outpainting, editing, and T2I in one framework.
- [Adversarial Score identity Distillation: Rapidly Surpassing the Teacher in One Step](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.14919): Adds adversarial loss to score-identity distillation by reusing the score-network encoder as a discriminator, speeding one-step distillation from EDM and EDM2 teachers.
- [SeaS: Few-Shot Industrial Anomaly Image Generation with Separation and Sharing Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.14987): Trains a unified industrial diffusion generator for normal products, anomaly variants, and masks with unbalanced abnormal prompts and decoupled anomaly alignment.
- [Synergistic Dual Spatial-aware Generation of Image-to-Text and Text-to-Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.15312): Jointly models spatial image-to-text and text-to-image with a shared 3D scene graph and spatial dual discrete diffusion that lets easier 3D-to-output paths guide inverse mappings.
- [Erasing Undesirable Concepts in Diffusion Models with Adversarial Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.15618): Erases unsafe concepts in Stable Diffusion by identifying adversarial concepts most affected by parameter changes and preserving them to reduce collateral damage.
- [Elucidating the design space of language models for image generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.16257): Ablates tokenizer, language-model backbone, vocabulary, scaling, and sampling choices for image-token prediction, showing larger autoregressive models capture global image context better.
- [One-Step Diffusion Distillation through Score Implicit Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.16794): Distills pretrained diffusion into one-step generators with score implicit matching, computing score-divergence gradients without training data and extending the method to T2I.
- [Scalable Ranked Preference Optimization for Text-To-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.18013): Creates synthetic ranked preference data with reward models and trains SDXL and SD3-Medium using RankDPO, improving prompt following on T2I-CompBench, GenEval, and DPG-Bench.
- [FreCaS: Efficient Higher-Resolution Image Generation via Frequency-aware Cascaded Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.18410): Generates above-training-resolution images through frequency-aware cascaded sampling, progressively expanding resolution bands and fusing cross-attention maps to preserve layout.
- [Scaling up Masked Diffusion Models on Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.18514): Establishes scaling behavior for masked diffusion language models up to 1.1B parameters and adds unsupervised classifier-free guidance for conditional text inference.
- [Ali-AUG: Innovative Approaches to Labeled Data Augmentation using One-Step Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.18678): Uses a one-step Stable Diffusion augmentation model with skip connections and LoRA to insert masked defect features into industrial images for labeled detector training data.
- [Schedule Your Edit: A Simple yet Effective Diffusion Noise Schedule for Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.18756): Introduces a logistic noise schedule for DDIM inversion, removing singularities that accumulate prediction errors and improving content preservation in text-guided editing.
- [Denoising diffusion probabilistic models are optimally adaptive to unknown low dimensionality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.18784): Proves DDPM iteration complexity adapts nearly linearly to intrinsic dimension, explaining efficient sampling on low-dimensional data manifolds.
- [Multi-Scale Diffusion: Enhancing Spatial Layout in High-Resolution Panoramic Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.18830): Improves high-resolution panorama layout by transferring structural information from low-resolution generations into higher-resolution diffusion outputs with multi-scale gradient guidance.
- [Diff-Instruct++: Training One-step Text-to-image Generator Model to Align with Human Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.18881): Aligns one-step T2I generators to human rewards with an integral KL-regularized objective, deriving how classifier-free guidance distillation relates to RLHF.
- [Structured Diffusion Models with Mixture of Gaussians as Prior Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.19149): Replaces the standard Gaussian diffusion prior with a mixture of Gaussians chosen to encode data structure, with a compatible training procedure for image and operational datasets.
- [Utilizing Image Transforms and Diffusion Models for Generative Modeling of Short and Long Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.19538): Converts short and long time series into images with invertible transforms, then applies vision diffusion models for unified time-series generation.
- [An Efficient Watermarking Method for Latent Diffusion Models via Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.20202): Embeds watermarks into frozen Latent Diffusion Models through LoRA parameters and a dynamic loss schedule that balances generated-image quality with watermark fidelity.
- [GrounDiT: Grounding Diffusion Transformers via Noisy Patch Transplantation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.20474): Grounds DiT generation without training by denoising object-sized noisy patches in separate branches and transplanting them into bounding-box regions at every timestep.
- [Diff-Instruct*: Towards Human-Preferred One-step Text-to-image Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.20898): Post-trains a 2.6B one-step SDXL generator with score-based RLHF regularization, improving ImageReward, PickScore, and CLIP score while using much less inference time.
- [Attention Overlap Is Responsible for The Entity Missing Problem in Text-to-image Diffusion Models!](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.20972): Diagnoses T2I entity omission as overlap between cross-attention maps and reduces missing objects with losses over IoU, center-of-mass distance, KL divergence, or compactness.
- [Shallow Diffuse: Robust and Invisible Watermarking through Low-Dimensional Subspaces in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.21088): Embeds invisible watermarks by placing much of the signal in the null space of a low-dimensional generation subspace, decoupling detection from image synthesis.
- [AdvI2I: Adversarial Image Attack on Image-to-Image Diffusion models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.21471): Optimizes adversarial input images that push image-to-image diffusion models toward unsafe outputs while keeping text prompts benign, exposing a gap beyond prompt filtering.
- [Exploring Local Memorization in Diffusion Models via Bright Ending Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.21665): Finds a bright-ending cross-attention anomaly that localizes memorized patches in T2I outputs and improves detectors for local rather than whole-image memorization.
- [PrefPaint: Aligning Image Inpainting Diffusion Model with Human Preference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.21966): Aligns inpainting diffusion with human aesthetic preference by training a reward model on about 51,000 annotated images and fine-tuning with reward-error-aware reinforcement learning.
- [Rare-to-Frequent: Unlocking Compositional Generation Power of Diffusion Models on Rare Concepts with LLM Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.22376): Uses LLM planning to expose frequent concepts related to rare prompt compositions during diffusion sampling, improving rare object-attribute composition on RareBench without training.
- [Consistency Diffusion Bridge Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.22637): Learns consistency functions for diffusion denoising bridge PF-ODEs, letting bridge models for image translation and restoration sample 4x to 50x faster than the base DDBM.
- [FlowDCN: Exploring DCN-like Architectures for Fast Image Generation with Arbitrary Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.22655): Builds an arbitrary-resolution flow generator from group-wise deformable convolution blocks, reducing computation relative to transformer diffusion while improving ImageNet sFID.
- [Controlling Language and Diffusion Models by Transporting Activations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.23054): Steers text-to-image diffusion by transporting internal activations with optimal transport, enabling fine-grained style control and concept negation with negligible extra compute.
- [CLIPErase: Efficient Unlearning of Visual-Textual Associations in CLIP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.23330): Separates CLIP unlearning into forgetting, retention, and consistency modules so selected visual-textual associations are removed while downstream zero-shot performance on retained data is preserved.
- [MoLE: Enhancing Human-centric Text-to-image Diffusion via Mixture of Low-rank Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.23332): Improves human-centric T2I with more than one million human-scene images plus hand and face close-ups, routing low-rank experts to refine weak body parts.
- [How Do Flow Matching Models Memorize and Generalize in Sample Data Subspaces?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.23594): Derives optimal velocity fields for flow matching on discrete data to explain memorization, subspace representation, and generalization outside sample subspaces.
- [EDT: An Efficient Diffusion Transformer Framework Inspired by Human-like Sketching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.23788): Reduces diffusion-transformer cost with a lightweight architecture, training-free sketch-inspired attention modulation, and token-relation masking for faster image synthesis.
- [Scaling Concept With Text-Guided Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.24151): Decomposes concepts in text-guided diffusion and scales them up or down in real inputs, enabling zero-shot enhancement or suppression without adding new scene elements.
- [Comparison-based Active Preference Learning for Multi-dimensional Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.00524): Introduces AMPLe, a Bayesian active preference-learning loop that infers implicit multidimensional user preferences from comparative feedback for personalized generation.
- [Randomized Autoregressive Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.00776): Randomly permutes image-token factorization orders during autoregressive training with annealing, improving bidirectional context modeling while staying compatible with language-model frameworks.
- [Infinite-Resolution Integral Noise Warping for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.01212): Derives an efficient integral noise-warping algorithm that preserves Gaussian noise statistics while adding temporal consistency for training-free video adaptation of image diffusion models.
- [Diffusion Models as Cartoonists: The Curious Case of High Density Regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.01293): Tracks high-density modes in diffusion denoising and finds that higher-likelihood samples often become cartoon-like or blurry, separating density from typical visual quality.
- [Model Integrity when Unlearning with T2I Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.02068): Introduces a retention metric comparing original and unlearned T2I outputs, then proposes unlearning baselines that better preserve retain-distribution image quality.
- [On Improved Conditioning Mechanisms and Pre-training Strategies for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.03177): Reimplements LDM recipes to isolate semantic and control metadata conditioning, then disentangles the condition streams to improve ImageNet and CC12M generation FID.
- [DiT4Edit: Diffusion Transformer for Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.03286): Adapts Diffusion Transformers to image editing with DPM-Solver inversion, unified attention control, and patch merging for shape-aware edits at high and arbitrary resolutions.
- [ROBIN: Robust and Invisible Watermarks for Diffusion Models with Adversarial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.03862): Implants a robust watermark in an intermediate diffusion state and optimizes a hiding prompt so the final image conceals the mark while reverse generation verifies it.
- [ET-SEED: Efficient Trajectory-Level SE(3) Equivariant Diffusion Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.03990): Uses trajectory-level SE(3) equivariant diffusion to generate robot manipulation action sequences with fewer demonstrations than standard diffusion policies.
- [Community Forensics: Using Thousands of Generators to Train Fake Image Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.04125): Samples 2.7M images from 4,803 latent diffusion models plus commercial generators to train fake-image detectors that generalize to unseen generation sources.
- [DiMSUM: Diffusion Mamba - A Scalable and Unified Spatial-Frequency Method for Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.04168): Combines Mamba scans with wavelet subbands, cross-attention fusion, and a shared global transformer so diffusion models capture spatial detail and frequency structure.
- [Multi-Reward as Condition for Instruction-based Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.04713): Conditions instruction editors on GPT-4o-derived reward scores and textual feedback for instruction following, detail preservation, and generation quality, learning from imperfect edit triplets.
- [Boosting Latent Diffusion with Perceptual Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.04873): Uses internal autoencoder decoder features as a latent perceptual loss during LDM or flow-matching training, sharpening generated images and improving FID at 256 and 512 resolutions.
- [LLM2CLIP: Powerful Language Model Unlocks Richer Visual Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.04997): Embeds an LLM into CLIP fine-tuning so image-text representations handle long, complex captions with nearly standard CLIP training cost.
- [A Quality-Centric Framework for Generic Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.05335): Adds a Quality Evaluator, low-quality data enhancement, and a forgery-quality curriculum with pacing so detector training accounts for sample forgery quality and generalizes better.
- [Region-Aware Text-to-Image Generation via Hard Binding and Soft Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.06558): Decouples regional T2I into hard object-region binding and soft refinement, improving layout composition without model-specific trainable modules.
- [ENAT: Rethinking Spatial-temporal Interactions in Token-based Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.06959): Analyzes non-autoregressive token generation and redesigns spatial and temporal token interactions to improve few-step image synthesis.
- [Edify Image: High-Quality Image Generation with Pixel Space Laplacian Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.07126): Builds cascaded pixel-space Laplacian diffusion models whose frequency-band noise process supports T2I, 4K upsampling, ControlNets, panoramas, and customization.
- [Token Merging for Training-Free Semantic Binding in Text-to-Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.07132): Uses training-free token merging to bind attributes and sub-objects in T2I prompts without layout specifications or model finetuning.
- [OmniEdit: Building Image Editing Generalist Models Through Specialist Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.07199): Trains a generalist image editor from specialist supervision to broaden edit skills, reduce noisy synthetic pairs, and support higher-resolution flexible-aspect editing.
- [Watermark Anything with Localized Messages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.07231): Introduces WAM, a localized image watermarking model whose embedder hides messages and whose extractor segments watermarked regions and recovers one or more messages.
- [Add-it: Training-Free Object Insertion in Images With Pretrained Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.07232): Extends diffusion attention over the source image, prompt, and generated image so training-free object insertion can find natural locations while preserving scene detail.
- [TIPO: Text to Image with Text Presampling for Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.08127): Uses a lightweight prompt-refinement model to sample richer T2I prompts from a targeted semantic subdistribution, improving visual quality without LLM or RL overhead.
- [Golden Noise for Diffusion Models: A Learning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.09502): Learns text-conditioned noise prompts that perturb Gaussian seeds into golden noises with better prompt alignment and human preference during diffusion sampling.
- [Adaptive Non-uniform Timestep Sampling for Accelerating Diffusion Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.09998): Accelerates diffusion training by prioritizing timesteps with high stochastic-gradient variance instead of sampling denoising times uniformly.
- [Safe Text-to-Image Generation: Simply Sanitize the Prompt Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.10329): Sanitizes T2I prompt embeddings before generation, targeting unsafe textual semantics that concept-erasure methods can miss under adversarial attacks.
- [Time Step Generating: A Universal Synthesized Deepfake Image Detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.11016): Builds a synthesized-image detector around diffusion timestep generation cues, using generation-process artifacts rather than generator-specific training data to improve cross-model deepfake detection.
- [Stacking Brick by Brick: Aligned Feature Isolation for Incremental Face Forgery Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.11396): Isolates forgery-type features during incremental face-forgery learning to reduce catastrophic forgetting across newly introduced manipulation methods.
- [CLUE-MARK: Watermarking Diffusion Models using CLWE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.11434): Provides a provably undetectable watermarking scheme for diffusion models based on CLWE, requiring no generator changes while preserving output quality.
- [Safe + Safe = Unsafe? Exploring How Safe Images Can Be Exploited to Jailbreak Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.11496): Shows that individually safe images and prompts can combine into LVLM jailbreaks, exposing compositional gaps in multimodal safety guardrails.
- [Aligning Few-Step Diffusion Models with Dense Reward Difference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.11727): Introduces SDPO, using dual noisy and clean states plus dense reward prediction to align few-step diffusion models with low-variance mixed-step optimization.
- [Continuous Speculative Decoding for Autoregressive Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.11925): Extends speculative decoding to continuous autoregressive image models with corrections for draft-target mismatch and modified continuous distributions.
- [Adversarial Diffusion Compression for Real-World Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.13383): Distills one-step OSEDiff super-resolution into a streamlined diffusion-GAN through adversarial diffusion compression for faster real-world ISR.
- [AI-generated Image Detection: Passive or Watermark?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.13553): Builds ImageDetectBench to compare passive detectors and watermark-based detectors for AI-generated images on effectiveness, robustness, and efficiency.
- [Detecting Human Artifacts from Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.13842): Curates the 37K-image Human Artifact Dataset and trains HADM to localize malformed human body parts in T2I outputs.
- [On the fairness, diversity and reliability of text-to-image generative models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.13981): Evaluates T2I reliability under global and local embedding perturbations to expose biased, unreliable, or manipulable class and concept behavior.
- [High-Resolution Image Synthesis via Next-Token Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.14808): Introduces D-JEPA.T2I, a continuous-token autoregressive T2I model with denoising joint embedding prediction for photorealistic arbitrary-resolution generation up to 4K.
- [mR2AG: Multimodal Retrieval-Reflection-Augmented Generation for Knowledge-Based VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.15041): Adds retrieval and reflection to knowledge-based VQA so MLLMs retrieve only when needed and identify evidence supporting the visual query.
- [Reward Fine-Tuning Two-Step Diffusion Models via Learning Differentiable Latent-Space Surrogate Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.15247): Learns a differentiable latent-space surrogate reward so two-step diffusion generators can be reward fine-tuned without repeatedly backpropagating through a non-differentiable evaluator.
- [Foundation Cures Personalization: Improving Personalized Models'Prompt Consistency via Hidden Foundation Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.15277): Proposes FreeCure, a training-free facial-personalization repair that extracts foundation-model cross-attention and semantic maps, then blends noise through inversion to restore prompt attributes while preserving identity.
- [DiffServe: Efficiently Serving Text-to-Image Diffusion Models with Query-Aware Model Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.15381): Builds query-aware cascades of lightweight and full T2I diffusion variants, automatically routing easy prompts and reallocating resources to reduce latency violations while preserving response quality.
- [Large-Scale Text-to-Image Model with Inpainting is a Zero-Shot Subject-Driven Image Generator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.15466): Shows that an inpainting-capable large T2I model can perform subject-driven generation zero-shot by treating reference identity regions as inpainting conditions instead of training a personalization adapter.
- [TKG-DM: Training-free Chroma Key Content Generation Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.15580): Uses training-free chroma-key guidance so diffusion models generate foreground content against removable key-color backgrounds for easier compositing and editing.
- [Effort: Efficient Orthogonal Modeling for Generalizable AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.15633): Separates generator-specific and generator-agnostic evidence with orthogonal feature modeling, improving AI-generated image detection on unseen synthesis models.
- [AnyEdit: Mastering Unified High-Quality Image Editing for Any Idea](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.15738): Trains a unified instruction-based image editor over broad edit types and high-quality edit pairs so one model handles local, global, object, style, and appearance changes.
- [Unveil Inversion and Invariance in Flow Transformer for Versatile Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.15843): Analyzes inversion and invariance in flow-transformer generators and uses those properties to edit images while retaining content that should remain unchanged.
- [One Diffusion to Generate Them All](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.16318): Trains OneDiffusion by treating conditions and targets as frame sequences with varying noise scales, unifying text-to-image, editing, multiview generation, personalization, and inverse vision tasks in one diffusion model.
- [Chat2SVG: Vector Graphics Generation with Large Language Models and Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.16602): Generates vector graphics by combining large language models with image diffusion models.
- [Revelio: Interpreting and Leveraging Semantic Information in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.16725): Probes semantic directions inside diffusion representations and uses the discovered signals to steer or interpret generated content without retraining the base model.
- [Augmenting Multimodal LLMs with Self-Reflective Tokens for Knowledge-based Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.16863): Adds self-reflective tokens to multimodal LLM reasoning so the model can mark uncertain visual answers and revise knowledge-based VQA outputs.
- [DiffSLT: Enhancing Diversity in Sign Language Translation via Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.17248): Uses diffusion decoding to sample diverse sign-language translation outputs, making the sequence generation objective less deterministic than standard encoder-decoder translation.
- [DRiVE: Diffusion-based Rigging Empowers Generation of Versatile and Expressive Characters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.17423): Learns diffusion-based character rigging so generated characters receive controllable skeletal or deformation structures for expressive posing and animation.
- [On Statistical Rates of Conditional Diffusion Transformers: Approximation, Estimation and Minimax Optimality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.17522): Provides approximation, estimation, and minimax-rate analysis for conditional diffusion transformers, clarifying sample-complexity behavior behind conditional image generation.
- [Collaborative Decoding Makes Visual Auto-Regressive Modeling Efficient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.17787): Speeds visual autoregressive generation by letting draft and target decoders collaborate on image-token prediction instead of sampling every token with the largest model.
- [Generative Image Layer Decomposition with Visual Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.17864): Decomposes generated images into visual-effect layers for controllable editing.
- [TSD-SR: One-Step Diffusion with Target Score Distillation for Real-World Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.18263): Distills a one-step real-world super-resolution model from diffusion target scores, compressing iterative restoration into a single fast generation pass.
- [Diffusion Self-Distillation for Zero-Shot Customized Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.18616): Self-distills a pretrained diffusion model on reference-image signals so zero-shot subject customization can preserve identity without per-subject finetuning.
- [Random Walks With Tweedie: A Unified View of Score-Based Diffusion Models (In the Spotlight)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.18702): Recasts score-based diffusion through Tweedie random walks, linking denoising updates, posterior means, and sampler behavior in one theoretical view.
- [Self-Cross Diffusion Guidance for Text-to-Image Synthesis of Similar Subjects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.18936): Combines self-attention and cross-attention guidance to separate similar subjects in one prompt, reducing identity and attribute leakage during T2I synthesis.
- [Improving Multi-Subject Consistency in Open-Domain Image Generation with Isolation and Reposition Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.19261): Uses isolation and reposition attention to keep multiple open-domain subjects distinct and consistently placed across the generated image.
- [Any-Resolution AI-Generated Image Detection by Spectral Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.19417): Learns spectral cues that remain available across image resolutions, making generated-image detection less dependent on fixed input size or resampling artifacts.
- [Forensics Adapter: Unleashing CLIP for Generalizable Face Forgery Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.19715): Adds a lightweight forensics adapter to CLIP so face-forgery detection benefits from pretrained visual semantics while adapting to manipulation artifacts.
- [JetFormer: An Autoregressive Generative Model of Raw Images and Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.19722): Models raw image bytes or pixels and text with an autoregressive transformer, avoiding a separate visual tokenizer for joint image-text generation.
- [DiffGuard: Text-Based Safety Checker for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.00064): Uses a text-side safety checker for diffusion prompts, screening unsafe generation intent before image synthesis rather than relying only on output filtering.
- [Steering Rectified Flow Models in the Vector Field for Controlled Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.00100): Steers rectified-flow generation by modifying the learned vector field, giving inference-time control without retraining the image generator.
- [Orthus: Autoregressive Interleaved Image-Text Generation with Modality-Specific Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.00127): Uses modality-specific output heads in an autoregressive model so text and image tokens can be generated in interleaved multimodal sequences.
- [Opt-In Art: Learning Art Styles Only from Few Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.00176): Learns opt-in artistic styles from a small set of authorized examples, separating consented style adaptation from broad web-scale style imitation.
- [LumiNet: Latent Intrinsics Meets Diffusion Models for Indoor Scene Relighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.00177): Combines latent intrinsic decomposition with diffusion priors to relight indoor scenes while preserving geometry, materials, and scene identity.
- [FreeCond: Free Lunch in the Input Conditions of Text-Guided Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.00427): Improves text-guided inpainting by adjusting input condition signals at inference time, extracting more control from masks and prompts without extra training.
- [DyMO: Training-Free Diffusion Model Alignment with Dynamic Multi-Objective Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.00759): Dynamically schedules multiple guidance objectives at inference time, aligning diffusion outputs to quality and prompt targets without finetuning.
- [LoyalDiffusion: A Diffusion Model Guarding Against Data Replication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.01118): Adds a generation-time guard against training-data replication so diffusion sampling avoids overly faithful copies while retaining prompt fidelity.
- [Schedule On the Fly: Diffusion Time Prediction for Faster and Better Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.01243): Predicts diffusion timesteps adaptively during sampling, replacing a fixed schedule with image-dependent time choices for faster and higher-quality generation.
- [EmojiDiff: Advanced Facial Expression Control with High Identity Preservation in Portrait Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.01254): Conditions portrait diffusion on emoji-style expression controls, changing facial affect while protecting the subject identity.
- [MuLan: Adapting Multilingual Diffusion Models for Hundreds of Languages with Negligible Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.01271): Adapts T2I diffusion to hundreds of prompt languages with lightweight multilingual alignment rather than retraining the full generator.
- [Negative Token Merging: Image-based Adversarial Feature Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.01339): Uses negative token merging to inject image-based adversarial feature guidance, steering diffusion away from unwanted visual attributes.
- [FoundIR: Unleashing Million-Scale Training Data to Advance Foundation Models for Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.01427): Scales image-restoration foundation training with million-scale degraded-clean data, improving general restoration across denoising, deblurring, deraining, and super-resolution.
- [XQ-GAN: An Open-source Image Tokenization Framework for Autoregressive Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.01762): Provides an open image-tokenization framework for autoregressive generators, focusing on codebook quality and tokenizer training needed before next-token image synthesis.
- [SceneFactor: Factored Latent 3D Diffusion for Controllable 3D Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.01801): Factors 3D scene latents before diffusion, enabling controllable generation of scene structure and appearance rather than a monolithic scene code.
- [Switti: Designing Scale-Wise Transformers for Text-to-Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.01819): Designs scale-wise transformer blocks for T2I synthesis so coarse-to-fine image scales are modeled explicitly during generation.
- [RandAR: Decoder-only Autoregressive Visual Generation in Random Orders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.01827): Trains a decoder-only visual autoregressive model over randomized token orders, improving bidirectional context use without abandoning next-token prediction.
- [ScImage: How Good Are Multimodal Large Language Models at Scientific Text-to-Image Generation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.02368): Evaluates MLLM scientific text-to-image generation with ScImage, scoring scientific plausibility, spatial reasoning, and instruction fidelity rather than proposing a new generator.
- [Scaling Image Tokenizers with Grouped Spherical Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.02632): Scales visual tokenizers with grouped spherical quantization, improving codebook capacity and stability for autoregressive image generation.
- [MetaShadow: Object-Centered Shadow Detection, Removal, and Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.02635): Centers shadow modeling on objects, combining detection, removal, and synthesis so generated or edited scenes preserve physically plausible cast shadows.
- [Supercharged One-Step Text-to-Image Diffusion Models with Negative Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.02687): Strengthens one-step T2I diffusion by incorporating negative-prompt conditioning, improving fast generation while retaining user control over undesired content.
- [Pixel-level and Semantic-level Adjustable Super-resolution: A Dual-LoRA Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.03017): Uses two LoRA branches to separately adjust pixel fidelity and semantic realism, giving super-resolution users a controllable restoration trade-off.
- [TokenFlow: Unified Image Tokenizer for Multimodal Understanding and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.03069): Trains a unified image tokenizer whose discrete tokens support both multimodal understanding and image generation instead of separate task-specific tokenizers.
- [Towards Understanding and Quantifying Uncertainty for Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.03178): Studies uncertainty signals in T2I generation, quantifying when prompt-conditioned outputs are ambiguous, unstable, or poorly aligned.
- [DynamicControl: Adaptive Condition Selection for Improved Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.03255): Selects conditioning signals adaptively during T2I generation so controls are applied only when they improve prompt alignment or visual quality.
- [Black-Box Forgery Attacks on Semantic Watermarks for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.03283): Demonstrates black-box attacks that forge semantic diffusion watermarks, testing attribution schemes when the attacker cannot inspect model internals.
- [Implicit Priors Editing in Stable Diffusion via Targeted Token Adjustment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.03400): Edits Stable Diffusion's implicit visual priors by adjusting targeted tokens, changing biased or default attributes without full model finetuning.
- [Mv-Adapter: Multi-View Consistent Image Generation Made Easy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.03632): Adds a multi-view adapter to pretrained diffusion models so generated views share geometry and appearance without training a full 3D generator.
- [CreatiLayout: Siamese Multimodal Diffusion Transformer for Creative Layout-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.03859): Uses a Siamese multimodal diffusion transformer to turn creative layouts into images while preserving both spatial structure and text semantics.
- [Safeguarding Text-to-Image Generation via Inference-Time Prompt-Noise Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.03876): Optimizes prompt and noise variables at inference time to steer T2I generation away from unsafe content without changing model weights.
- [ZipAR: Parallel Autoregressive Image Generation through Spatial Locality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.04062): Exploits spatial locality so autoregressive image tokens can be generated in parallel blocks rather than strict raster order.
- [SwiftEdit: Lightning Fast Text-Guided Image Editing via One-Step Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.04301): Distills text-guided editing into a one-step diffusion model, reducing edit latency while preserving instruction adherence.
- [Infinity∞: Scaling Bitwise AutoRegressive Modeling for High-Resolution Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.04431): Represents images as bitwise token sequences and scales autoregressive modeling to high-resolution synthesis with flexible aspect ratios.
- [LayerFusion: Harmonized Multi-Layer Text-to-Image Generation with Generative Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.04460): Generates editable multi-layer images by harmonizing layer-wise T2I outputs with generative priors for composition and appearance consistency.
- [Not Just Text: Uncovering Vision Modality Typographic Threats in Image Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.05538): Studies typographic attacks embedded in the visual modality of generated images, showing that safety filters must handle text rendered inside images.
- [Language-Guided Image Tokenization for Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.05796): Guides image tokenizer learning with language supervision so discrete visual tokens preserve semantics useful for downstream generation.
- [SILMM: Self-Improving Large Multimodal Models for Compositional Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.05818): Uses a self-improving multimodal loop to critique and refine compositional T2I outputs, targeting object binding and relation errors.
- [Proactive Agents for Multi-Turn Text-to-Image Generation Under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.06771): Prototypes T2I agents that ask clarification questions under uncertainty and expose editable belief graphs, evaluated with a two-agent ground-truth-image protocol over ImageInWords, COCO, and DesignBench.
- [FIRE: Robust Detection of Diffusion-Generated Images via Frequency-Guided Reconstruction Error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.07140): Detects diffusion-generated images through frequency-guided reconstruction error, emphasizing spectral artifacts that survive generator and post-processing changes.
- [DFREC: DeepFake Identity Recovery Based on Identity-Aware Masked Autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.07260): Uses an identity-aware masked autoencoder to recover source identity cues from deepfakes, supporting forensic analysis beyond binary fake detection.
- [DiffSensei: Bridging Multi-Modal LLMs and Diffusion Models for Customized Manga Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.07589): Connects a multimodal LLM planner with diffusion generation to customize manga characters and panels from reference images and instructions.
- [TraSCE: Trajectory Steering for Concept Erasure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.07658): Erases unwanted concepts by steering diffusion sampling trajectories, avoiding full retraining while targeting concept-specific generation paths.
- [Learning Visual Generative Priors without Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.07767): Learns visual generative priors from image data without text supervision, probing how far diffusion-style generation can scale without captions.
- [UniReal: Universal Image Generation and Editing via Learning Real-world Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.07774): Learns real-world visual dynamics as a common prior for universal image generation and editing across object, scene, and transformation tasks.
- [Boosting Alignment for Post-Unlearning Text-to-Image Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.07808): Restores prompt alignment after concept unlearning, addressing the quality and instruction-following degradation introduced by erasure methods.
- [CC-Diff: Enhancing Contextual Coherence in Remote Sensing Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.08464): Adds contextual-coherence constraints to remote-sensing diffusion so synthesized aerial scenes maintain plausible object, land-cover, and neighborhood relationships.
- [Learning Flow Fields in Attention for Controllable Person Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.08486): Learns attention-level flow fields that control person pose or appearance transfer while preserving identity and garment structure.
- [StyleStudio: Text-Driven Style Transfer with Selective Control of Style Elements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.08503): Enables text-driven style transfer with selective controls, letting users change chosen style elements while leaving other visual attributes intact.
- [Flowedit: Inversion-Free Text-Based Editing Using Pre-Trained Flow Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.08629): Performs text-based image editing directly with pretrained flow models, avoiding explicit inversion while guiding source and target trajectories.
- [Inference-Time Diffusion Model Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.08871): Distills diffusion behavior during inference, using the current sampling trajectory to reduce steps without a separate offline student-training stage.
- [RAD: Region-Aware Diffusion Models for Image Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.09191): Makes inpainting region-aware so masked areas, boundaries, and surrounding context receive different diffusion treatment for coherent fills.
- [OFTSR: One-Step Flow for Image Super-Resolution with Tunable Fidelity-Realism Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.09465): Uses a one-step flow model for super-resolution and exposes a control knob between faithful reconstruction and perceptual realism.
- [EasyRef: Omni-Generalized Group Image Reference for Diffusion Models via Multimodal LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.09618): Uses a multimodal LLM to organize group image references into generalized conditioning signals for diffusion personalization and reference-guided generation.
- [SuperMark: Robust and Training-free Image Watermarking via Diffusion-based Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.10049): Embeds watermarks through diffusion-based super-resolution, giving a training-free mark that is tied to restoration rather than generator internals.
- [The Art of Deception: Color Visual Illusions and Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.10122): Uses diffusion models to generate or analyze color visual illusions, exposing how generative priors can create perceptual mismatches between local and global color appearance.
- [BrushEdit: All-In-One Image Inpainting and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.10316): Combines MLLMs with a dual-branch inpainting model so free-form edit instructions trigger edit-type classification, object identification, mask acquisition, and region inpainting, improving mask preservation and edit coherence.
- [SVGFusion: Scalable Text-to-SVG Generation via Vector Space Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.10437): Uses VP-VAE to jointly encode SVG code and rendered pixels, then applies a Vector Space Diffusion Transformer with rendering-sequence modeling on the 240K-item SVGX dataset for editable text-to-SVG generation.
- [SUGAR: Subject-Driven Video Customization in a Zero-Shot Manner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.10533): Builds a zero-shot subject-driven video generator with 2.5M synthetic image-video-text triplets, specialized attention, improved training, and refined sampling so one reference image can control identity while text controls style and motion.
- [EVLM: Self-Reflective Multimodal Reasoning for Cross-Dimensional Visual Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.10566): Trains an Editing Vision-Language Model on 30K chain-of-thought examples with human-rated rationales, using RKTO alignment to turn ambiguous image, video, 3D, and 4D edit requests into structured prompts.
- [Grid: Omni Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.10718): Reformulates temporal visual sequences as grid layouts and trains with parallel flow matching plus coarse-to-fine scheduling, reusing image-generation capacity for video, 3D editing, and image tasks at much lower compute cost.
- [Progressive Compression with Universally Quantized Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.10935): Replaces the diffusion forward process with uniform noise so the negative ELBO matches universal-quantization compression cost, yielding progressively decodable image bitstreams.
- [SHMT: Self-supervised Hierarchical Makeup Transfer via Latent Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.11058): Uses a self-supervised decoupling-and-reconstruction latent diffusion pipeline, Laplacian-pyramid texture decomposition, and Iterative Dual Alignment to transfer diverse makeup styles without paired ground truth.
- [Empowering LLMs to Understand and Generate Complex Vector Graphics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.11102): Studies how large language models can understand and generate complex vector graphics.
- [GenLit: Reformulating Single-Image Relighting as Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.11224): Distills point-light manipulation from a graphics engine into Stable Video Diffusion, treating relighting a single image as generating a short illumination-change sequence without explicit 3D reconstruction.
- [MPQ-DM: Mixed Precision Quantization for Extremely Low Bit Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.11549): Quantizes diffusion models to 2-4 bit settings with Outlier-Driven Mixed Quantization and Time-Smoothed Relation Distillation, cutting W2A4 FID substantially compared with low-bit baselines.
- [IDProtector: An Adversarial Noise Encoder to Protect Against ID-Preserving Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.11638): Applies imperceptible adversarial noise to portraits in one forward pass, protecting against encoder-based identity generators such as InstantID, IP-Adapter, and PhotoMaker under JPEG, resizing, and affine transforms.
- [Causal Diffusion Transformers for Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.12095): Introduces CausalFusion, a decoder-only transformer that factorizes data across sequential tokens and diffusion noise levels, bridging autoregressive and diffusion generation for ImageNet, captioning, and in-context image manipulation.
- [OmniPrism: Learning Disentangled Visual Concept for Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.12242): Learns natural-language-guided disentangled concept representations from the PCD-200K paired concept dataset, then injects them through extra diffusion cross-attention layers and block embeddings.
- [Efficient Scaling of Diffusion Transformers for Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.12391): Runs controlled DiT scaling ablations from 0.3B to 8B parameters on up to 600M images, finding pure self-attention U-ViT scales more simply than cross-attention variants and that long captions improve text-image alignment.
- [Attentive Eraser: Unleashing Diffusion Model's Object Removal Potential via Self-Attention Redirection Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.12974): Provides tuning-free object removal by activating and suppressing self-attention with a mask, then using Self-Attention Redirection Guidance to refill foreground regions coherently across diffusion checkpoints.
- [CoMPaSS: Enhancing Spatial Understanding in Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.13195): Combines a SCOP data engine for spatially accurate prompt-image pairs with TENOR token-order preservation, improving UNet and MMDiT T2I models on VISOR, T2I-CompBench Spatial, and GenEval Position.
- [T$^3$-S2S: Training-free Triplet Tuning for Sketch to Scene Synthesis in Controllable Concept Art Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.13486): Adds training-free Prompt Balance, Characteristic Priority, and Dense Tuning modules to ControlNet cross-attention so sketch-to-scene generation preserves multiple instances and terrain layout details.
- [Multimodal Latent Diffusion Model for Complex Sewing Pattern Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.14453): Proposes SewingLDM, which compresses detailed sewing-pattern vectors into a latent space and uses two-step multimodal diffusion conditioning on body shape, text, and garment sketch for body-suited pattern generation.
- [Parallelized Autoregressive Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.15119): Generates weakly dependent distant visual tokens in parallel while keeping strongly dependent local tokens sequential, giving standard autoregressive image and video models 3.6x speedup at comparable quality.
- [FlowAR: Scale-wise Autoregressive Image Generation Meets Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.15205): Simplifies scale-wise autoregressive generation to successive doubled scales, uses an off-the-shelf VAE, and integrates flow matching for stronger ImageNet-256 synthesis than prior VAR-style designs.
- [UIP2P: Unsupervised Instruction-Based Image Editing via Edit Reversibility Constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.15216): Removes the need for ground-truth edited triplets by applying forward and reverse edits in one training step and enforcing consistency in image, text, and attention spaces.
- [Next Patch Prediction for Autoregressive Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.15321): Groups image tokens into high-information patch tokens and trains coarse-to-fine next-patch prediction, reducing autoregressive visual-generation training cost to about 0.6x while improving ImageNet FID.
- [When Worse is Better: Navigating the compression-generation tradeoff in visual tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.16326): Studies the rate-distortion tradeoff for discrete autoregressive image tokenizers and introduces Causally Regularized Tokenization, making stage-two generation 2-3x more compute efficient despite more aggressive compression.
- [Data-Driven Fairness Generalization for Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.16428): Generates balanced synthetic demographic data and optimizes detectors on it so deepfake detection fairness generalizes better across unseen race and gender distributions.
- [RealisID: Scale-Robust and Fine-Controllable Identity Customization via Local and Global Complementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.16832): Splits identity customization into local and global ControlNet branches, using cropped upsampled faces for fine identity fidelity and a global branch for harmony, location, pose, expression, and multi-person control.
- [Human-Guided Image Generation for Expanding Small-Scale Training Image Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.16839): Couples multimodal projection with sample-level human feedback so users refine prompts and regenerate synthetic examples for small datasets, improving classification and object-detection case studies.
- [A Bias-Free Training Paradigm for More General AI-generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.17671): Constructs B-Free training data by generating fake images from matched real images through Stable Diffusion conditioning, isolating generator artifacts from content, format, and resolution biases across 27 generators.
- [Causal Composition Diffusion Model for Closed-loop Traffic Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.17920): Uses CCDiff to inject automatically identified causal structures into the diffusion process, balancing user controllability and realism for closed-loop long-tail traffic simulation.
- [Accelerating Diffusion Transformers with Dual Feature Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.18911): Introduces DuCa, alternating aggressive and conservative feature caching with random token selection after showing fixed "important token" caching is often unnecessary in DiT, PixArt, FLUX, and OpenSora.
- [UNIC-Adapter: Unified Image-Instruction Adapter with Multi-Modal Transformer for Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.18928): Builds a unified image-instruction adapter on MMDiT, extracting conditional images and task instructions and injecting them through RoPE-enhanced cross-attention for spatial, subject, and style control.
- [Motion Planning Diffusion: Learning and Adapting Robot Motion Planning With Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.19948): Learns diffusion priors over low-dimensional B-spline trajectory representations, then samples posterior paths guided by motion-planning costs for simulated and real robot tasks.
- [MaIR: A Locality-and Continuity-Preserving Mamba for Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.20066): Designs a Mamba restoration backbone with nested S-shaped scanning and cross-sequence aggregation so flattened image sequences preserve local continuity across rows and columns.
- [Distilled transformers with locally enhanced global representations for face forgery detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.20156): Trains a distilled transformer detector with mixture-of-expert forgery embeddings and local enhancement so global attention captures both broad dependencies and local manipulation traces.
- [An analytic theory of creativity in convolutional diffusion models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.20292): Explains diffusion "creativity" through locality and equivariance biases, deriving local-score machines that predict trained convolutional diffusion outputs with high reported median r-squared values.
- [EraseAnything: Enabling Concept Erasure in Rectified Flow Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.20413): Formulates concept erasure in flow-based T2I transformers as bi-level LoRA tuning with an attention-map regularizer and self-contrastive learning, targeting SD3 and Flux-style architectures.
- [Zero-Shot Image Restoration Using Few-Step Guidance of Consistency Models (and Beyond)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.20596): Uses few-step consistency-model guidance for zero-shot image restoration.
- [HFI: A unified framework for training-free detection and implicit watermarking of latent diffusion model generated images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.20704): Detects LDM-generated images without training data by measuring high-frequency aliasing from the LDM autoencoder reconstruction, and reuses the same signal as an implicit watermark for specified LDMs.
- [VisionReward: Fine-Grained Multi-Dimensional Human Preference Learning for Image and Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.21059): Learns interpretable image and video preferences with hierarchical visual assessment, linear weighting, and multi-dimensional consistent reward use during preference optimization, outperforming prior reward models in human and machine evaluations.
- [Fusion2Void](https://doi.org/10.1109/TCSVT.2024.3502200): uses image inpainting for unsupervised multi-focus image fusion.
- [Inversion-Free Image Editing with Language-Guided Diffusion Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/cvpr52733.2024.00903): Edits images with language-guided diffusion without requiring inversion, improving practical text-driven editing.
- [SemStamp: A Semantic Watermark with Paraphrastic Robustness for Text Generation](https://doi.org/10.18653/v1/2024.naacl-long.226): Watermarks generated text by hashing sentence embeddings with LSH and rejection sampling, making this an off-axis text-generation provenance row for the image model page.
- [Faster and More Efficient Subject Image Generation for Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/smc54092.2024.10831577): Trains the DDCA plug-and-play image-prompt adapter for subject-conditioned T2I generation, avoiding repeated per-subject training while improving text and image consistency.
- [FlowDiffuser: Advancing Optical Flow Estimation with Diffusion Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/cvpr52733.2024.01813): Uses a diffusion formulation for optical-flow estimation, treating flow fields as generated targets so uncertainty-aware denoising improves dense motion prediction.
- [WFormer: A Transformer-Based Soft Fusion Model for Robust Image Watermarking](https://doi.org/10.1109/tetci.2024.3386916): Uses a Transformer watermark preprocess module and soft fusion between cover-image and watermark features, replacing simple watermark duplication and direct concatenation in robust image watermarking.
- [Image Intrinsic Components Guided Conditional Diffusion Model for Low-Light Image Enhancement](https://doi.org/10.1109/tcsvt.2024.3441713): Guides conditional diffusion with Retinex intrinsic components, injecting multi-scale illumination and reflectance features instead of only concatenating degraded low-light inputs.
- [Countering Personalized Text-to-Image Generation with Influence Watermarks](https://doi.org/10.1109/cvpr52733.2024.01165): Embeds influence watermarks into protected personal images so later personalized T2I fine-tuning carries a traceable signal that can expose unauthorized identity customization.
- [TF²: Few-Shot Text-Free Training-Free Defect Image Generation for Industrial Anomaly Inspection](https://doi.org/10.1109/tcsvt.2024.3424435): Generates industrial defect images without text prompts or additional training, using a few normal or defective samples to guide synthetic anomaly creation for inspection data expansion.
- [Improving Generalization of Deepfake Detectors by Imposing Gradient Regularization](https://doi.org/10.1109/tifs.2024.3396064): Improves deepfake detector generalization by adding gradient regularization, discouraging reliance on source-specific forgery artifacts that fail under dataset shift.
- [Monte Carlo guided Denoising Diffusion models for Bayesian linear inverse problems](https://proceedings.iclr.cc/paper_files/paper/2024/hash/bfb3c1db0deda449ff4027f77dd48704-Abstract-Conference.html): Guides diffusion posterior sampling for Bayesian linear inverse problems with Monte Carlo corrections, targeting reconstruction uncertainty rather than ordinary T2I synthesis.
- [EasyDrag: Efficient Point-Based Manipulation on Diffusion Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/cvpr52733.2024.00803): Provides interactive point-based diffusion editing without DragDiffusion-style LoRA fine-tuning or masks, improving robustness for pose, shape, and scene manipulation.
- [ADA-FInfer: Inferring Face Representations From Adaptive Select Frames for High-Visual-Quality Deepfake Detection](https://doi.org/10.1109/tdsc.2024.3523289): Selects video frames adaptively with optical-flow cues and infers interpretable face representations to expose high-visual-quality deepfakes whose visible artifacts are weak.
- [LFDT-Fusion: A latent feature-guided diffusion Transformer model for general image fusion](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.inffus.2024.102639): Uses a latent feature-guided diffusion Transformer for general image fusion, according to Information Fusion publisher metadata; mechanism details need a full-text follow-up.
- [Latent Diffusion Enhanced Rectangle Transformer for Hyperspectral Image Restoration](https://doi.org/10.1109/tpami.2024.3475249): Combines horizontal and vertical rectangle self-attention for spatial non-local similarity with a spectral latent-diffusion enhancement module for low-rank hyperspectral restoration.
- [MMGInpainting: Multi-Modality Guided Image Inpainting Based on Diffusion Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/tmm.2024.3382484): Extends diffusion inpainting to jointly use image and text guidance so missing regions inherit both reference visual structure and semantic instructions.
- [LPSRGAN: Generative adversarial networks for super-resolution of license plate image](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.neucom.2024.127426): Applies a GAN super-resolution model to license plate images, using publisher metadata to identify it as a domain-specific restoration entry rather than a general T2I generator.
- [Integrating neural operators with diffusion models improves spectral representation in turbulence modelling](https://doi.org/10.1098/rspa.2024.0819): Conditions diffusion models on neural-operator predictions to recover high-frequency turbulent structures and improve energy-spectrum alignment over neural operators alone.
- [WaterFormer: A Global–Local Transformer for Underwater Image Enhancement With Environment Adaptor](https://doi.org/10.1109/mra.2024.3351487): Enhances underwater images with a global-local Transformer block, detail enhancement, and an environment adaptor that handles varied color degradation across underwater scenes.
- [RealtimeGen: An Intervenable AI Image Generation System for Commercial Digital Art Asset Creators](https://doi.org/10.1080/10447318.2024.2382508): Builds an intervenable image-generation tool for commercial art creators, emphasizing transparent creator control during generation rather than a new base image model.
- [UV-IDM: Identity-Conditioned Latent Diffusion Model for Face UV-Texture Generation](https://doi.org/10.1109/cvpr52733.2024.01007): Uses an identity-conditioned latent diffusion module to generate photorealistic face UV textures for BFM-based 3D face reconstruction while preserving identity from in-the-wild images.
- [Cambricon-D: Full-Network Differential Acceleration for Diffusion Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/isca59077.2024.00070): Accelerates diffusion inference through full-network differential computing, forwarding timestep deltas across nonlinear operations to reduce redundant recomputation.
- [StableSwap: Stable Face Swapping in a Shared and Controllable Latent Space](https://doi.org/10.1109/tmm.2024.3369853): Performs face swapping with a reversible autoencoder and latent alignment in a shared controllable latent space, reducing instability from hand-balanced loss terms.
- [DCGF: Diffusion-Color-Guided Framework for Underwater Image Enhancement](https://doi.org/10.1109/tgrs.2024.3522685): Guides underwater diffusion restoration with a conditional image and a color-correction module so denoising follows the target domain while limiting color drift.
- [CrossDiff: Exploring Self-SupervisedRepresentation of Pansharpening via Cross-Predictive Diffusion Model](https://doi.org/10.1109/tip.2024.3461476): Pretrains a conditional DDPM on a cross-predictive pansharpening task, then adapts it to fuse PAN spatial detail with multispectral information under scale mismatch.
- [An Efficient Attribute-Preserving Framework for Face Swapping](https://doi.org/10.1109/tmm.2024.3354573): Introduces AP-Swap with a global residual attribute-preserving encoder so swapped faces retain target pose, expression, and gaze while transferring source identity.
- [Common Canvas: Open Diffusion Models Trained on Creative-Commons Images](https://doi.org/10.1109/cvpr52733.2024.00788): Trains open diffusion models only on Creative Commons imagery, giving image generation a rights-aware data curation and model-release reference.
- [A multimodal data generation method for imbalanced classification with dual-discriminator constrained diffusion model and adaptive sample selection strategy](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.inffus.2024.102843): Uses a dual-discriminator constrained diffusion model plus adaptive sample selection for multimodal synthetic data in imbalanced classification, based on Information Fusion metadata.
- [AnimeDiff: Customized Image Generation of Anime Characters Using Diffusion Model](https://doi.org/10.1109/tmm.2024.3415357): Customizes diffusion generation for anime characters by binding characters to their names while keeping text-token embeddings fixed, then addresses multi-character attribute confusion.
- [SF-GAN: Semantic fusion generative adversarial networks for text-to-image synthesis](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.eswa.2024.125583): Fuses semantic cues inside a GAN for text-to-image synthesis so generated regions better match fine-grained textual descriptions.
- [Exploring Collaborative Diffusion Model Inferring for AIGC-Enabled Edge Services](https://doi.org/10.1109/tccn.2024.3519320): Studies collaborative diffusion inference for AIGC edge services, treating model partitioning, caching, and device cooperation as scheduling decisions to reduce latency and resource cost.
- [Dual Diffusion for Unified Image Generation and Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.00289): Trains an end-to-end diffusion model for both multimodal understanding and image generation, combining MM-DiT-style cross-modal modeling with discrete diffusion language modeling under cross-modal maximum likelihood.
- [Token Pruning for Caching Better: 9 Times Acceleration on Stable Diffusion for Free](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.00375): Introduces DaTo, a dynamics-aware token-pruning method that keeps feature caching useful by pruning low-dynamic tokens instead of blindly reusing stale timestep features.
- [SAT-LDM: Provably Generalizable Image Watermarking for Latent Diffusion Models with Self-Augmented Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.00463): Aligns latent-diffusion watermark training and testing with a free-generation distribution, yielding a provable generalization bound without collecting extra data.
- [RORem: Training a Robust Object Remover with Human-in-the-Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.00740): Trains robust object removal with human-in-the-loop data, fitting the image-editing Model section.
- [Text2Earth: Unlocking text-driven remote sensing image generation with a global-scale dataset and a foundation model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.00895): Builds Git-10M and a Text2Earth foundation model for global-scale remote-sensing T2I, targeting multi-resolution, controllable, unbounded Earth imagery.
- [Hierarchical Vision-Language Alignment for Text-to-Image Generation via Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.00917): Introduces VLAD, using a Contextual Composition Module to split prompts into global and local representations and a hierarchical multi-stage diffusion process to align complex descriptions with visual content.
- [EliGen: Entity-Level Controlled Image Generation with Regional Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.01097): Controls entities in text-to-image generation with regional attention, binding each object-level prompt to a target image region for stronger multi-entity layout and attribute control.
- [DuMo: Dual Encoder Modulation Network for Precise Concept Erasure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.01125): Uses a dual-encoder modulation network for precise concept erasure, adding a controllability and safety method for generative image models.
- [Semantics-Guided Diffusion for Deep Joint Source-Channel Coding in Wireless Image Transmission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.01138): Adds semantics-guided diffusion to DeepJSCC so wireless image transmission can reconstruct perceptually aligned, high-fidelity outputs under learned channel coding.
- [Reconstruction vs. Generation: Taming Optimization Dilemma in Latent Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.01423): Studies latent diffusion optimization tradeoffs, relevant to image-generation model design.
- [Object-level Visual Prompts for Compositional Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.01424): Uses object-level visual prompts to improve compositional control in image generation.
- [ArtCrafter: Text-Image Aligning Style Transfer via Embedding Reframing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.02064): Performs text-to-image style transfer with attention-based style extraction and embedding reframing, balancing text semantics, style capture, and output diversity.
- [CorrFill](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.02355): Uses correspondence guidance to improve faithfulness in reference-based diffusion inpainting, adding a model-side control method for image editing.
- [ACE++](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.02487): Extends ACE with an improved long-context conditioning unit and a two-stage FLUX-based training scheme, supporting instruction-based generation and editing across zero-reference and context-aware content-filling tasks.
- [SceneVTG++](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.02962): Targets controllable multilingual visual text generation in natural scenes.
- [CAT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.03120): Adapts image-token capacity to content complexity, making autoregressive image generation more token efficient.
- [Materialist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.03717): Combines neural initialization with physically based inverse rendering for controllable, physically consistent single-image editing.
- [Exploring Unbiased Deepfake Detection via Token-Level Shuffling and Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.04376): Reduces deepfake detector bias with token-level shuffling and mixing, targeting position and content shortcuts that hurt cross-forgery generalization.
- [EditAR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.04699): unifies conditional image generation and editing tasks with an autoregressive model.
- [EmotiCrafter: Text-to-Emotional-Image Generation Based on Valence-Arousal Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.05710): Introduces continuous emotional image-content generation, conditioning T2I synthesis on valence-arousal coordinates rather than only discrete emotion labels.
- [Test-time Alignment of Diffusion Models without Reward Over-optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.05803): Aligns diffusion models at test time while avoiding reward over-optimization.
- [Personalized Preference Fine-tuning of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.06655): Fine-tunes diffusion models on personalized preference signals so a general generator can adapt to user-specific style, composition, and quality preferences.
- [Padding Tone: A Mechanistic Analysis of Padding Tokens in T2I Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.06751): Mechanistically analyzes how padding tokens affect attention and outputs in T2I models, explaining why blank padding can shift tone, composition, or semantics.
- [A General Framework for Inference-time Scaling and Steering of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.06848): Provides inference-time scaling and reward steering for diffusion models using interacting particles and intermediate potentials.
- [Multilingual Prompting for Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.07086): Uses multilingual prompting through large multimodal models to improve text-to-image generation, adding a prompt-side route for cross-lingual visual generation control.
- [OmniEraser: Remove Objects and Their Effects in Images with Paired Video-Frame Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.07397): Trains object removal from paired video-frame data so the model removes both target objects and their shadows, reflections, or other physical effects.
- [Democratizing Text-to-Image Masked Generative Models with Compact Text-Aware One-Dimensional Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.07730): Uses compact text-aware one-dimensional tokens for masked generative T2I models, reducing training and inference cost while preserving text-conditioned control.
- [SmartEraser: Remove Anything from Images using Masked-Region Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.08279): Guides denoising inside masked regions so arbitrary object removal produces background content consistent with surrounding semantics and texture.
- [Few-Shot Learner Generalizes Across AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.08763): Trains a few-shot learner for AI-generated image detection that transfers across generators rather than overfitting to one synthetic source.
- [Grounding Text-To-Image Diffusion Models For Controlled High-Quality Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.09194): Adds grounding for text-to-image diffusion models to improve controllable generation.
- [ViTok Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.09755): Studies how scaling visual tokenizers affects reconstruction and generation, clarifying tokenizer capacity for image and video generators.
- [One-D-Piece](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.10064): Connects image tokenization with quality-controllable compression for visual generation pipelines.
- [Ditto: Accelerating Diffusion Model via Temporal Value Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.11211): Exploits high value similarity between adjacent diffusion timesteps, processing only temporal differences under quantization and adding a hardware execution flow for faster diffusion inference.
- [StyleSSP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.11319): Improves training-free diffusion style transfer through sampling-start enhancement, adding an image-editing control method.
- [SILO: Solving Inverse Problems with Latent Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.11746): Learns latent operators for diffusion-based inverse problems, reducing repeated autoencoder calls while improving image restoration quality.
- [GPS as a Control Signal for Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.12390): Conditions diffusion image generation on GPS metadata and text, learning city-scale visual variation so generated scenes reflect neighborhoods, landmarks, and local appearance.
- [PreciseCam: Precise Camera Control for Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.12910): Adds explicit intrinsic and extrinsic camera controls to text-to-image generation.
- [One-Prompt-One-Story: Free-Lunch Consistent Text-to-Image Generation Using a Single Prompt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.13554): Maintains story identity from a single prompt by exploiting shared attention and consistency cues in T2I generation, avoiding model architecture changes or large story-specific training sets.
