- [Controlling ensemble variance in diffusion models: an application for reanalyses downscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.14822): Controls meteorological ensemble variance by varying DDIM reverse steps in a reanalysis-downscaling diffusion model, making this a climate-data generation boundary item.
- [Visual Generation Without Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.15420): Trains visual generators to match classifier-free guidance quality while sampling with a single conditional model, reducing generation cost.
- [StochSync: Stochastic Diffusion Synchronization for Image Generation in Arbitrary Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.15445): Generates images on spheres, mesh surfaces, and other arbitrary spaces by synchronizing pretrained diffusion processes stochastically in the target space.
- [Universal Image Restoration Pre-training via Degradation Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.15510): Image-restoration pre-training method based on degradation classification.
- [CubeDiff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.17162): repurposes multi-view diffusion image models to generate 360-degree panoramas as cubemap faces.
- [Janus-Pro](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.17811): A unified multimodal understanding and text-to-image generation model improved through optimized training, larger data, and scaling.
- [SAeUron: Interpretable Concept Unlearning in Diffusion Models with Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.18052): Uses sparse autoencoders to identify interpretable concept directions inside diffusion models and erase target concepts while preserving unrelated generation ability.
- [SANA 1.5: Efficient Scaling of Training-Time and Inference-Time Compute in Linear Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.18427): Linear diffusion-transformer scaling method for visual generation training and inference.
- [Fantastic Targets for Concept Erasure in Diffusion Models and Where To Find Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.18950): Studies how to choose effective targets for erasing concepts in diffusion models, strengthening the model-side safety-control line for visual generators.
- [Text-to-CAD Generation Through Infusing Visual Feedback in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.19054): Injects visual feedback into an LLM-driven text-to-CAD pipeline so rendered or inspected CAD outputs can iteratively correct structure from text.
- [Accelerating Diffusion Transformer via Error-Optimized Cache](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.19243): Accelerates diffusion transformers with an error-optimized cache, reusing stable intermediate features and refreshing them when estimated error grows.
- [A Diffusion Model Translator for Efficient Image-to-Image Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.00307): Adds a lightweight translator to a diffusion model so image-to-image translation transfers source distributions efficiently instead of injecting source information at every denoising step.
- [Representation-Aligned Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.00359): Aligns latent spaces with richer representations to improve diffusion-style generation quality.
- [Mitigating the Modality Gap: Few-Shot Out-of-Distribution Detection with Multi-modal Prototypes and Image Bias Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.00662): Combines image and text prototypes with image-bias estimation to reduce false positives in VLM few-shot OOD detection caused by image-text modality gaps.
- [RealRAG: Retrieval-augmented Realistic Image Generation via Self-reflective Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.00848): Adds retrieval augmentation and self-reflective contrastive learning to realistic image generation so retrieved evidence improves visual realism instead of adding noise.
- [Diffusion Model as a Noise-Aware Latent Reward Model for Step-Level Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.01051): Uses a diffusion model as a noise-aware latent reward model, supplying step-level preference signals during denoising optimization.
- [LayerTracer: Cognitive-Aligned Layered SVG Synthesis via Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.01105): Synthesizes layered SVGs with a diffusion transformer aligned to human layer-wise drawing cognition, yielding editable vector object structure.
- [MakeAnything: Harnessing Diffusion Transformers for Multi-Domain Procedural Sequence Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.01572): Uses diffusion transformers for multi-domain procedural sequence generation, extending DiT-style generation beyond ordinary image pixels.
- [Score as Action: Fine-Tuning Diffusion Generative Models by Continuous-time Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.01819): Treats diffusion scores as continuous-time actions and fine-tunes generation trajectories with reinforcement learning toward preference or task rewards.
- [Exploring the latent space of diffusion models directly through singular value decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.02225): Model candidate for image generation and editing: Analyzes diffusion-model latent space through singular value decomposition.
- [Variational Control for Guidance in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.03686): Derives Diffusion Trajectory Matching from variational inference and control, guiding pretrained diffusion trajectories toward terminal costs for linear, nonlinear, and blind inverse problems without extra training.
- [Mechanisms of Projective Composition of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.04549): Defines projective composition for score-combined diffusion models and analyzes when such compositions can extrapolate or length-generalize outside the training distribution.
- [QLIP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.05178): Aligns visual tokenization with text so autoregressive multimodal models can support both understanding and generation.
- [Physics-Conditioned Diffusion Models for Lattice Gauge Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.05504): Incorporates stochastic quantization as a physical condition for diffusion sampling in lattice gauge theory, with exactness enforced by the sampling procedure.
- [Training-Free Constrained Generation With Stable Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.05625): Couples stable diffusion with constrained optimization so generated outputs satisfy physical, functional, or copyright-style constraints without retraining.
- [AutoSketch: VLM-assisted Style-Aware Vector Sketch Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.06860): Completes partial vector sketches with AutoSketch, using VLM-derived style descriptions and sketch-aware generation so new strokes follow the original drawing style.
- [SketchFlex: Facilitating Spatial-Semantic Coherence in Text-to-Image Generation with Region-Based Sketches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.07556): Lets non-expert users guide T2I layout with rough region sketches, improving spatial-semantic coherence when prompts alone underspecify multi-object scenes.
- [Satellite Observations Guided Diffusion Model for Accurate Meteorological States at Arbitrary Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.07814): Uses satellite observations to guide diffusion-based downscaling of meteorological states at arbitrary locations and resolutions, making it a scientific-generation boundary entry.
- [DiffRatio: Training One-Step Diffusion Models Without Teacher Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.08005): Trains one-step diffusion models without a pretrained teacher score model, reducing teacher-supervision bias and student score-estimation bias in distillation.
- [Training-Free Safe Denoisers for Safe Use of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.08011): Provides safe denoisers for diffusion models, adding a safety method for generated-image systems.
- [SwiftSketch: A Diffusion Model for Image-to-Vector Sketch Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.08642): Uses a diffusion Transformer over vector stroke control points to generate image-conditioned sketches quickly, adding a vector-graphics generation route.
- [Redistribute Ensemble Training for Mitigating Memorization in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.09434): Mitigates visual-data memorization by redistributing training exposure across an ensemble, targeting diffusion privacy risk beyond text-conditioned prompt leakage.
- [EQ-VAE: Equivariance Regularized Latent Space for Improved Generative Image Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.09509): Regularizes latent spaces with equivariance for generative image modeling.
- [I Think, Therefore I Diffuse: Enabling Multimodal In-Context Reasoning in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.10458): Aligns diffusion models with multimodal in-context reasoning through ThinkDiff, using VLM-generated reasoning data rather than only pixel reconstruction supervision.
- [SWA-LDM: Toward Stealthy Watermarks for Latent Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.10495): Embeds stealthy watermarks directly in latent noise for LDMs, aiming to preserve the generator while enabling copyright tracing and misuse attribution.
- [Adaptive-Scaling Classifier-Free Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.10574): adapts classifier-free guidance scaling, improving controllability and quality trade-offs in diffusion generation.
- [Script&Shift: A Layered Interface Paradigm for Integrating Content Development and Rhetorical Strategy with LLM Writing Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.10638): Proposes a layered writing-assistant interface for planning, content development, and rhetorical strategy, making it a migration candidate outside image-generation models.
- [Beyond Known Fakes: Generalized Detection of AI-Generated Images via Post-hoc Distribution Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.10803): Introduces PDA, a post-hoc distribution-alignment framework that detects AI-generated images from unknown generators without retraining on each new fake source.
- [ControlText](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.10999): Enables controllable multilingual font rendering without font annotations.
- [Characterizing Photorealism and Artifacts in Diffusion Model-Generated Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.11989): Measures human detection of 450 diffusion images versus 149 real images, linking photorealism judgments to scene complexity, artifact type, display time, and curation.
- [How compositional generalization and creativity improve as diffusion models are trained](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.12089): Analyzes how compositional generalization and creativity change during diffusion-model training, informing model-side control of image generation behavior.
- [Diffusion Models without Classifier-free Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.12154): Studies diffusion generation without classifier-free guidance, clarifying an alternative model-side route for conditional synthesis.
- [RAPID: Retrieval Augmented Training of Differentially Private Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.12794): Combines retrieval with differentially private diffusion training so image generators can reuse nearby public examples while limiting memorization of sensitive training samples.
- [Secure and Efficient Watermarking for Latent Diffusion Models in Model Distribution Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.13345): Secures distributed latent diffusion models with a watermarking scheme designed for many model users, emphasizing robustness, efficiency, and model-ownership verification.
- [A Baseline Method for Removing Invisible Image Watermarks using Deep Image Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.13998): Removes invisible image watermarks in a black-box setting by fitting a single watermarked image with Deep Image Prior and selecting intermediate evasion outputs.
- [Vulnerability of Text-to-Image Models to Prompt Template Stealing: A Differential Evolution Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.14285): Introduces Prism and EvoStealer, using differential evolution to recover commercial prompt templates from a small set of sample images.
- [RelaCtrl](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.14377): Adds relevance-guided control to diffusion transformers for more efficient controlled generation.
- [PhotoDoodle: Learning Artistic Image Editing from Few-Shot Pairwise Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.14397): Learns artistic image editing from few-shot pairwise data.
- [CopyJudge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.15278): Uses an automated judging pipeline and mitigation strategy to identify and reduce copyright-infringing behavior in text-to-image diffusion models.
- [Concept Corrector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.16368): Removes unwanted concepts on the fly in text-to-image diffusion outputs.
- [KV-Edit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.17363): Preserves DiT background tokens in the KV cache rather than regenerating them, enabling training-free regional editing with stronger background consistency and an inversion-free O(1) cache optimization.
- [Rethinking the Vulnerability of Concept Erasure and a New Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.17537): Shows that erased text-to-image concepts remain vulnerable in prompt-embedding space and introduces coordinate-descent restoration attacks to evaluate concept-erasure robustness.
- [Training Consistency Models with Variational Noise Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.18197): Proposes VCT, learning data-dependent noise-data couplings to reduce variance and instability in non-distillation consistency-model training.
- [K-LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.18461): Fuses subject and style LoRAs without training to support personalized image generation.
- [BEVDiffuser: Plug-and-Play Diffusion Model for BEV Denoising with Ground-Truth Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.19694): Denoises BEV feature maps during training with ground-truth object-layout guidance, improving nuScenes 3D detection mAP and NDS without changing the downstream BEV architecture.
- [One Model for ALL: Low-Level Task Interaction Is a Key to Task-Agnostic Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.19854): Adds a task-agnostic low-level image-fusion model.
- [Multimodal Representation Alignment for Image Generation: Text-Image Interleaved Control is Easier than You Think](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.20172): Aligns multimodal representations for image generation through text-image interleaved control, improving controllability of generated outputs.
- [Attention Distillation: A Unified Approach to Visual Characteristics Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.20235): Transfers reference visual characteristics by optimizing latent samples with a diffusion self-attention distillation loss and injecting that loss into classifier guidance during denoising.
- [FlexVAR: Flexible Visual Autoregressive Modeling without Residual Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.20313): Replaces residual prediction in visual autoregression with ground-truth image prediction, enabling flexible resolutions, aspect ratios, image refinement, inpainting, and variable-step inference.
- [Beyond Next-Token: Next-X Prediction for Autoregressive Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.20388): Generalizes visual autoregression from next-token prediction to xAR entities such as cells, scales, subsamples, or whole images, reducing exposure-bias accumulation in 2D generation.
- [Does Generation Require Memorization?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.21278): studies creative diffusion models using ambient diffusion to separate generation from memorization.
- [AesthetiQ: Enhancing Graphic Layout Design via Aesthetic-Aware Preference Alignment of Multi-modal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.00591): Aligns multimodal layout-generation models with aesthetic preferences for graphic design.
- [Expandora: Broadening Design Exploration with Text-to-Image Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.00791): Supports design exploration by letting users state exploratory intent and diversity levels, then using LLM-assisted prompt expansion with T2I generation for broader reference search.
- [ACCORD: Alleviating Concept Coupling through Dependence Regularization for Text-to-Image Diffusion Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.01122): Uses ACCORD dependence regularization to reduce concept coupling in personalized T2I, improving separation between the target subject and incidental reference-image details.
- [Fine-Grained Controllable Apparel Showcase Image Generation via Garment-Centric Outpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.01294): Uses garment-centric outpainting in latent diffusion, starting from a segmented garment image and adding pose, face, and text controls for apparel showcase generation.
- [MINT: Multi-modal Chain of Thought in Unified Generative Models for Enhanced Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.01298): Adds multimodal chain-of-thought reasoning to unified generative models so visual content is planned before image synthesis.
- [DLF Extreme Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.01428): uses dual-generative latent fusion for extreme image compression.
- [DesignDiffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.01645): Generates high-quality design images from text with diffusion models, extending text-to-image coverage into structured design generation.
- [ToLo: A Two-Stage, Training-Free Layout-To-Image Generation Framework For High-Overlap Layouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.01667): Adds a two-stage training-free layout-to-image generation framework.
- [Dynamic Search for Inference-Time Alignment in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.02039): Inference-time search method for alignment in diffusion models.
- [HanDrawer: Leveraging Spatial Information to Render Realistic Hands Using a Conditional Diffusion Model in Single Stage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.02127): Extracts MANO hand-mesh spatial constraints with graph convolutions, fuses them by cross-attention and position-preserving zero padding, and trains a single-stage conditional diffusion model with an extra hand reconstruction loss.
- [h-Edit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.02187): Applies Doob's h-transform to make diffusion-based image editing more flexible and controllable.
- [Q&C: When Quantization Meets Cache in Efficient Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.02508): Studies the interaction between DiT post-training quantization and cache reuse, identifying calibration-sample loss and exposure bias before proposing a joint quantization-cache acceleration recipe.
- [Towards Understanding Text Hallucination of Diffusion Models via Local Generation Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.03595): Analyzes text hallucination in diffusion models through local generation bias.
- [Energy-Guided Optimization for Personalized Image Editing with Pretrained Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.04215): Performs personalized image editing with energy-guided optimization, using pretrained T2I diffusion while preserving object identity and integrating arbitrary objects into complex scenes.
- [Frequency Autoregressive Image Generation with Continuous Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.05305): Performs autoregressive image generation with continuous tokens in the frequency domain, separating coarse structure from fine detail prediction.
- [Fine-Grained Alignment and Noise Refinement for Compositional Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.06506): Targets compositional T2I failures with fine-grained text-image alignment and noise refinement, separating object-level semantic matching from denoising correction.
- [TR-DQ: Time-Rotation Diffusion Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.06564): Introduces time-rotation diffusion quantization to reduce timestep-dependent quantization error for faster diffusion deployment.
- [Adding Additional Control to One-Step Diffusion with Joint Distribution Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.06652): Uses Joint Distribution Matching to adapt one-step diffusion to new controls by optimizing an image-condition joint distribution, decoupling condition learning from fidelity preservation and avoiding full redistillation.
- [Towards Generalization of Tactile Image Generation: Reference-Free Evaluation in a Leakage-Free Setting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.06860): Shows that train-test leakage inflates tactile image generation metrics and proposes reference-free evaluation for synthetic tactile images under a leakage-free split.
- [From Reusing to Forecasting: Accelerating Diffusion Models With Taylorseers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.06923): Accelerates diffusion transformers by forecasting future features from earlier timesteps for image and video synthesis.
- [TIDE : Temporal-Aware Sparse Autoencoders for Interpretable Diffusion Transformers in Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.07050): Uses temporal-aware sparse autoencoders to interpret DiT representations across denoising timesteps and track evolving generation concepts.
- [NFIG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.07076): orders frequencies for multi-scale autoregressive image generation.
- [TRCE: Towards Reliable Malicious Concept Erasure in Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.07389): Targets reliable malicious concept erasure in text-to-image diffusion models.
- [SPEED: Scalable, Precise, and Efficient Concept Erasure for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.07392): Erases concepts from diffusion models with a scalable and precise method aimed at lowering side effects and compute cost for many removals.
- [Aligning Text to Image in Diffusion Models is Easier Than You Think](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.08250): Improves text-image alignment in diffusion models with a direct alignment adjustment rather than full model retraining.
- [Adv-CPG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.08269): Adds adversarial identity protection to customized portrait generation, connecting portrait synthesis with facial-privacy control.
- [OminiControl2: Efficient Conditioning for Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.08280): Provides efficient conditioning for diffusion transformers, injecting control signals into DiT generation with low additional overhead.
- [D3PO: Preference-Based Alignment of Discrete Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.08295): Adapts direct preference optimization to discrete diffusion models, adding preference-alignment machinery for discrete visual generation.
- [Robust Latent Matters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.08354): Improves image-generation tokenizers by synthesizing sampling errors and evaluating whether latent robustness predicts downstream generation quality.
- [DyArtbank: Diverse artistic style transfer via pre-trained stable diffusion and dynamic style prompt Artbank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.08392): Learns a Dynamic Style Prompt ArtBank to store artwork-style information and guide pretrained Stable Diffusion toward diverse, realistic stylized outputs.
- [OmniPaint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.08677): Couples object removal and insertion through progressive paired and unpaired diffusion training for object-oriented image editing.
- [NAMI: Efficient Image Generation via Bridged Progressive Rectified Flow Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.09242): Introduces bridged progressive rectified-flow transformers for efficient image generation.
- [Unicombine: Unified Multi-Conditional Combination with Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.09277): Uses diffusion transformers for unified multi-conditional combination in image generation.
- [Silent Branding Attack: Trigger-free Data Poisoning Attack on Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.09669): Shows a trigger-free data-poisoning attack that silently biases T2I diffusion models toward attacker-chosen branding under normal prompts.
- [Exploring Position Encoding in Diffusion U-Net for Training-free High-resolution Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.09830): Diagnoses high-resolution artifacts as position-encoding inconsistency in diffusion U-Nets and adjusts position propagation for training-free high-resolution generation.
- [Modeling Thousands of Human Annotators for Generalizable Text-to-Image Person Re-identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.09962): Uses Human Annotator Modeling to make MLLM-generated pedestrian captions mimic thousands of annotation styles for more generalizable text-to-image ReID.
- [PlanGen: Towards Unified Layout Planning and Image Generation in Auto-Regressive Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.10127): Unifies layout planning and image generation in autoregressive VLMs, predicting spatial plans before synthesizing visual content.
- [Randomized Parallel Decoding for Autoregressive Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.10568): accelerates autoregressive image generation with randomized parallel decoding.
- [Distilling Diversity and Control in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.10637): Finds distilled diffusion models retain diversity directions but commit too early, then adds diversity distillation to restore multi-sample variation without losing control transfer.
- [Neighboring Autoregressive Modeling for Efficient Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.10696): Decodes visual tokens as progressive near-to-far outpainting from an initial token, using next-neighbor prediction and parallel adjacent-token generation to exploit spatial locality.
- [FlowTok: Flowing Seamlessly Across Text and Image Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.10772): Builds a shared flow across text and image tokens so one model can switch smoothly between language and visual generation states.
- [Flow to the Mode: Mode-Seeking Diffusion Autoencoders for State-of-the-Art Image Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.11056): Learns mode-seeking diffusion autoencoder tokens that better preserve high-probability visual structure for downstream image generation.
- [Safe-VAR: Safe Visual Autoregressive Model for Text-to-Image Generative Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.11324): Adapts invisible watermarking to visual autoregressive T2I models, handling sequential image tokens where diffusion-oriented watermark methods do not directly apply.
- [MoDM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.11972): Serves image-generation workloads with mixture-of-diffusion routing, making diffusion efficiency a model-side image-generation issue rather than a generic systems metric.
- [SEAL: Semantic Aware Image Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.12172): Embeds semantic information about the generated image into the watermark itself, enabling distortion-free verification without long key-dictionary searches.
- [Reflect-DiT: Inference-Time Scaling for Text-to-Image Diffusion Transformers via In-Context Reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.12271): Scales T2I diffusion transformers at inference time through in-context reflection, letting the model self-evaluate and revise sampling trajectories.
- [Localized Concept Erasure for Text-to-Image Diffusion Models Using Training-Free Gated Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.12356): Applies training-free gated LoRA locally for concept erasure, restricting low-rank modulation to target regions to reduce global damage.
- [Personalize Anything](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.12590): A training-free personalization method for diffusion transformers using timestep-adaptive token replacement and patch perturbation.
- [LATINO-PRO: Latent Consistency Inverse Solver with Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.12615): Embeds Latent Consistency Models into a zero-shot plug-and-play inverse solver, then self-calibrates the text prompt from measurements with empirical Bayes marginal likelihood.
- [UniVG: A Generalist Diffusion Model for Unified Image Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.12652): Builds a generalist diffusion model that handles unified image generation, local editing, and instruction-guided editing through one interface.
- [DreamLayer: Simultaneous Multi-Layer Generation via Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.12838): Generates transparent foreground and background layers together using context-aware cross-attention, layer-shared self-attention, latent harmonization, and a 400K multi-layer dataset.
- [Reward-Instruct: A Reward-Centric Approach to Fast Photo-Realistic Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.13070): Converts pretrained diffusion models into few-step generators by directly sampling reward-tilted parameter updates, avoiding diffusion distillation losses while targeting controllability and preference alignment.
- [Edit Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.13327): Learns image editing through vision in-context relations, supporting transfer of edit behavior.
- [TarPro: Targeted Protection against Malicious Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.13994): Protects images from malicious NSFW edits with semantic-aware constraints and an imperceptible perturbation generator while preserving benign editability.
- [CRCE: Coreference-Retention Concept Erasure in Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.14232): Uses LLM-derived coreferential and retained concepts to erase unwanted concepts from text-to-image diffusion models while preserving distinct nearby concepts.
- [DiffMoE: Dynamic Token Selection for Scalable Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.14487): Scales diffusion transformers with dynamic token selection, spending computation on important tokens or paths to improve the quality-efficiency tradeoff.
- [Unlocking the Capabilities of Large Vision-Language Models for Generalizable and Explainable Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.14853): Uses large vision-language models for generalizable and explainable deepfake detection, pairing binary decisions with visual evidence reasoning.
- [CAM-Seg: A Continuous-valued Embedding Approach for Semantic Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.15617): Reformulates semantic-mask generation as continuous image-to-embedding diffusion with a VAE encoder, diffusion-guided autoregressive Transformer, and VAE decoder.
- [FreeFlux: Understanding and Exploiting Layer-Specific Roles in RoPE-Based MMDiT for Versatile Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16153): Exploits layer-specific roles in RoPE-based MMDiT to perform versatile image editing at inference time without training a new editor.
- [Guardians of Generation: Dynamic Inference-Time Copyright Shielding with Adaptive Guidance for AI Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16171): Adds inference-time copyright shielding through prompt and intermediate-step detection, prompt rewriting, and adaptive guidance without retraining diffusion weights.
- [Coarse-to-Fine AR Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16194): improves autoregressive image generation through coarse-to-fine token prediction.
- [Bézier Splatting for Fast and Differentiable Vector Graphics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16424): Represents vector graphics with Bézier Splatting, sampling 2D Gaussians along curves for fast differentiable rasterization and editable vector synthesis.
- [Bridging Continuous and Discrete Tokens for Autoregressive Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16430): Bridges continuous and discrete visual tokens for autoregressive visual generation, improving the representation interface for image generators.
- [DCEdit: Dual-Level Controlled Image Editing via Precisely Localized Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16795): Uses dual-level control for localized semantic image editing, targeting precise edits rather than global prompt-only generation.
- [Vision-Language Gradient Descent-driven All-in-One Deep Unfolding Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16930): Proposes VLU-Net, a deep unfolding restoration framework that uses a VLM refined on degraded image-text pairs to align degradation descriptions with image features across noise, blur, and lighting changes.
- [HyperLoRA: Parameter-Efficient Adaptive Generation for Portrait Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16944): Uses HyperLoRA for portrait synthesis, predicting adaptive LoRA-style parameters so personalization can be efficient without per-person full fine-tuning.
- [Follow-Your-Color](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16948): Handles multi-instance sketch colorization, adding fine-grained controllable image editing coverage.
- [Zero-Shot Styled Text Image Generation, but Make It Autoregressive](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.17074): Recasts zero-shot styled text image generation as autoregressive visual-token prediction for controlled typography and style rendering.
- [D2C: Unlocking the Potential of Continuous Autoregressive Image Generation with Discrete Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.17155): Bridges discrete tokenization and continuous autoregressive image generation, aiming to combine the scalability of AR modeling with the visual quality associated with continuous latent representations.
- [CO-SPY: Combining Semantic and Pixel Features to Detect Synthetic Images by AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.18286): Combines semantic and pixel features to improve detection of AI-generated images under model and post-processing shifts.
- [Diffusion-4K](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.18352): Targets ultra-high-resolution image synthesis with latent diffusion models.
- [Panorama Generation From NFoV Image Done Right](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.18420): Generates panoramas from narrow-field-of-view inputs, targeting view expansion, boundary continuity, and wide-field structural consistency.
- [Latent Space Super-Resolution for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.18446): Uses latent-space super-resolution to enable higher-resolution image generation with diffusion models.
- [InPO: Inversion Preference Optimization with Reparametrized DDIM for Efficient Diffusion Model Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.18454): Proposes InPO, an efficient diffusion alignment method using reparameterized DDIM inversion and preference optimization.
- [FDS: Frequency-Aware Denoising Score for Text-Guided Latent Diffusion Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.19191): Uses wavelet frequency decomposition to localize text-guided latent edits to selected spatial regions and frequency bands, reducing unintended color and detail changes.
- [ISPDiffuser: Learning RAW-to-sRGB Mappings with Texture-Aware Diffusion Models and Histogram-Guided Color Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.19283): Splits RAW-to-sRGB mapping into texture-aware diffusion detail recovery in grayscale space and histogram-guided color consistency for DSLR-like sRGB output.
- [Inference-Time Scaling for Flow Models via Stochastic Generation and Rollover Budget Forcing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.19385): Scales flow models at inference time with stochastic generation and rollover budget forcing, allocating more compute to harder trajectories.
- [High-Quality Spatial Reconstruction and Orthoimage Generation Using Efficient 2D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.19703): Generates true digital orthophotos with efficient 2D Gaussian Splatting, replacing DSM-heavy pipelines for spatial reconstruction and orthoimage rendering.
- [PCM : Picard Consistency Model for Fast Parallel Sampling of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.19731): Accelerates diffusion sampling with Picard Consistency Models, combining Picard iteration with a consistency objective for faster parallel denoising.
- [Fine-Grained Erasure in Text-To-Image Diffusion-Based Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.19783): Erases fine-grained concepts in text-to-image diffusion foundation models, strengthening visual-generator safety control.
- [Learning 3D Object Spatial Relationships From Pre-Trained 2D Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.19914): Learns 3D object-object spatial relationships from synthetic samples produced by pretrained 2D diffusion models, making this a spatial-reasoning boundary entry.
- [Latent Beam Diffusion Models for Generating Visual Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.20429): Applies beam search in diffusion latent space, dynamically sampling previous latents so generated image sequences remain coherent in non-linear visual storytelling.
- [Dissecting and Mitigating Diffusion Bias via Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.20483): Uses mechanistic interpretability to locate and intervene on diffusion-model bias sources inside the generator.
- [Unified Multimodal Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.20853): Unifies multimodal discrete diffusion across text, image, and structured states for cross-modal generation.
- [Toward Generalizable Forgery Detection and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.21210): Defines an FDR task and FakeReasoning model that combine AI-image detection with MLLM reasoning over forgery attributes using the 120K-image MMFR dataset.
- [Parametric Shadow Control for Portrait Generation in Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.21943): Shadow Director learns hidden shadow attributes from synthetic data, enabling parametric control over shadow shape, placement, and intensity during portrait generation.
- [Spatial Transport Optimization by Repositioning Attention Map for Training-Free Text-to-Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.22168): Introduces STORM, a training-free optimal-transport method that repositions object attention maps so T2I outputs follow prompted spatial locations.
- [Imperceptible but Forgeable: Practical Invisible Watermark Forgery via Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.22330): Shows invisible image watermarks can be forged onto arbitrary images with WMCopier, stress-testing provenance schemes against false-attribution attacks.
- [Semantix: An Energy Guided Sampler for Semantic Style Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.22344): Introduces semantic style transfer with an energy-guided sampler that aligns reference style and appearance to semantically corresponding regions in images or videos.
- [Object Isolated Attention for Consistent Story Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.23353): Improves story visualization by isolating object attention so generated image sequences keep characters consistent across scenes.
- [Investigating Text Insulation and Attention Mechanisms for Complex Visual Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.23461): Studies text insulation and attention in complex visual text generation, reducing interference between rendered text and surrounding visual semantics.
- [Enhancing Creative Generation on Stable Diffusion-based Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.23538): Introduces C3, a training-free feature-amplification method that boosts creative variation during Stable Diffusion denoising with practical amplification-factor guidelines.
- [Training-Free VAR Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.23897): uses a visual autoregressive model for training-free text-guided image editing.
- [HumanAesExpert](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.23907): builds a multimodal foundation model for human-image aesthetic assessment, useful as a quality model for human-image generation and editing.
- [Consistent Subject Generation via Contrastive Instantiated Concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.24387): Introduces CoCoIns, mapping latent codes to reusable pseudo-words for instantiated concepts so independent text-to-image generations can preserve the same subject without per-subject fine-tuning.
- [LayerCraft: Enhancing Text-to-Image Generation with CoT Reasoning and Layered Object Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.00010): Uses chain-of-thought reasoning to plan object layers before T2I synthesis, improving layered integration in complex scenes.
- [TurboFill: Adapting Few-step Text-to-image Model for Fast Image Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.00996): Trains an inpainting adapter on the few-step DMD2 T2I model with a three-step adversarial scheme, then evaluates fast inpainting on DilationBench and HumanBench.
- [IntrinsiX: High-Quality PBR Generation using Image Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.01008): Generates editable PBR intrinsic maps from image priors for relighting, material editing, and texture-generation workflows.
- [ATA: Adaptive Transformation Agent for Text-Guided Subject-Position Variable Background Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.01603): Introduces subject-position-variable background inpainting, with a PosAgent block that predicts foreground displacement so the retained subject fits the generated background.
- [Implicit Bias Injection Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.01819): Shows how text-to-image diffusion models can be steered to inject implicit bias, informing model-side safety and robustness for generation.
- [ILLUME+: Illuminating Unified MLLM with Dual Visual Tokenization and Diffusion Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.01934): Unified MLLM using dual visual tokenization and diffusion refinement.
- [Random Conditioning with Distillation for Data-Efficient Diffusion Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.02011): Compresses diffusion models with random conditioning and distillation, reducing data requirements while preserving conditional generation behavior.
- [Fine-Tuning Visual Autoregressive Models for Subject-Driven Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.02612): Fine-tunes visual autoregressive models for subject-driven generation so reference identity persists across prompts and scenes.
- [RoSMM: A Robust and Secure Multi-Modal Watermarking Framework for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.02640): Adds a diffusion-model watermarking framework that protects generated images through multimodal watermark signals designed for robustness and security.
- [Comprehensive Relighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.03011): Targets generalizable monocular human relighting and harmonization, adding a controllable illumination-editing model for image generation workflows.
- [FaR: Enhancing Multi-Concept Text-to-Image Diffusion via Concept Fusion and Localized Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.03292): Combines Concept Fusion data augmentation with a Localized Refinement loss to reduce overfitting and attribute leakage in multi-concept T2I personalization.
- [TabRep: Training Tabular Diffusion Models with a Simple and Effective Continuous Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.04798): Trains tabular diffusion models with a unified continuous representation informed by data-manifold geometry, making it a non-image generative-model migration candidate.
- [From Specificity to Generality: Revisiting Generalizable Artifacts in Detecting Face Deepfakes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.04827): Re-examines common artifacts across face deepfake generators and proposes a more general detection framework for synthetic-face provenance.
- [Parasite: A Steganography-based Backdoor Attack Framework for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.05815): Hides image-to-image diffusion backdoor triggers with steganography and embeds target content as the trigger, improving concealability and flexibility against existing defenses.
- [A Training-Free Style-aligned Image Generation with Scale-wise Autoregressive Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.06144): Uses a scale-wise autoregressive model for training-free style-aligned generation, preserving style consistency across generated images without extra fine-tuning.
- [HiFlow: Training-free High-Resolution Image Generation with Flow-Aligned Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.06232): Generates high-resolution images without training by applying flow-aligned guidance during sampling to stabilize detail synthesis.
- [OmniSVG: A Unified Scalable Vector Graphics Generation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.06263): Parameterizes SVG commands and coordinates as discrete tokens and uses VLM priors for structured, editable SVG generation beyond simple icon synthesis.
- [Matters: Training-free Fine-grained Image Caption Enhancement via Local Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.06666): Improves long-form image captions through a divide-then-aggregate patch perception strategy, supporting generation data quality rather than introducing a generator.
- [Compass Control: Multi Object Orientation Control for Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.06752): Conditions diffusion on orientation-aware compass tokens predicted from each object's 3D orientation, enabling multi-object T2I scenes with explicit object-centric orientation control.
- [DyDiT++: Diffusion Transformers With Timestep and Spatial Dynamics for Efficient Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.06803): Model candidate for image generation and editing: Improves diffusion transformers through timestep and spatial dynamics.
- [PDE Diffusion Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.07437): unifies and extends diffusion models through PDEs for inverse problems.
- [PIDSR: Complementary Polarized Image Demosaicing and Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.07758): Jointly handles polarized image demosaicing and super-resolution, adding a restoration route for polarization-sensitive image inputs.
- [PixelFlow: Pixel-Space Generative Models with Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.07963): Develops pixel-space generative flow models, reducing dependence on latent autoencoders while preserving fine image detail.
- [GigaTok: Scaling Visual Tokenizers to 3 Billion Parameters for Autoregressive Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.08736): Scales visual tokenizers to billion-parameter size for autoregressive image generation, strengthening the tokenizer backbone used by discrete visual generators.
- [Sculpting Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.09039): Uses dynamic masks and concept-aware optimization for multi-concept forgetting in diffusion models.
- [FractalForensics: Proactive Deepfake Detection and Localization via Fractal Watermarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.09451): Uses fractal watermarks for proactive deepfake detection and localization, adding explainable provenance signals for generated images.
- [MonoDiff9D: Monocular Category-Level 9D Object Pose Estimation via Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.10433): Generates monocular category-level 9D object poses with diffusion, combining DINOv2 coarse depth, point-cloud features, image features, and timestep conditioning.
- [PT-Mark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.10853): Embeds invisible watermarks in text-to-image diffusion models through semantic-aware pivotal tuning.
- [Seedream 3.0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.11346): A bilingual image-generation foundation-model report covering data construction, mixed-resolution training, cross-modality RoPE, reward-model post-training, and acceleration.
- [Flux Already Knows - Activating Subject-Driven Image Generation without Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.11478): Activates subject representations already present in FLUX to perform training-free subject-driven image generation.
- [PCDiff: Proactive Control for Ownership Protection in Diffusion Models with Watermark Compatibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.11774): Adds encrypted-credential access control to the diffusion decoder through a trainable fuser and hierarchical authentication layers, degrading image quality for unauthorized users while staying watermark-compatible.
- [WaterFlow: Learning Fast & Robust Watermarks using Stable Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.12354): Learns fast, robust image watermarks with Stable Diffusion, tying provenance signals to the generator rather than relying only on post-hoc watermark insertion.
- [InstantCharacter: Personalize Any Characters with a Scalable Diffusion Transformer Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.12395): Personalizes arbitrary characters with a scalable diffusion-transformer framework that preserves character appearance across poses and scenes.
- [SmartFreeEdit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.12704): A mask-free instruction-based image editing model combining an MLLM, region-aware tokens, reasoning segmentation, and hypergraph-enhanced inpainting.
- [Mask Image Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.12739): Introduces MaskWM-D and MaskWM-ED, using mask-guided decoding for global embedding, localization, and local extraction of robust image watermarks.
- [Set You Straight: Auto-Steering Denoising Trajectories to Sidestep Unwanted Concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.12782): A model-side contribution for image generation and editing models; core idea: Auto-Steering Denoising Trajectories to Sidestep Unwanted Concepts.
- [UniEdit-Flow: Unleashing Inversion and Editing in the Era of Flow Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.13109): Redesigns inversion and editing for flow models so real images can be mapped to flow trajectories and edited with less reconstruction error.
- [Personalized Text-to-Image Generation with Auto-Regressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.13162): Personalizes autoregressive T2I models by adapting discrete visual-token generation to reference subjects, extending personalization beyond diffusion backbones.
- [IMAGGarment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.13176): generates garments with fine-grained control over silhouette, color, and logo placement.
- [ESPLoRA: Enhanced Spatial Precision with Low-Rank Adaption in Text-to-Image Diffusion Models for High-Definition Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.13745): Fine-tunes T2I diffusion with LoRA on spatially explicit prompts mined from LAION-400M, improving object-position and layout consistency without extra generation-time modules.
- [DanceText: A Training-Free Layered Framework for Controllable Multilingual Text Transformation in Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.14108): Training-free controllable multilingual text transformation in images belongs under image editing Model.
- [PVLM: Parsing-Aware Vision-Language Model With Dynamic Contrastive Learning for Zero-Shot Deepfake Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.14129): Uses face parsing, text cues, and dynamic contrastive learning for zero-shot attribution of forged faces to unseen advanced generators.
- [Any Image Restoration via Efficient Spatial-Frequency Degradation Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.14249): Uses spatial-frequency degradation adaptation for all-in-one image restoration, unifying multiple restoration tasks without relying on large language models.
- [Towards NSFW-Free Text-to-Image Generation via Safety-Constraint Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.14290): Introduces LibraAlign-100K and T2I-SPO, extending DPO with a composite safety-and-quality reward so T2I safety alignment suppresses NSFW content without collapsing image quality.
- [3D Gaussian Head Avatars with Expressive Dynamic Appearances by Compact Tensorial Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.14967): Encodes dynamic texture attributes of 3D Gaussians with compact tensorial representations, reducing storage and runtime overhead for expressive head-avatar synthesis.
- [GIFDL: Generated Image Fluctuation Distortion Learning for Enhancing Steganographic Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.15139): Learns steganographic distortion from fluctuations among highly similar generated cover images, exploiting generated-image variability to improve steganographic security.
- [DSPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.15176): Applies direct semantic preference optimization to real-world image super-resolution, adding a preference-optimization recipe for image restoration and enhancement.
- [Manifold Biases for Generated-Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.15470): studies manifold-induced biases in zero-shot and few-shot generated-image detection.
- [Joint Image-Feature Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.16064): boosts generative image modeling by synthesizing images and features jointly.
- [From Reflection to Perfection: Scaling Inference-Time Optimization for Text-to-Image Diffusion Models via Reflection Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.16080): Scales inference-time optimization for text-to-image diffusion with reflection tuning.
- [Token-Shuffle: Towards High-Resolution Image Generation with Autoregressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.17789): Uses token shuffling to make autoregressive models handle high-resolution image tokens with better long-range dependence and efficiency.
- [REED-VAE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.18989): Re-encodes generated results into the VAE latent space to train diffusion models for iterative image editing.
- [GenPTW: Latent Image Watermarking for Provenance Tracing and Tamper Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.19567): Embeds latent image watermarks for provenance tracing and tamper localization, tying watermark recovery to both source attribution and manipulated-region identification.
- [RepText: Rendering Visual Text via Replicating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.19724): Targets visual text rendering through replication, relevant to text rendering in image generation.
- [CompleteMe: Reference-Based Human Image Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.20042): Completes human images with a dual U-Net and Region-focused Attention block that pulls fine clothing and accessory details from reference images.
- [Forging and Removing Latent-Noise Diffusion Watermarks Using a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.20111): Demonstrates single-image attacks that forge or remove latent-noise diffusion watermarks, probing watermark security without needing model internals.
- [Any-Subset Autoregressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.20456): revives any-subset autoregressive generation with principled parallel sampling and speculative decoding.
- [In-Context Edit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.20690): Uses large DiTs for in-context instructional editing without architecture changes, adds minimal parameter-efficient tuning, and filters inference-time noise samples with a VLM to improve precision with far less training data.
- [Yo'Chameleon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.20998): Personalizes multimodal generation from a few subject images through soft-prompt tuning, supporting both subject-specific QA and recreating visual details in new image contexts.
- [Capturing Conditional Dependence via Auto-regressive Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.21314): Models conditional dependence with auto-regressive diffusion, targeting generation settings where outputs must reflect structured dependence among conditions.
- [Improving Editability in Image Generation with Layer-wise Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.01079): Stores latent features and prompt embeddings from previous edits as layer-wise memory, then uses background-consistency guidance for sequential edits that preserve earlier changes.
- [SuperEdit: Rectifying and Facilitating Supervision for Instruction-Based Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.02370): Rectifies supervision for instruction-based image editing, cleaning or reformulating edit pairs to improve instruction fidelity and region preservation.
- [MUSAR: Exploring Multi-Subject Customization from Single-Subject Dataset via Attention Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.02823): Builds multi-subject customization from single-subject data through debiased diptych learning, dual-branch LoRA, and dynamic attention routing that maps each condition to the right generated subject.
- [Dataset Copyright Evasion Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.02824): Studies dataset-copyright evasion against personalized text-to-image diffusion models, adding a model-side safety and robustness stress line for visual generators.
- [No Other Representation Component Is Needed: Diffusion Transformers Can Provide Representation Guidance by Themselves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.02831): Extracts representation guidance directly from diffusion transformers, avoiding a separate representation model for generation control.
- [Not All Parameters Matter: Masking Diffusion Models for Enhancing Generation Ability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.03097): Analyzes time-wise U-Net parameter contributions during denoising and masks less useful parameters at selected stages to better separate structure formation from detail synthesis.
- [PiCo: Enhancing Text-Image Alignment with Improved Noise Selection and Precise Mask Control in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.03203): Adds training-free noise selection and referring-mask control to improve complex-prompt text-image alignment in compositional diffusion generation.
- [Safer Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.03338): Reduces intellectual-property risk in visual generative AI through prompt-side mitigation.
- [Deepfakes on Demand: The rise of accessible non-consensual deepfake image generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.03859): Audits Hugging Face and Civitai metadata, finding almost 35,000 public deepfake model variants and about 15 million downloads since November 2022.
- [Multi-turn Consistent Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.04320): Uses flow-matching inversion, dual-objective LQR sampling, and adaptive attention highlighting to reduce error accumulation across iterative edits.
- [Learning Real Facial Concepts for Independent Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.04460): Learns real facial concepts for independent deepfake detection, separating authentic identity cues from generator-specific artifacts to improve cross-method generalization.
- [Mogao: An Omni Foundation Model for Interleaved Multi-Modal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.05472): Omni foundation model for interleaved multimodal generation.
- [MonetGPT: Solving Puzzles Enhances MLLMs' Image Retouching Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.06176): Teaches an MLLM to critique raw photos, choose procedural retouching remedies, and execute pre-authored image operations after puzzle-style training.
- [HDGlyph: A Hierarchical Disentangled Glyph-Based Framework for Long-Tail Text Rendering in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.06543): Uses HDGlyph to render long-tail text in diffusion images, disentangling glyph structure hierarchically so rare characters and layouts remain legible.
- [KDC-Diff: A Latent-Aware Diffusion Model with Knowledge Retention for Memory-Efficient Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.06995): Streamlines a U-Net with dual-layer knowledge distillation and latent-space replay continual learning to reduce text-to-image diffusion compute while retaining quality.
- [Generative Pre-trained Autoregressive Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.07344): Combines autoregressive latent-frame prediction with diffusion loss in GPDiT, adding causal attention and rotation-based time conditioning for efficient long-range visual generation.
- [Unified Continuous Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.07447): Unifies training and sampling for diffusion, flow-matching, and consistency-style continuous generative models, with image-generation results on standard benchmarks.
- [PosterO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.07843): Structures layout trees so language models can perform generalized content-aware visual layout generation.
- [Improving Data Fidelity via Diffusion Model-based Correction and Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.08526): Uses diffusion-based correction with imbalanced stochastic editing followed by cascaded conditional diffusion super-resolution to improve low-fidelity scientific and climate data.
- [SPAST: Arbitrary style transfer with style priors via pre-trained large-scale model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.08695): Speeds arbitrary style transfer with a local-global window stylization module and style-prior loss that better preserves content structure while using a pretrained large-scale model.
- [MixBridge: Heterogeneous Image-to-Image Backdoor Attack through Mixture of Schrödinger Bridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.08809): Studies heterogeneous backdoor triggers in diffusion Schrödinger bridge image-to-image models by training with poisoned image pairs instead of modifying SDEs.
- [IntrinsicEdit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.08889): Performs precise generative image manipulation in intrinsic representation space.
- [BLIP3-o](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.09568): An open unified multimodal model family that generates CLIP image features with a diffusion transformer and uses sequential pretraining plus curated instruction data.
- [LightLab](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.09608): Controls image light sources with diffusion models, adding a physically meaningful image-editing control axis.
- [Exploring the Deep Fusion of Large Language Models and Diffusion Transformers for Text-to-Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.10046): Deeply fuses LLMs with diffusion transformers so language reasoning participates inside T2I synthesis rather than only text encoding.
- [Style Customization of Text-to-Vector Generation with Image Diffusion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.10558): Customizes text-to-vector generation with image-diffusion priors, transferring style while keeping vector outputs editable rather than raster-only.
- [3D-Fixup: Advancing Photo Editing with 3D Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.10566): Uses 3D priors to improve photo editing, adding geometry-aware control to image editing.
- [Text-Image Collaborative Erasing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.11131): Combines text and image evidence to erase undesirable concepts from text-to-image diffusion while preserving benign usability.
- [Diffusion-NPO: Negative Preference Optimization for Better Preference Aligned Generation of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.11245): Applies negative preference optimization to diffusion models, improving preference alignment for visual generation.
- [A Fourier Space Perspective on Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.11278): Analyzes diffusion in Fourier space, showing high-frequency components lose SNR earlier than low-frequency ones under DDPM noise and explaining why reverse diffusion tends to form coarse structure before fine detail.
- [Decomposing stimulus-specific sensory neural information via diffusion models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.11309): Uses diffusion models to decompose stimulus-specific neural information, making this a neuroscience analysis boundary item rather than a visual generator.
- [PSDiffusion: Harmonized Multi-Layer Image Generation via Layout and Appearance Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.11468): Aligns layout and appearance across multiple generated layers so foreground, background, and layer relations stay coherent.
- [Bootstrapping Diffusion: Diffusion Model Training Leveraging Partial and Corrupted Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.11825): Trains diffusion models from complementary partial views such as low-resolution images or short videos by first fitting separate view models and then bootstrapping a full-data model.
- [Measurement Score-Based Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.11853): Learns partial measurement scores from noisy, subsampled measurements and stochastically combines them to generate full images or solve inverse problems without clean targets.
- [Training Latent Diffusion Models with Interacting Particle Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.12412): Trains latent diffusion with interacting particle algorithms, replacing independent trajectory updates with particle interactions for improved generative training dynamics.
- [DragLoRA: Online Optimization of LoRA Adapters for Drag-based Image Editing in Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.12427): Optimizes LoRA adapters online for drag-based editing, using denoising score distillation and improved point-tracking consistency instead of directly editing DDIM-inverted features.
- [Degradation-Aware Feature Perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.12630): Uses degradation-aware feature perturbations for all-in-one image restoration, complementing restoration-model coverage in image editing.
- [CURE: Concept Unlearning via Orthogonal Representation Editing in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.12677): Performs concept unlearning in diffusion models through orthogonal representation editing, targeting safer and more controllable visual generation.
- [SounDiT: Geo-Contextual Soundscape-to-Landscape Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.12734): Synthesizes geographically coherent landscape images from environmental soundscapes with a DiT conditioned on sound and geo-context, supported by SoundingSVI and SonicUrban.
- [OmniStyle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.14028): filters high-quality style-transfer data at scale for visual generation and editing.
- [UniGen: Enhanced Training & Test-Time Strategies for Unified Multimodal Understanding and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.14682): Combines training and test-time strategies for unified multimodal understanding and generation, tightening the bridge between comprehension and synthesis.
- [BAGEL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.14683): A decoder-only unified multimodal pretraining model trained on interleaved text, image, video, and web data for reasoning, generation, and image manipulation.
- [FaceCrafter: Identity-Conditional Diffusion with Disentangled Control over Facial Pose, Expression, and Emotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.15313): Adds lightweight cross-attention control modules to identity-conditional diffusion so pose, expression, and emotion can change independently while preserving identity.
- [Responsible Diffusion Models via Constraining Text Embeddings within Safe Regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.15427): Finds safe semantic directions in text-embedding space and constrains prompts within safe regions, reducing NSFW or biased outputs without full diffusion fine-tuning.
- [IA-T2I: Internet-Augmented Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.15779): Augments T2I generation with internet retrieval, bringing external visual-textual evidence into the generation process for prompts needing up-to-date or specific details.
- [Style Transfer with Diffusion Models for Synthetic-to-Real Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.16360): Uses diffusion-based style transfer for synthetic-to-real adaptation, combining class-wise adaptive instance normalization with cross-attention and selective attention filtering to preserve semantics under adverse-domain translation.
- [One-Step Diffusion Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.16687): uses semantic distillation for one-step diffusion-based image compression.
- [REPA Works Until It Doesn't: Early-Stopped, Holistic Alignment Supercharges Diffusion Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.16792): Shows where REPA representation alignment fails and uses early-stopped holistic alignment to improve diffusion training.
- [Guided Diffusion Sampling on Function Spaces with Applications to PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.17004): Trains a discretization-agnostic function-space diffusion model with neural operators and plug-and-play guidance for sparse-observation PDE inverse problems.
- [When Are Concepts Erased From Diffusion Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.17013): Studies when concept-erasure methods actually remove concepts from diffusion models, informing safe image generation and editing.
- [Delving into RL for Image Generation with CoT: A Study on DPO vs. GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.17017): Study of DPO and GRPO for chain-of-thought-style reinforcement learning in image generation.
- [TextFlux: An OCR-Free DiT Model for High-Fidelity Multilingual Scene Text Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.17778): Uses a DiT architecture for multilingual scene-text synthesis without OCR encoders, relying on the diffusion model's contextual reasoning for glyph accuracy and scene integration.
- [RestoreVAR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.18047): Uses visual autoregressive generation for all-in-one image restoration, extending generative image editing toward restoration tasks.
- [OmniConsistency: Learning Style-Agnostic Consistency from Paired Stylization Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.18445): Learns style-agnostic consistency from paired stylization data, relevant to image editing consistency.
- [Mod-Adapter: Tuning-Free and Versatile Multi-concept Personalization via Modulation Adapter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.18612): Introduces a tuning-free modulation adapter for multi-concept personalization, controlling several subjects or styles without per-concept training.
- [Towards Generalized Proactive Defense against Face Swapping with Contour-Hybrid Watermark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.19081): Protects faces against swapping with a contour-hybrid watermark, targeting generalized proactive defense when attackers use unseen face-swap methods.
- [Enhancing Text-to-Image Diffusion Transformer via Split-Text Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.19261): Improves text-to-image diffusion transformers through split-text conditioning.
- [HF-VTON: High-Fidelity Virtual Try-On via Consistent Geometric and Semantic Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.19638): Aligns garment geometry and semantics across poses so virtual try-on preserves clothing structure, texture, and fine details on target human images.
- [DiSA: Diffusion Step Annealing in Autoregressive Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.20297): Anneals diffusion steps during autoregressive image generation, using fewer steps for later, more constrained tokens to improve inference efficiency.
- [Integrating Intermediate Layer Optimization and Projected Gradient Descent for Solving Inverse Problems with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.20789): Combines intermediate layer optimization with projected gradient descent in DMILO and DMILO-PGD to reduce memory burden and improve diffusion inverse-problem convergence.
- [Rendering-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Vector Graphics Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.20793): Trains SVG-generating vision-language models with rendered-image feedback to improve faithful vector graphics.
- [Create Anything Anywhere: Layout-Controllable Personalized Diffusion Model for Multiple Subjects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.20909): Controls multi-subject personalized diffusion with layouts, allowing specified concepts to appear at user-chosen locations while preserving each subject identity.
- [Conditional Diffusion Models with Classifier-Free Gibbs-like Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.21101): Shows standard CFG need not correspond to a well-defined diffusion model near zero noise, then proposes Gibbs-like guidance to improve the quality-diversity trade-off in conditional generation.
- [DetailFlow: 1D Coarse-to-Fine Autoregressive Image Generation via Next-Detail Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.21473): Generates images autoregressively through coarse-to-fine next-detail prediction, adding a detail-progressive visual-generation route.
- [DiffDecompose: Layer-Wise Decomposition of Alpha-Composited Images via Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.21541): Decomposes alpha-composited images into layers with diffusion transformers, recovering editable objects, transparency, and occlusion structure.
- [What is Adversarial Training for Diffusion Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.21742): Recasts adversarial training for diffusion as equivariance and flow-smoothness enforcement, improving robustness to outliers, corrupted data, and memorization without assuming a noise model.
- [One-Way Ticket: Time-Independent Unified Encoder for Distilling Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.21960): Distills T2I diffusion with a time-independent shared encoder and time-specific decoders, reducing redundant U-Net encoder computation while preserving few-step image quality.
- [D-Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.22002): Applies direct preference optimization to diffusion models so generated samples are aligned for visual consistency.
- [Rhetorical Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.22792): Uses two-layer diffusion policy optimization so text-to-image models can render implicit meanings in rhetorical prompts.
- [FlowAlign: Trajectory-Regularized, Inversion-Free Flow-based Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.23145): Regularizes flow trajectories for inversion-free image editing, preserving source structure while reducing inversion artifacts.
- [KGMark: A Diffusion Watermark for Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.23873): Applies diffusion watermarking to knowledge graphs, an off-axis provenance method for graph data rather than generated images.
- [EasyText: Controllable Diffusion Transformer for Multilingual Text Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.24417): Builds a controllable diffusion-transformer model for multilingual scene-text rendering with explicit content, position, and script control.
- [Reason-SVG: Hybrid Reward RL for Aha-Moments in Vector Graphics Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.24499): Applies hybrid-reward reinforcement learning to vector-graphics generation, improving SVG structure and semantic alignment.
- [ReasonGen-R1: CoT for Autoregressive Image generation models through SFT and RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.24875): Adds chain-of-thought style planning to autoregressive image generation through supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning.
- [RelDiff: Relational Data Generative Modeling with Graph-Based Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.00710): Uses graph-based diffusion for relational data generation, making this a structured-data generative model rather than an image-generation entry.
- [Breaking Latent Prior Bias in Detectors for Generalizable AIGC Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.00874): Mitigates latent-prior bias in AIGC image detectors so detection generalizes across generators instead of relying on prior shortcuts.
- [Autoregressive Images Watermarking through Lexical Biasing: An Approach Resistant to Regeneration Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.01011): Watermarks autoregressive image generation through lexical biasing over tokens, targeting robustness to regeneration attacks.
- [Ultra-High-Resolution Image Synthesis: Data, Method and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.01331): Provides Aesthetic-4K plus Diffusion-4K, using a scale-consistent VAE and wavelet-based latent fine-tuning for direct 4K image synthesis and evaluation.
- [NoiseAR: AutoRegressing Initial Noise Prior for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.01337): Learns an autoregressive, controllable initial-noise prior for diffusion models, replacing fixed Gaussian starts with a structured prior that can encode external control.
- [DNAEdit: Direct Noise Alignment for Text-Guided Rectified Flow Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.01430): Aligns noise directly for text-guided rectified-flow editing, reducing inversion and reconstruction error in edited images.
- [Guiding Registration with Emergent Similarity from Pre-Trained Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.02419): Uses off-the-shelf diffusion features as semantic similarity for deformable medical image registration, aligning corresponding structures when intensity losses fail.
- [RelationAdapter: Learning and Transferring Visual Relation with Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.02528): Learns transferable visual-relation adapters for diffusion transformers so object relations remain stable in new generated scenes.
- [Smoothed Preference Optimization via ReNoise Inversion for Aligning Diffusion Models with Varied Human Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.02698): Smooths preference optimization for text-to-image diffusion under varied human preferences.
- [Native-Resolution Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.03131): Proposes NiT, a native-resolution diffusion transformer that models variable-length visual tokens so one generator handles arbitrary resolutions and aspect ratios.
- [Sampling Preferences Yields Simple Trustworthiness Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.03399): Derives simple trustworthiness scores by sampling preferences, using preference variability as an uncertainty signal for generative or multimodal systems.
- [A Data-Driven Diffusion-based Approach for Audio Deepfake Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.03425): Uses diffusion-generated counterfactuals or explanations for audio deepfake analysis, making this an audio-forensics boundary item.
- [Diffusion-Based Priors for Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.03979): solves inverse problems with approximation-free ensemble sampling over diffusion priors.
- [HMAR: Efficient Hierarchical Masked Auto-Regressive Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.04421): Reworks visual autoregression with hierarchical masked generation, avoiding all-token parallel prediction at each scale and reducing sequence cost while allowing flexible sampling schedules.
- [Text-Aware Real-World Image Super-Resolution via Diffusion Model with Joint Segmentation Decoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.04641): Introduces TADiSR, combining text-aware attention with joint segmentation decoders and full-image text-mask synthesis so real-world super-resolution preserves textual structures.
- [Multi-scale Image Super Resolution with a Single Auto-Regressive Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.04990): Uses VAR-style next-scale latent residual prediction for image super-resolution, extending a single autoregressive model across multiple output scales instead of fixing one resolution.
- [AliTok: Towards Sequence Modeling Alignment between Tokenizer and Autoregressive Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.05289): Aligns tokenizer sequence modeling with autoregressive generation, improving discrete-token image-generation pipelines.
- [FocusDiff: Advancing Fine-Grained Text-Image Alignment for Autoregressive Visual Generation through RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.05501): Optimizes fine-grained text-image alignment in autoregressive visual generation with reinforcement learning over object, attribute, and relation rewards.
- [PartCrafter: Structured 3D Mesh Generation via Compositional Latent Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.05573): Generates structured 3D meshes with compositional latent diffusion transformers that model part-level relationships.
- [Unleashing the Potential of Consistency Learning for Detecting and Grounding Multi-Modal Media Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.05890): Uses consistency learning to detect and ground multimodal media manipulation, linking fake-region localization with cross-modal manipulation signals.
- [Feedback Guidance for Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.06085): adds feedback guidance to diffusion models, improving controllable generation without relying only on static prompts.
- [Antithetic Noise in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.06185): Studies antithetic noise for diffusion models, pairing negatively correlated noise samples to reduce sampling variance or improve generation efficiency.
- [STARFlow: Scaling Latent Normalizing Flows for High-resolution Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.06276): Scales latent normalizing flows for high-resolution image synthesis, testing invertible flow models as large visual generators.
- [AssetDropper: Asset Extraction via Diffusion Models with Reward-Driven Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.07738): Extracts visual assets with diffusion plus reward-driven optimization, turning generated or edited images into reusable asset cutouts.
- [Diffuse Everything: Multimodal Diffusion Models on Arbitrary State Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.07903): Generalizes multimodal diffusion to arbitrary state spaces, covering discrete, continuous, and structured visual states in one framework.
- [Rethinking Cross-Modal Interaction in Multimodal Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.07986): Rebalances cross-modal attention in multimodal diffusion transformers to improve text-image alignment.
- [SkipVAR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.08908): Accelerates visual autoregressive generation through adaptive frequency-aware skipping. Core idea: skip less informative visual tokens or steps so autoregressive generators can run faster with limited quality loss.
