- [Do Concept Replacement Techniques Really Erase Unacceptable Concepts?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.08991): Audits concept replacement in diffusion-based T2I and editing services, testing whether unacceptable concepts truly disappear or can re-emerge through edited inputs and replacement prompts.
- [Edit Flows: Flow Matching with Edit Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.09018): Adds edit operations to flow matching, directly relevant to instruction-guided image editing models.
- [Diffuse and Disperse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.09027): improves image generation with representation regularization for diffusion training.
- [Text-Aware Image Restoration with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.09993): Defines text-aware image restoration and introduces TAIR, targeting diffusion restoration failures where degraded textual regions are hallucinated into plausible but incorrect glyphs.
- [SPARKE: Scalable Prompt-Aware Diversity and Novelty Guidance in Diffusion Models via RKE Score](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.10173): Uses a prompt-aware relative kernel embedding score as sampling guidance, encouraging diffusion outputs to remain diverse and novel across semantically related prompts.
- [Pisces: An Auto-regressive Foundation Model for Image Understanding and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.10395): Pisces is an autoregressive foundation model designed to unify image understanding and generation.
- [A Crack in the Bark: Leveraging Public Knowledge to Remove Tree-Ring Watermarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.10502): Attacks Tree-Ring diffusion watermarks by using the public VAE to approximate latent inversion, showing provenance marks can be weakened without full access to the target generator.
- [SpectralAR: Spectral Autoregressive Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.10962): Tokenizes images into ordered spectral components from low to high frequency, making autoregressive causality better match visual structure than raster patch order.
- [What Exactly Does Guidance Do in Masked Discrete Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.10971): Analyzes guidance in masked discrete diffusion models, clarifying how guidance alters token distributions during discrete generative sampling.
- [A Watermark for Auto-Regressive Image Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.11371): Adapts token-level language-model watermarking to autoregressive image tokens, addressing re-tokenization and reverse-cycle-consistency issues in generated-image provenance.
- [GaussMarker: Robust Dual-Domain Watermark for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.11444): Embeds diffusion-model watermarks in both spatial and frequency domains and uses Gaussian-noise restoration to improve robustness against distortions and attacks.
- [Fusion of multi-source precipitation records via coordinate-based generative models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.11698): Fuses multi-source precipitation records with coordinate-based generative models, a geoscience data-generation item outside ordinary image editing.
- [MARché: Fast Masked Autoregressive Image Generation with Cache-Aware Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.12035): Accelerates masked autoregressive image generation with cache-aware attention and selective KV refresh, separating active and cached tokens to avoid recomputing stable representations.
- [ViSTA: Visual Storytelling using Multi-modal Adapters for Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.12198): Builds visual storytelling with multimodal adapters for T2I diffusion, keeping characters and story context aligned across generated panels.
- [AttentionDrag: Exploiting Latent Correlation Knowledge in Pre-trained Diffusion Models for Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.13301): Performs one-step point-based editing by exploiting latent feature correlations and attention knowledge inside pretrained diffusion models instead of iterative latent optimization.
- [CDST: Color Disentangled Style Transfer for Universal Style Reference Customization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.13770): Trains a two-stream style-transfer model that isolates color from style, enabling tuning-free reference customization that preserves content while borrowing style characteristics.
- [Provable Maximum Entropy Manifold Exploration via Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.15385): Uses diffusion models as implicit data-manifold priors and optimizes maximum-entropy exploration, a model-side novelty-generation method that is adjacent to image synthesis.
- [PAROAttention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.16054): Reorders attention patterns for efficient sparse and quantized visual generation. Core idea: exploit recurring attention structure so generative visual models can run with lower memory and compute cost.
- [Watermarking Autoregressive Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.16349): Embeds provenance at the discrete-token level of autoregressive image generators, adapting green-list watermark logic while handling image tokenizer inconsistency.
- [Visual-Instructed Degradation Diffusion for All-in-One Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.16960): Builds Defusion, an all-in-one restoration framework that conditions degradation diffusion on visual instructions rather than ambiguous text prompts or task-specific restoration heads.
- [SELFI: Selective Fusion of Identity for Generalizable Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.17592): Selectively fuses face-identity representations into a deepfake detector, using identity cues as forensic evidence while controlling their poor cross-manipulation generalization.
- [Controlled Generation with Equivariant Variational Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.18340): Derives controlled generation under variational flow matching, supporting both end-to-end conditional training and post-hoc Bayesian control of unconditional generators with equivariance constraints.
- [Auto-Regressively Generating Multi-View Consistent Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.18527): Generates multi-view consistent images autoregressively, improving viewpoint consistency for image and scene generation.
- [OmniGen2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.18871): An instruction-aligned multimodal generation model that decouples text and image decoding and adds editing, in-context generation, and reflection data pipelines.
- [Diffusion Tree Sampling: Scalable inference-time alignment of diffusion models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.20701): Uses diffusion-tree sampling for scalable inference-time alignment of diffusion models.
- [On Convolutions, Intrinsic Dimension, and Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.20705): Analyzes how convolutional structure and intrinsic dimension affect diffusion models, contributing architectural theory rather than a task-specific image editor.
- [Rethink Sparse Signals for Pose-Guided Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.20983): Revisits OpenPose-like sparse pose controls for T2I generation, adding spatially adaptive conditioning so sparse signals remain editable while preserving pose fidelity.
- [Elucidating and Endowing the Diffusion Training Paradigm for General Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.21722): Analyzes time-step dependencies, network hierarchy, noise-level relations, and multi-degradation behavior to adapt the diffusion training paradigm to general image-restoration networks.
- [Exploring Image Generation via Mutually Exclusive Probability Spaces and Local Correlation Hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.21731): Frames image generation through mutually exclusive probability spaces and local correlation hypotheses, proposing a probabilistic view of visual synthesis behavior.
- [Concept Pinpoint Eraser for Text-to-image Diffusion Models via Residual Attention Gate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.22806): Uses residual attention gates for concept erasure, addressing the limitation that cross-attention-only fine-tuning can preserve target removal poorly or distort unrelated concepts.
- [LD-RPS: Zero-Shot Unified Image Restoration via Latent Diffusion Recurrent Posterior Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.00790): Performs zero-shot unified image restoration with latent diffusion recurrent posterior sampling and multimodal semantic priors.
- [Representation Entanglement for Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.01467): Shows how representation entanglement affects diffusion-transformer training, simplifying high-quality generative-model optimization.
- [Autoregressive Image Generation with Linear Complexity: A Spatial-Aware Decay Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.01652): Uses spatial-aware decay to make autoregressive image generation linear-complexity, reducing sequence cost while preserving 2D image dependencies.
- [FreeMorph: Tuning-Free Generalized Image Morphing with Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.01953): Performs generalized image morphing without per-instance fine-tuning, using pretrained diffusion trajectories to handle inputs with different semantics or layouts.
- [Holistic Tokenizer for Autoregressive Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.02358): Introduces Hita, a holistic tokenizer for autoregressive image generation that preserves global and local visual structure before token prediction.
- [Fair Deepfake Detectors Can Generalize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.02645): Links fairness and cross-manipulation generalization in deepfake detection through back-door adjustment, treating data distribution and model architecture as confounders.
- [Aigi-Holmes: Towards Explainable and Generalizable AI-Generated Image Detection via Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.02664): Uses multimodal LLM reasoning for explainable, generalizable AI-generated image detection based on visual evidence.
- [RichControl: Structure- and Appearance-Rich Training-Free Spatial Control for Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.02792): Provides training-free structure- and appearance-rich spatial control for text-to-image generation. Core idea: schedule condition-feature injection and restart refinement so structural guidance improves without leaking artifacts or degrading appearance.
- [FreqCross: A Multi-Modal Frequency-Spatial Fusion Network for Robust Detection of Stable Diffusion 3.5 Generated Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.02995): Detects SD3.5-generated images with a three-branch network that fuses RGB spatial features, frequency-domain artifacts, and radial energy patterns.
- [SecureT2I: No More Unauthorized Manipulation on AI Generated Images from Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.03636): Separates images into permit and forbid sets during lightweight fine-tuning, preserving high-quality edits for authorized inputs while training forbidden inputs toward vague or blurred outputs.
- [Flow-Anchored Consistency Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.03738): Anchors consistency-model training in flow matching to stabilize one- or few-step visual generation and accelerate large text-to-image models.
- [Domain Generalizable Portrait Style Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.04243): Establishes dense semantic correspondence between input and reference portraits with a semantic adapter, then uses warped references for region-aligned style transfer across domains.
- [A Training-Free Style-Personalization via SVD-Based Feature Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.04482): Performs training-free style personalization with SVD-based feature decomposition, separating style-relevant directions from content features during generation.
- [Taming the Tri-Space Tension: ARC-Guided Hallucination Modeling and Control for Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.04946): Models and controls hallucination in text-to-image generation with ARC guidance.
- [DC-AR: Efficient Masked Autoregressive Image Generation with Deep Compression Hybrid Tokenizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.04947): Pairs a 32x deep-compression hybrid tokenizer with masked autoregressive generation, first predicting structure tokens and then residual refinements for efficient T2I.
- [LAID: Lightweight AI-Generated Image Detection in Spatial and Spectral Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.05162): Benchmarks lightweight neural detectors for AI-generated images across spatial, spectral, and fused domains on GenImage, targeting deployable detection rather than large forensic backbones.
- [Ensemble-Based Deepfake Detection using State-of-the-Art Models with Robust Cross-Dataset Generalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.05996): Studies ensemble deepfake detectors with robust cross-dataset generalization, adding a model-side authenticity method for generated visual media.
- [Enhancing LLM Watermark Resilience Against Both Scrubbing and Spoofing Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.06274): Proposes SEEK with sub-vocabulary decomposed equivalent texture keys to improve LLM watermark robustness against both scrubbing and spoofing, making this an off-axis provenance entry for the image-generation page.
- [Mitigating Watermark Forgery in Generative Models via Randomized Key Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.07871): Defends generative-model watermarks against forgery by randomizing key selection, aiming for provable robustness without embedding many simultaneous keys into each output.
- [Vision Foundation Models as Effective Visual Tokenizers for Autoregressive Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.08441): Uses vision foundation models as visual tokenizers for autoregressive image generation, connecting pretrained visual representations to generation.
- [Learning Diffusion Models with Flexible Representation Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.08980): Provides training objectives that align diffusion-model internal representations with pretrained representation models, improving generation through flexible feature guidance.
- [Memory-Efficient Personalization of Text-to-Image Diffusion Models via Selective Optimization Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.10029): Switches between low-resolution backpropagation and high-resolution zeroth-order optimization, enabling memory-efficient T2I personalization while reducing structural distortions on edge devices.
- [RefSTAR: Blind Facial Image Restoration with Reference Selection, Transfer, and Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.10470): Restores blind facial images with RefSTAR, selecting references, transferring identity-relevant details, and reconstructing degraded faces.
- [CharaConsist: Fine-Grained Consistent Character Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.11533): Improves fine-grained character consistency so one character keeps identity details across poses, expressions, and scenes.
- [EC-Diff: Fast and High-Quality Edge-Cloud Collaborative Inference for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.11980): Splits diffusion inference across edge and cloud with EC-Diff, trading compute placement for faster high-quality generation.
- [RODS: Robust Optimization Inspired Diffusion Sampling for Detecting and Reducing Hallucination in Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.12201): Treats diffusion sampling as robust optimization, detecting high-risk steps from loss-landscape geometry and adaptively correcting perturbations to reduce hallucinated samples without retraining.
- [Compositional Discrete Latent Code for High Fidelity, Productive Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.12318): Conditions diffusion models on compositional discrete latent codes from self-supervised simplicial embeddings, improving ImageNet fidelity while enabling novel out-of-training combinations.
- [Resurrect Mask AutoRegressive Modeling for Efficient and Scalable Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.13032): Revives masked autoregressive image generation with bidirectional attention and 2D RoPE, improving parallel decoding while supporting text-driven generation.
- [DLSF: Dual-Layer Synergistic Fusion for High-Fidelity Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.13388): Fuses base and refined latent representations with adaptive global or dynamic spatial modules, preserving global coherence and local texture in Stable Diffusion synthesis.
- [IConMark: Robust Interpretable Concept-Based Watermark For AI Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.13407): Uses interpretable concept-based watermarking for AI images, tying watermark evidence to semantic concepts rather than only low-level signals.
- [PoemTale Diffusion: Minimising Information Loss in Poem to Image Generation with Multi-Stage Prompt Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.13708): Uses a training-free multi-stage prompt refinement pipeline so diffusion models better preserve abstract poetic imagery when generating images from verse.
- [Rethinking Individual Fairness in Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.14326): Revisits individual fairness in deepfake detection, examining whether detectors treat demographic or identity groups consistently under manipulation.
- [Seeing Through Deepfakes: A Human-Inspired Framework for Multi-Face Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.14807): Uses human-inspired multi-face reasoning to detect deepfakes across images containing several faces rather than assuming a single centered target.
- [Diffusion models for multivariate subsurface generation and efficient probabilistic inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.15809): Applies diffusion modeling to multivariate subsurface fields and probabilistic inversion, an off-axis generative-model use case rather than a natural-image synthesis method.
- [HarmonPaint: Harmonized Training-Free Diffusion Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.16732): Achieves training-free inpainting by manipulating diffusion attention with masks, harmonizing inserted content with surrounding structure and style without fine-tuning.
- [Generative Diffusion Models for Wireless Networks: Fundamental, Architecture, and State-of-the-Art](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.16733): Surveys diffusion architectures for wireless-network generation and optimization, making this a classification concern for the image-generation model page.
- [Lumina-mGPT 2.0: Stand-Alone AutoRegressive Image Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.17801): Releases a stand-alone autoregressive image-modeling version of Lumina-mGPT 2.0 for direct visual-token generation.
- [Livatar-1: Real-Time Talking Heads Generation with Tailored Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.18649): Uses tailored flow matching plus system optimizations for audio-driven talking-head generation, targeting lip-sync accuracy, pose stability, and real-time avatar throughput.
- [Enhancing Reward Models for High-Quality Image Generation: Beyond Text-Image Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.19002): Improves reward models for image generation beyond text-image alignment.
- [3DGauCIM: Accelerating Static/Dynamic 3D Gaussian Splatting via Digital CIM for High Frame Rate Real-Time Edge Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.19133): Accelerates static and dynamic 3D Gaussian Splatting with digital compute-in-memory, a rendering-hardware boundary item rather than a 2D image generator.
- [Continual Learning-Based Unified Model for Unpaired Image Restoration Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.19184): Unifies continual learning for unpaired image-restoration tasks.
- [ChoreoMuse: Robust Music-to-Dance Video Generation with Style Transfer and Beat-Adherent Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.19836): Uses SMPL-style motion intermediates in a diffusion framework so music-to-dance video generation can follow beat structure while transferring dancer style.
- [SCALAR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.19946): adds scale-wise conditional decoding for controllable visual autoregressive generation.
- [Frequency-Aware Autoregressive Modeling for Efficient High-Resolution Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.20454): Uses frequency-aware autoregressive modeling to synthesize high-resolution images more efficiently, prioritizing frequency structure rather than treating all visual tokens uniformly.
- [AIcomposer: Any Style and Content Image Composition via Feature Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.20721): Composes cross-domain image content without text prompts by integrating features during backward inversion and forward denoising while preserving the pretrained diffusion prior.
- [HAMLET-FFD: Hierarchical Adaptive Multi-modal Learning Embeddings Transformation for Face Forgery Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.20913): Uses CLIP-style vision-language reasoning with bidirectional fusion, letting textual authenticity embeddings guide hierarchical visual features while visual evidence refines image-adaptive prompts for face forgery detection.
- [MaXsive: High-Capacity and Robust Training-Free Generative Image Watermarking in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.21195): Improves training-free diffusion watermarking with a high-capacity design robust to rotation, scaling, and translation attacks without sacrificing watermark payload.
- [Suppressing Gradient Conflict for Generalizable Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.21530): Studies why online synthetic forgeries can hurt deepfake detectors, then suppresses gradient conflict between original and generated training sources for better unseen-manipulation generalization.
- [Exploiting Diffusion Prior for Task-Driven Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.22459): Uses pretrained diffusion priors to recover task-relevant details from heavily degraded images, targeting downstream vision performance rather than only perceptual restoration quality.
- [Visual Language Models as Zero-Shot Deepfake Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.22469): Uses VLM image-text reasoning as a zero-shot deepfake detector, replacing specialized binary classifiers with promptable forensic judgments over manipulated faces.
- [ShortFT: Diffusion Model Alignment via Shortcut-Based Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.22604): Aligns diffusion models with rewards through shortcut-based fine-tuning, backpropagating along a shorter trajectory-preserving denoising chain to reduce cost and gradient instability.
- [PixNerd: Pixel Neural Field Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.23268): Combines pixel neural fields with diffusion so images are generated through continuous pixel-field representations.
- [Training-Free Geometric Image Editing on Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.23300): Decouples geometric editing into object transformation, source-region inpainting, and target-region refinement, using a training-free diffusion pipeline evaluated on the GeoBench editing benchmark.
- [DC-AE 1.5: Accelerating Diffusion Model Convergence with Structured Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.00413): Improves DC-AE with a structured latent space that accelerates diffusion convergence and better supports high-quality generation.
- [ROVI: A VLM-LLM Re-Captioned Dataset for Open-Vocabulary Instance-Grounded Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.01008): Builds a VLM-LLM re-captioned dataset for open-vocabulary instance-grounded text-to-image generation. Core idea: connect rich global prompts with instance annotations so generators can better ground objects and categories in images.
- [Trans-Adapter: Transparent Image Generation with Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.01098): Provides a plug-and-play adapter that lets diffusion inpainting models process RGBA transparent images directly, supports ControlNet conditioning, and introduces LayerBench plus an alpha-edge quality metric.
- [Personalized Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.01151): Personalizes safety alignment for text-to-image diffusion models. Core idea: adapt image-generation safety constraints to user-specific preferences while preserving controllability.
- [MiraGe: Multimodal Discriminative Representation Learning for Generalizable AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.01525): Learns multimodal discriminative representations for AI-generated image detection, separating real and synthetic embeddings so detectors transfer to unseen generators.
- [Devil is in the Detail: Towards Injecting Fine Details of Image Prompt in Image Generation via Conflict-free Guidance and Stratified Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.02004): Injects fine details from image prompts with conflict-free guidance and stratified attention, reducing clashes between reference-detail preservation and prompt control.
- [Uni-Layout: Integrating Human Feedback in Unified Layout Generation and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.02374): Unifies layout tasks under one generator, trains a human-mimicking evaluator on Layout-HF100k, and aligns generation with that evaluator through dynamic-margin preference optimization.
- [BadBlocks: Lightweight and Stealthy Backdoor Threat in Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.03221): Demonstrates a low-cost T2I backdoor by poisoning selected U-Net blocks while leaving other diffusion components intact, making the trigger harder to spot through full-model inspection.
- [Beyond Isolated Words: Diffusion Brush for Handwritten Text-Line Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.03256): Uses Diffusion Brush for handwritten text-line generation, moving beyond isolated word synthesis toward full-line handwriting with diffusion control.
- [Draw Your Mind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.03481): Models user preferences at the condition level for personalized text-to-image diffusion generation.
- [StorySync: Training-Free Subject Consistency in Text-to-Image Generation via Region Harmonization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.03735): Maintains subject consistency in training-free T2I stories through region harmonization, aligning subject regions across generated images.
- [Zero-Residual Concept Erasure via Progressive Alignment in Text-to-Image Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.04472): Uses progressive alignment and closed-form model updates for concept erasure, targeting residual unsafe concepts left by efficient T2I editing methods.
- [Two-Way Garment Transfer: Unified Diffusion Framework for Dressing and Undressing Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.04551): Introduces TWGTM, a unified diffusion framework that jointly handles virtual try-on and virtual try-off so dressing synthesis and garment extraction constrain each other.
- [X-SAM: From Segment Anything to Any Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.04655): Extends Segment Anything toward any-segmentation behavior, supplying region control useful for generation and editing pipelines.
- [LightSwitch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.06494): Performs multi-view relighting with material-guided diffusion, adding material-aware lighting control for image and scene editing.
- [Prompt Stealing Attack against Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.06837): Studies effective prompt-stealing attacks against text-to-image diffusion models, adding a privacy and provenance risk for visual generation.
- [AR-GRPO: Training Autoregressive Image Generation Models via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.06924): AR-GRPO applies reinforcement learning to autoregressive image generation, connecting GRPO-style optimization with visual generation model training.
- [Canvas3D: Empowering Precise Spatial Control for Image Generation with Constraints from a 3D Virtual Canvas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.07135): Uses a 3D virtual canvas as a spatial-control interface for image generation, extending layout constraints beyond 2D boxes to object position, depth, and viewpoint cues.
- [SketchAnimator: Animate Sketch via Motion Customization of Text-to-Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.07149): Customizes motion in text-to-video diffusion so a static sketch can be animated according to a prompt, making this a video-generation edge case for the image page.
- [ForensicsSAM: Toward Robust and Unified Image Forgery Detection and Localization Resisting to Adversarial Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.07402): Adapts SAM-style vision foundations for forgery detection and localization with adversarially robust parameter-efficient tuning.
- [LaRender: Training-Free Occlusion Control in Image Generation via Latent Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.07647): Controls occlusion in image generation through latent rendering, allowing training-free foreground-background ordering edits inside the denoising process.
- [Comparison Reveals Commonality: Customized Image Generation through Contrastive Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.07755): Extracts the shared concept across a small reference set through contrastive inversion, reducing reliance on manual masks or prompts for customized image generation.
- [MambaTrans: Multimodal Fusion Image Translation via Large Language Model Priors for Downstream Visual Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.07803): Adds multimodal image translation using LLM priors.
- [NeeCo: Image Synthesis of Novel Instrument States Based on Dynamic and Deformable 3-D Gaussian Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.07897): Synthesizes novel instrument states from dynamic and deformable 3D Gaussian reconstructions, a domain-specific image-synthesis boundary case.
- [Leveraging Unlabeled Data from Unknown Sources via Dual-Path Guidance for Deepfake Face Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.09022): Uses dual-path guidance to exploit unlabeled face data from unknown sources, improving deepfake detector adaptation without assuming source labels.
- [SVGen: Interpretable Vector Graphics Generation with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.09168): Builds text-to-SVG generation with SVG-1M data, curriculum learning, and reinforcement optimization for editable vector graphics.
- [Semantic-Aware Reconstruction Error for Detecting AI-Generated Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.09487): Scores AI-generated images with reconstruction errors weighted by semantic regions, aiming to separate generator artifacts from ordinary low-level distortion.
- [High Fidelity Text to Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.10280): Improves text-to-image generation with contrastive alignment and structural guidance. Core idea: align prompt semantics with generated structure so fidelity is improved beyond surface visual quality.
- [Translation of Text Embedding Via Delta Vector to Suppress Strongly Entangled Content in Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.10407): Delta-vector text-embedding translation for controlling text-to-image diffusion belongs in image-generation Model, not multilingual.
- [Discriminative Codebook Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.10719): Uses a discriminative codebook prior to improve autoregressive image generation over discrete visual tokens.
- [Object Fidelity Diffusion for Remote Sensing Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.10801): Proposes OF-Diff for controllable remote-sensing synthesis, emphasizing object morphology fidelity so generated scenes remain useful for downstream detectors.
- [Match & Choose: Model Selection Framework for Fine-tuning Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.10993): Selects fine-tuning base models for text-to-image diffusion by matching target data and objectives, reducing trial-and-error before adaptation.
- [TimeMachine: Fine-Grained Facial Age Editing with Identity Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.11284): Edits facial age with fine-grained controls while constraining identity features, targeting age progression or regression without subject drift.
- [LoRAtorio: An intrinsic approach to LoRA Skill Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.11624): Composes multiple T2I LoRA adapters without training by comparing each adapter's denoised output against base-model behavior, selecting in-distribution LoRA skills during sampling.
- [SafeCtrl: Region-Based Safety Control for Text-to-Image Diffusion via Detect-Then-Suppress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.11904): Detects unsafe prompt regions and suppresses them during diffusion guidance, giving localized safety control rather than blanket prompt rejection.
- [USO: Unified Style and Subject-Driven Generation via Disentangled and Reward Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.18966): Unifies style- and subject-driven generation with disentangled learning and reward learning for separate style and identity control.
- [Improving Generalization in Deepfake Detection with Face Foundation Models and Metric Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.19730): Combines face foundation-model embeddings with metric learning to improve video deepfake detection on in-the-wild manipulations beyond the training distribution.
- [Pref-GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.20751): Replaces pointwise reward scores in T2I GRPO with pairwise preference win rates, mitigating reward hacking and stabilizing reinforcement learning.
- [Veritas: Generalizable Deepfake Detection via Pattern-Aware Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.21048): Detects deepfakes with pattern-aware reasoning, explaining forgery patterns instead of relying on dataset-specific artifacts.
- [Beyond the Safety Tax: Mitigating Unsafe Text-to-Image Generation via External Safety Rectification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.21099): Rectifies unsafe T2I outputs with an external safety module, aiming to reduce harmful content without paying the usual quality or alignment penalty.
- [FLORA: Efficient Synthetic Data Generation for Object Detection in Low-Data Regimes via finetuning Flux LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.21712): Fine-tunes Flux 1.1 Dev with LoRA to generate object-detection training images in low-data regimes, reducing synthetic-data cost enough for consumer-grade GPUs.
- [OptMark: Robust Multi-bit Diffusion Watermarking via Inference Time Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.21727): Embeds multi-bit diffusion watermarks through inference-time optimization, trading extra sampling computation for robustness and payload capacity.
- [Category-level Text-to-Image Retrieval Improved: Bridging the Domain Gap with Diffusion Models and Vision Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.00177): Turns category text queries into generated visual queries with a diffusion model, then uses vision encoders and an aggregation network to bridge the text-image retrieval domain gap.
- [FICGen: Frequency-Inspired Contextual Disentanglement for Layout-driven Degraded Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.01107): Addresses degraded layout-to-image synthesis with frequency-inspired contextual disentanglement, separating foreground layout cues from low-light or underwater context dominance.
- [2D Gaussian Splatting with Semantic Alignment for Image Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.01964): Represents missing image regions with semantically aligned 2D Gaussian splats, using explicit spatial primitives for inpainting rather than only pixel denoising.
- [Data-Driven Loss Functions for Inference-Time Optimization in Text-to-Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.02295): Learns loss functions for inference-time T2I optimization from data, replacing hand-designed guidance terms with task-calibrated objectives.
- [An Efficient and Adaptive Watermark Detection System with Tile-based Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.02447): Detects generated-image watermarks with tile-level error correction, making extraction more adaptive to localized edits or corruptions.
- [DrDiff: Dynamic Routing Diffusion with Hierarchical Attention for Breaking the Efficiency-Quality Trade-off](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.02785): Uses hierarchical attention and dynamic routing in diffusion to allocate computation by region or token and improve the efficiency-quality tradeoff.
- [Delta Rectified Flow Sampling for Text-to-Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.05342): Introduces DRFS, an inversion-free rectified-flow editing sampler that models source-target velocity discrepancies and adds a time-dependent shift toward the target trajectory.
- [High-Frequency Fine Details in Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.05441): links missing fine visual details in generated images to high-frequency behavior, informing image-generation fidelity.
- [BIR-Adapter: A parameter-efficient diffusion adapter for blind image restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.06904): Adds a lightweight adapter to diffusion restoration models so blind image restoration can handle unknown degradations without full model retraining.
- [Universal Few-Shot Spatial Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.07530): A few-shot control adapter for diffusion models that adapts to novel spatial conditions from a few image-condition pairs.
- [FS-Diff: Semantic guidance and clarity-aware simultaneous multimodal image fusion and super-resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.09427): Jointly performs multimodal image fusion and super-resolution with semantic guidance and clarity-aware diffusion so weak target and background structures remain usable.
- [Locality in Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.09672): explains locality in image diffusion models as an effect of data statistics.
- [MarkDiffusion: An Open-Source Toolkit for Generative Watermarking of Latent Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.10569): Provides a latent-diffusion watermarking toolkit with unified algorithm integration, watermark visualization, and evaluation modules for fidelity, robustness, and detectability.
- [MetaSeal: Defending Against Image Attribution Forgery Through Content-Dependent Cryptographic Watermarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.10766): Binds attribution watermarks cryptographically to image content, defending generated-image provenance against forgery and copy attacks.
- [ToMA: Token Merge with Attention for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.10918): Redesigns token merging as attention-like GPU-friendly operations for diffusion models, reducing generation cost while preserving quality.
- [Removal Attack and Defense on AI-generated Content Latent-based Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.11745): Shows latent-distribution watermarks can be removed despite indistinguishability claims and studies defenses for diffusion watermark robustness under active attacks.
- [Robust Concept Erasure in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.12024): Gives a theoretical account of concept erasure robustness in diffusion models, informing safety control for visual generators.
- [Agent4FaceForgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.12546): Simulates realistic forgery creation with LLM agents equipped with profile and memory modules, a social-interaction environment for text-image consistency labels, and adaptive rejection sampling to filter diverse training samples for detectors.
- [End4: End-to-end Denoising Diffusion for Diffusion-Based Inpainting Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.13214): Trains an end-to-end denoising-diffusion detector for diffusion inpainting, using generation-process traces as the signal for localized image-manipulation detection.
- [StyleProtect: Safeguarding Artistic Identity in Fine-tuned Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.13711): Protects artistic identity during diffusion fine-tuning, targeting unauthorized style imitation while preserving legitimate generation quality.
- [Noise-Level Diffusion Guidance: Well Begun is Half Done](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.13936): Studies guidance at different denoising noise levels and adjusts early diffusion steps to improve generation control before later refinements.
- [Structured Information for Improving Spatial Relationships in Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.15962): Converts natural-language prompts into tuple-based structured spatial information with a fine-tuned language model, improving T2I spatial-relation faithfulness without changing the generator.
- [MANZANO: A Simple and Scalable Unified Multimodal Model with a Hybrid Vision Tokenizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.16197): Unified multimodal model with hybrid visual tokenization for understanding and generation.
- [SISMA: Semantic Face Image Synthesis with Mamba](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.17651): Uses a Mamba-based architecture for semantic face synthesis, modeling facial attributes and identity-conditioned structure in generated images.
- [Text Slider: Efficient and Plug-and-Play Continuous Concept Control for Image/Video Synthesis via LoRA Adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.18831): Finds low-rank directions inside a pretrained text encoder to provide plug-and-play continuous concept sliders with multi-concept composition for image and video synthesis.
- [DevFD: Developmental Face Forgery Detection by Learning Shared and Orthogonal LoRA Subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.19230): Learns shared and orthogonal LoRA subspaces so a face-forgery detector can adapt to new manipulation domains without forgetting previously learned forgery types.
- [DriftLite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.21655): adds lightweight drift control for inference-time scaling of diffusion models.
- [Deepfakes: we need to re-think the concept of "real" images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.21864): Reframes deepfake detection around the increasingly blurred boundary between captured, edited, and generated face images rather than a simple real/fake split.
- [SpecXNet: A Dual-Domain Convolutional Network for Robust Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.22070): Couples local spatial texture features with frequency-domain artifacts through a dual-domain convolutional network for robust deepfake detection.
- [Guidance Watermarking for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.22126): Injects watermark signals through diffusion guidance, enabling provenance marking during sampling without retraining the base generator.
- [Zero-Effort Image-to-Music Generation: An Interpretable RAG-based VLM Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.22378): Uses retrieval-augmented VLM reasoning to map images to music in an interpretable pipeline, which is off-axis for this image-generation model section.
- [FreqDebias: Towards Generalizable Deepfake Detection via Consistency-Driven Frequency Debiasing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.22412): Debiases deepfake detectors in the frequency domain with consistency constraints, reducing reliance on generator-specific spectral shortcuts.
- [Group Critical-token Policy Optimization for Autoregressive Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.22485): Optimizes critical image-token groups with policy optimization to align autoregressive image generation with rewards.
- [Follow-Your-Preference: Towards Preference-Aligned Image Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.23082): Aligns image inpainting with preferences, relevant to human-aligned image editing.
- [LightFair: Towards an Efficient Alternative for Fair T2I Diffusion via Debiasing Pre-trained Text Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.23639): Debiases text-to-image diffusion through efficient pre-trained text-encoder changes.
- [EditScore](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.23909): Builds EditReward-Bench and a 7B-72B EditScore reward-model family, then uses that editing-specific reward to make online RL effective for instruction-guided image editing.
- [Hyperspherical Latents Improve Continuous-Token Autoregressive Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.24335): Constrains continuous autoregressive image tokens to a fixed-radius hypersphere, reducing variance collapse under classifier-free guidance.
- [Real-Aware Residual Model Merging for Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.24367): Merges deepfake-specialist detectors by preserving real-image representations while attenuating generator-specific fake cues, reducing repeated retraining for new manipulation sources.
- [Causal-Adapter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.24798): Adapts frozen text-to-image diffusion backbones for faithful counterfactual image generation under causal interventions.
- [STAGE: Stable and Generalizable GRPO for Autoregressive Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.25027): Designs a stable, generalizable GRPO recipe for reinforcement learning in autoregressive image generation.
- [Advantage Weighted Matching: Aligning RL with Pretraining in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.25050): Aligns reinforcement learning with diffusion pretraining through advantage-weighted matching, reducing damage to the pretrained generative prior.
- [LayerD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.25134): Decomposes raster graphic designs into editable layers. Core idea: recover structured design components from flat images so visual generation and editing can support finer-grained control.
- [Aligning Visual Foundation Encoders to Tokenizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.25162): Aligns visual foundation encoders with diffusion tokenizers, improving the representation interface for generative diffusion models.
- [IMG: Calibrating Diffusion Models via Implicit Multimodal Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.26231): Calibrates diffusion sampling with implicit multimodal guidance, using cross-modal signals to steer generated images without explicit retraining.
- [Query-Kontext: An Unified Multimodal Model for Image Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.26641): Builds Query-Kontext as a unified multimodal model for image generation, local editing, and context-aware editing.
- [LAKAN: Landmark-assisted Adaptive Kolmogorov-Arnold Network for Face Forgery Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.00634): Combines facial landmarks with adaptive Kolmogorov-Arnold network layers to detect face forgeries from geometry-aware cues.
- [Extreme Blind Image Restoration via Prompt-Conditioned Information Bottleneck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.00728): Decomposes extreme blind restoration by projecting severely degraded inputs onto a milder LQ manifold, then using a frozen restoration model under a prompt-conditioned information bottleneck.
- [Fast, Secure, and High-Capacity Image Watermarking with Autoencoded Text Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.00799): Proposes LatentSeal, encoding full-sentence payloads into 256-dimensional unit vectors, securing them with an invertible rotation, and embedding them through a fine-tuned image watermarking model.
- [DragFlow: Unleashing DiT Priors with Region Based Supervision for Drag Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.02253): Uses DiT priors with region-based supervision for drag editing, translating point or region movement into local edit signals.
- [Fine-Tuning Diffusion Models via Intermediate Distribution Shaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.02692): Fine-tunes diffusion models by shaping intermediate distributions, guiding denoising trajectories rather than only optimizing final sample quality.
- [MASC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.04220): Improves autoregressive image generation with manifold-aligned semantic clustering over visual tokens.
- [TAG:Tangential Amplifying Guidance for Hallucination-Resistant Diffusion Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.04533): Applies training-free tangential amplifying guidance, using intermediate samples as projection bases to steer diffusion scores toward higher-probability manifold regions.
- [SpecGuard: Spectral Projection-Based Advanced Invisible Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.07302): Uses spectral projection for invisible watermarking, embedding robust provenance signals through frequency-domain structure.
- [SpotDiff: Spotting and Disentangling Interference in Feature Space for Subject-Preserving Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.07340): Disentangles interference in feature space for subject-preserving generation, improving learning-based personalization without costly per-subject optimization.
- [Local MAP Sampling for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.07343): Recasts diffusion inverse-problem inference as a sequence of local MAP subproblems along the denoising trajectory, bridging point-estimate reconstruction with posterior-sampling methods.
- [RePainter: Empowering E-commerce Object Removal via Spatial-matting Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.07721): Uses spatial-matting trajectory refinement with GRPO and composite rewards to remove e-commerce watermarks or promotional objects while preserving product backgrounds.
- [Optimal Stopping in Latent Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.08409): formulates latent-diffusion inference as an optimal stopping problem.
- [Towards Better & Faster Autoregressive Image Generation: From the Perspective of Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.09012): Uses spatial token entropy to tune sampling temperature and speculative-decoding acceptance, improving autoregressive image quality while cutting inference cost.
- [SynthID-Image: Image watermarking at internet scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.09263): Documents Google's deep-learning invisible watermarking system for AI-generated imagery, including deployment desiderata, threat models, verification service, and use across more than ten billion images and video frames.
- [MosaicDiff: Training-free Structural Pruning for Diffusion Model Acceleration Reflecting Pretraining Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.11962): Prunes diffusion sampling trajectories according to pretraining-stage dynamics, accelerating generation by exploiting where denoising computation is least useful.
- [Local-Global Context-Aware and Structure-Preserving Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.13649): Uses local-global context modeling and structure-preserving constraints to prevent pretrained diffusion super-resolution from hallucinating noise or incorrect content on highly degraded images.
- [NoisePrints: Distortion-Free Watermarks for Authorship in Private Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.13793): Verifies authorship for private diffusion models through distortion-free noise-space watermarks that do not require model-weight access at detection time.
- [OmniVerifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.13804) ([open-source code](https://github.com/Cominclip/OmniVerifier)): Adds a universal visual verifier to generation and editing loops, using ViVerBench-style verification data and sequential test-time scaling to reflect on visual outcomes and guide fine-grained correction.
- [PIA: Deepfake Detection Using Phoneme-Temporal and Identity-Dynamic Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.14241): Detects deepfakes with phoneme-temporal and identity-dynamic analysis, targeting lip-sync, face-swap, and avatar-driven manipulations.
- [Accelerated Multi-Modal Motion Planning Using Context-Conditioned Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.14615): Conditions diffusion-based robot motion planning on environment context so one planner can adapt across scenarios, making it an off-axis robotics planning item for this image-generation page.
- [WithAnyone: Towards Controllable and ID Consistent Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.14975): Constrains controllable person generation for ID consistency across pose, scene, and style changes.
- [Constantly Improving Image Models Need Constantly Improving Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.15021): Introduces ECHO, a benchmark-construction framework mined from real image-model use cases; this is a benchmark entry rather than a model-side image generator.
- [Latent Diffusion Model without Variational Autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.15301): Studies latent diffusion without a VAE, integrating latent representation learning into the generator to avoid reconstruction bottlenecks.
- [SEGA: A Stepwise Evolution Paradigm for Content-Aware Layout Generation with Design Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.15749): Generates layouts over background images through a feedback-style stepwise evolution process, using design priors to correct failed content-aware element placement.
- [OmniCast: A Masked Latent Diffusion Model for Weather Forecasting Across Time Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.18707): Builds a masked latent diffusion model for probabilistic weather forecasting across time scales, so it is a diffusion forecasting entry rather than an image-generation method.
- [Adversarial Concept Distillation for One-Step Diffusion Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.20512): Personalizes one-step diffusion models with OPAD, combining teacher-student distillation and adversarial supervision so the student preserves concept alignment in a single denoising step.
- [Face-MakeUpV2: Facial Consistency Learning for Controllable Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.21775): Uses Face-MakeUpV2 for controllable facial T2I, pairing FaceCaptionMask-1M with consistency learning to reduce attribute leakage and preserve reference identity.
- [Discovering Latent Graphs with GFlowNets for Diverse Conditional Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.22107): Discovers latent graphs with GFlowNets for diverse conditional image generation, representing multiple plausible outputs beyond seed variation.
- [Self-Attention Decomposition For Training Free Diffusion Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.22650): Decomposes self-attention for training-free diffusion editing.
- [Semantic Surgery: Zero-Shot Concept Erasure in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.22851): Performs training-free concept erasure by estimating target-concept presence in prompt embeddings, subtracting calibrated semantic directions, and using co-occurrence encoding plus visual feedback for multi-concept cases.
- [Training-Free Safe Text Embedding Guidance for Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.24012): A model-side contribution for image generation and editing models; core idea: Training-Free Safe Text Embedding Guidance for Text-to-Image Diffusion Models.
- [ETC: training-free diffusion models acceleration with Error-aware Trend Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.24129): Accelerates diffusion sampling without training by predicting denoising trends from historical trajectories and adding error-aware controls to limit multi-step reuse drift.
- [Routing Matters in MoE: Scaling Diffusion Transformers with Explicit Routing Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.24711): Adds explicit routing guidance to MoE diffusion transformers so expert choice better matches visual tokens and denoising stages.
- [SafeEditor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.24820): A post-hoc T2I safety editing method that uses a unified MLLM editing model to repair unsafe generated images after generation.
- [PSTF-AttControl: Per-Subject-Tuning-Free Personalized Image Generation with Controllable Face Attributes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.25084): Controls per-subject face attributes without tuning through PSTF-AttControl, aiming for personalized identity preservation plus fine-grained attribute edits.
- [Hawk: Leveraging Spatial Context for Faster Autoregressive Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.25739): Applies speculative decoding to autoregressive T2I generation and uses two-dimensional spatial context to align draft and target model outputs during token-by-token sampling.
- [SplitFlow: Flow Decomposition for Inversion-Free Text-to-Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.25970): Uses flow decomposition for inversion-free text-to-image editing.
- [FreeSliders: Training-Free, Modality-Agnostic Concept Sliders for Fine-Grained Diffusion Control in Images, Audio, and Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.00103): Estimates concept-slider directions at inference time, giving training-free, modality-agnostic fine-grained diffusion control across images, audio, and video.
- [TAUE: Training-free Noise Transplant and Cultivation Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.02580): Generates layered T2I scenes with TAUE by transplanting and cultivating noise across layers, avoiding fine-tuning while producing editable foreground and background components.
- [Seeing What You Say: Expressive Image Generation from Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.03423): Conditions image generation on speech so vocal prosody and spoken content can steer expressive visual synthesis beyond text-only prompts.
- [Prompt-Based Safety Guidance Is Ineffective for Unlearned Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.04834): Tests prompt-only safety guidance after concept unlearning and shows that unlearned text-to-image diffusion models need stronger model-side safeguards than surface prompt rewriting.
- [DeepForgeSeal: Latent Space-Driven Semi-Fragile Watermarking for Deepfake Detection Using Multi-Agent Adversarial Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.04949): Uses latent-space semi-fragile watermarking and multi-agent adversarial reinforcement learning for proactive deepfake detection under benign and malicious manipulations.
- [Personalized Image Editing in Text-to-Image Diffusion Models via Collaborative Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.05616): Introduces Collaborative DPO for image editing, representing users in a dynamic preference graph and using graph-derived embeddings to align edits with individual and neighbor preferences.
- [MambaOVSR: Multiscale Fusion with Global Motion Modeling for Chinese Opera Video Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.06172): Combines multiscale fusion with global motion modeling in a Mamba-based video super-resolution system for low-frame-rate Chinese opera archives.
- [Gait Recognition via Collaborating Discriminative and Generative Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.06245): Couples discriminative gait representation learning with diffusion-based distribution modeling, making it a biometric recognition method rather than a direct image-generation entry.
- [Improving Deepfake Detection with Reinforcement Learning-Based Adaptive Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.07051): Improves deepfake detector generalization with reinforcement-learning-based adaptive augmentation, choosing synthetic forgery transformations as detector weaknesses evolve.
- [VectorSynth: Fine-Grained Satellite Image Synthesis with Structured Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.07744): Uses VectorSynth for satellite image synthesis from polygonal semantic annotations, aligning vector geometry to pixels for fine-grained spatially grounded generation.
- [Continual Unlearning for Text-to-Image Diffusion Models: A Regularization Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.07970): Studies sequential concept-erasure requests in T2I diffusion and adds regularizers that limit parameter drift, reducing the utility collapse seen when unlearning methods are applied repeatedly.
- [Decoupling Bias, Aligning Distributions: Synergistic Fairness Optimization for Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.10150): Optimizes deepfake detectors with paired fairness debiasing and distribution alignment, aiming to reduce demographic bias without losing detection accuracy.
- [A Style is Worth One Code: Unlocking Code-to-Style Image Generation with Discrete Style Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.10555): Uses a discrete style space to unlock code-to-style image generation, fitting controllable image-generation models.
- [SP-Guard: Selective Prompt-adaptive Guidance for Safe Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.11014): Guides T2I safety with SP-Guard, estimating prompt harmfulness and applying selective masks so unsafe regions receive stronger inference-time control.
- [Arcee: Differentiable Recurrent State Chain for Generative Vision Modeling with Mamba SSMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.11243): Redesigns Mamba-style vision generation with a differentiable recurrent state chain, preserving cross-block state instead of reinitializing selective-scan memory.
- [Fine-Grained DINO Tuning with Dual Supervision for Face Forgery Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.12107): Tunes DINO features with dual supervision so face-forgery detectors can attend to fine-grained manipulation artifacts rather than only global identity cues.
- [MixAR: Mixture Autoregressive Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.12181): Uses MixAR for continuous-latent autoregressive image generation, reducing discrete-codebook fidelity limits through mixture modeling.
- [Infinite-Story: A Training-Free Consistent Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.13002): Maintains identity and style across multi-prompt story images with training-free identity prompt replacement and consistency controls over a scale-wise autoregressive generator.
- [MeanFlow Transformers with RAEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.13019): combines MeanFlow Transformers with representation autoencoders for visual generative modeling.
- [Trust in Vision-Language Models: Insights from a Participatory User Workshop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.13458): Reports a participatory workshop on when users trust VLMs, making this HCI evidence about model judging rather than an image-generation model.
- [FashionMAC: Deformation-Free Fashion Image Generation with Fine-Grained Model Appearance Customization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.14031): Generates garment-centric fashion images without deforming clothing textures, adding fine-grained controls over the human model's appearance.
- [Semantic Context Matters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.14063): improves semantic conditioning for autoregressive visual generation models.
- [A Comprehensive Study on Visual Token Redundancy for Discrete Diffusion-based Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.15098): This study analyzes and reduces visual-token redundancy in discrete diffusion-based multimodal LLMs.
- [Illustrator's Depth: Monocular Layer Index Prediction for Image Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.17454): Predicts monocular layer indices for raster images so flat illustrations can be decomposed into editable, ordered layers.
- [UnfoldLDM: Degradation-Aware Unfolding with Iterative Latent Diffusion Priors for Blind Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.18152): Integrates deep unfolding with latent diffusion priors, using multi-stage degradation-aware optimization to avoid known-degradation assumptions and over-smoothed blind restoration.
- [Uni-DAD: Unified Distillation and Adaptation of Diffusion Models for Few-step Few-shot Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.18281): Unifies diffusion distillation and few-shot domain adaptation in Uni-DAD, avoiding separate adapt-then-distill or distill-then-adapt pipelines.
- [When Generative Replay Meets Evolving Deepfakes: Domain-Aware Relative Weighting for Incremental Face Forgery Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.18436): Uses Domain-Aware Relative Weighting to separate risky and safe generative replay samples when updating face-forgery detectors for newly evolving deepfake domains.
- [Now You See It, Now You Don't - Instant Concept Erasure for Safe Text-to-Image and Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.18684): Provides a training-free one-shot weight modification method for concept erasure across text-to-image and text-to-video generators, aiming to remove target concepts while preserving nearby content.
- [DiP: Taming Diffusion Models in Pixel Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.18822): Trains diffusion models directly in pixel space, targeting detail fidelity and stable generation without latent compression.
- [DeCo: Frequency-Decoupled Pixel Diffusion for End-to-End Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.19365): Decouples pixel diffusion by frequency, separately modeling low-frequency structure and high-frequency texture for end-to-end image generation.
- [Beyond Binary Classification: A Semi-supervised Approach to Generalized AI-generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.19499): Proposes TriDetect, a semi-supervised detector that clusters latent fake-image patterns across GAN and diffusion artifacts with Sinkhorn assignment and cross-view consistency.
- [Clair Obscur: an Illumination-Aware Method for Real-World Image Vectorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.20034): Introduces COVec, an illumination-aware vectorization method that decomposes intrinsic image structure into editable vector regions for real-world raster images.
- [A Training-Free Approach for Multi-ID Customization via Attention Adjustment and Spatial Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.20401): Adapts single-identity customization models to multi-person image generation through attention adjustment and spatial control. Core idea: preserve multiple identities while reducing copy-paste artifacts and improving prompt controllability without extra training.
- [PixelDiT: Pixel Diffusion Transformers for Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.20645): Introduces pixel-space diffusion transformers for image generation, showing DiT backbones can operate directly on pixels.
- [DinoLizer: Learning from the Best for Generative Inpainting Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.20722): Adds a linear localization head over DINOv2 patch embeddings to detect semantically altered regions produced by generative inpainting.
- [AuthenLoRA: Entangling Stylization with Imperceptible Watermarks for Copyright-Secure LoRA Adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.21216): Couples stylization LoRA adapters with imperceptible image watermarks, making customized diffusion outputs traceable rather than only verifying adapter files.
- [Saddle-Free Guidance: Improved On-Manifold Sampling without Labels or Additional Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.21863): Uses positive curvature in log-density saddle regions as label-free guidance, improving on-manifold score-based sampling without an unconditional model or extra training.
- [Z-Image: An Efficient Image Generation Foundation Model with Single-Stream Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.22699): Introduces an efficient single-stream diffusion-transformer image generation foundation model.
- [StyleYourSmile: Cross-Domain Face Retargeting Without Paired Multi-Style Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.01895): Performs one-shot cross-domain face retargeting with data augmentation and dual encoders that separate domain-invariant identity from expression and style cues.
- [Improved Mean Flows: On the Challenges of Fastforward Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.02012): Studies the optimization and architecture challenges behind fast-forward mean-flow generative models, complementing later pixel and RAE mean-flow image-generation entries.
- [SplatSuRe: Selective Super-Resolution for Multi-view Consistent 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.02172): Selectively applies super-resolution to multi-view 3D Gaussian Splatting inputs to avoid view-inconsistent blur, making it a 3D rendering boundary item for this page.
- [Delta Sampling: Data-Free Knowledge Transfer Across Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.03056): Transfers adapters and fine-tuned behavior across diffusion base models with Delta Sampling, decoupling LoRA, LyCORIS, or ControlNet components from one fixed backbone.
- [Fully Unsupervised Self-debiasing of Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.03749): Applies unsupervised test-time self-debiasing to T2I diffusion, steering biased UNet attention without labeled fairness data or retraining.
- [DirectDrag: High-Fidelity, Mask-Free, Prompt-Free Drag-based Image Editing via Readout-Guided Feature Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.03981): Enables drag-based image editing without masks or prompts by aligning readout features, balancing artifact suppression with precise point motion.
- [MVRoom: Controllable 3D Indoor Scene Generation with Multi-View Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.04248): Generates 3D indoor scenes from coarse layouts with two-stage multi-view diffusion, layout-aware epipolar attention, and iterative text-to-scene expansion, making it a 3D boundary item.
- [Data-regularized Reinforcement Learning for Diffusion Models at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.04332): Anchors diffusion RL with forward KL to an off-policy data distribution, combining reward maximization and diffusion loss to reduce reward hacking at scale.
- [A Sanity Check for Multi-In-Domain Face Forgery Detection in the Real World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.04837): Defines the MID-FFD setting and proposes DevDet, using a face-forgery developer plus dose-adaptive fine-tuning to make real/fake cues dominate over domain cues.
- [Autoregressive Image Generation Needs Only a Few Lines of Cached Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.04857): Introduces LineAR, a training-free line-level KV cache compression method that exploits 2D visual attention structure to reduce memory in autoregressive image generation.
- [Rethinking the Use of Vision Transformers for AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.04969): Rethinks ViT features for AI-image detection, showing earlier CLIP-ViT layers provide more local and generalizable synthetic-image cues than final features.
- [Your Latent Mask is Wrong: Pixel-Equivalent Latent Compositing for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.05198): Proposes pixel-equivalent latent compositing for diffusion inpainting, replacing downsampled latent-mask interpolation with full-resolution mask and soft-alpha behavior.
- [One Layer Is Enough](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.07829): adapts pretrained visual encoders for image generation with a minimal additional layer.
- [Exposing Hidden Biases in Text-to-Image Models via Automated Prompt Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.08724): Uses Bias-Guided Prompt Search, combining an LLM prompt generator with attribute classifiers, to find neutral prompts that trigger demographic bias in T2I outputs.
- [Omni-Attribute: Open-vocabulary Attribute Encoder for Visual Concept Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.10955): Uses Omni-Attribute as an open-vocabulary attribute encoder, isolating identity, expression, lighting, style, or other factors for concept personalization.
- [FactorPortrait: Controllable Portrait Animation via Disentangled Expression, Pose, and Viewpoint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.11645): Disentangles expression, head pose, and viewpoint factors for controllable portrait animation, giving image-driven face generation more separable control signals.
- [SVG-T2I: Scaling Up Text-to-Image Latent Diffusion Model Without Variational Autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.11749): Scales T2I latent diffusion directly in visual-foundation-model feature space, replacing VAE latents with self-supervised visual representations for generation.
- [On the Design of One-step Diffusion via Shortcutting Flow Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.11831): Unifies one-step shortcut diffusion designs around flow-path shortcutting, clarifying the training choices behind few-step and one-step image generators.
- [Integrating Fourier Neural Operator with Diffusion Model for Autoregressive Predictions of Three-dimensional Turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.12628): Combines an implicit adaptive Fourier neural operator with diffusion for 3D turbulence prediction, a scientific forecasting application rather than image generation.
- [Scalable Pre-Training of Visual Tokenizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.13687): studies scalable visual-tokenizer pretraining for generation models.
- [Semantic Mismatch and Perceptual Degradation: A New Perspective on Image Editing Immunity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.14320): Introduces SIFM, which perturbs intermediate diffusion features so protected images either mismatch malicious edit prompts or degrade perceptually, and evaluates this with the MLLM-based ISR metric.
- [Where is the Watermark? Interpretable Watermark Detection at the Block Level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.14994): Embeds image watermarks with block-wise DWT statistics and produces region-level detection maps, making watermark presence and tampering locations interpretable.
- [Qwen-Image-Layered: Towards Inherent Editability via Layer Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.15603): Makes Qwen-Image inherently editable by decomposing generated images into layers for separate object, background, and transparency edits.
- [Restore-R1: Efficient Image Restoration Agents via Reinforcement Learning with Multimodal LLM Perceptual Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.18599): Uses multimodal LLM perceptual feedback to train image-restoration agents with RL.
- [AsyncDiff: Asynchronous Timestep Conditioning for Enhanced Text-to-Image Diffusion Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.18675): Decouples the denoiser conditioning timestep from the numerical update timestep and trains a lightweight predictor with GRPO to improve T2I inference.
- [DVI: Disentangling Semantic and Visual Identity for Training-Free Personalized Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.18964): Disentangles semantic and visual identity in DVI, preserving facial identity while matching lighting, skin texture, and environmental tone in tuning-free personalization.
- [WaTeRFlow: Watermark Temporal Robustness via Flow Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.19048): Improves image-watermark survival under image-to-video generation by enforcing flow consistency so provenance signals remain detectable across generated frames.
- [3SGen: Unified Subject, Style, and Structure-Driven Image Generation with Adaptive Task-specific Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.19271): Unifies subject-, style-, and structure-driven image generation with adaptive task-specific memory for controllable visual synthesis.
- [FreeInpaint: Tuning-free Prompt Alignment and Visual Rationality Enhancement in Image Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.21104): Improves text-guided inpainting with FreeInpaint, aligning prompts and visual rationality at inference without tuning the base diffusion model.
- [TAMEing Long Contexts in Personalization: Towards Training-Free and State-Aware MLLM Personalized Assistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.21616): Studies long-context personalized MLLM assistance for named visual concepts, closer to multimodal assistant memory than image-generation model training.
- [DPAR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.21867): uses dynamic patchification to make autoregressive visual generation more efficient.
- [Self-Evaluation Unlocks Any-Step Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.22374): Trains Self-E from scratch with flow-style local learning plus self-evaluation from its own score estimates, enabling one model to support few-step and long-trajectory T2I sampling.
- [NOWA: Null-space Optical Watermark for Invisible Capture Fingerprinting and Tamper Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.22501): Combines an aperture phase mask with a Null-Space Network so invisible optical watermarks survive reconstruction and enable pixel-level tamper localization.
- [Reverse Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.22984): Analyzes reverse personalization for T2I faces, identifying how identity-specific features can be removed or modified without per-identity fine-tuning.
- [GARDO: Reinforcing Diffusion Models without Reward Hacking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.24138): Reinforces diffusion models while constraining reward optimization to avoid reward hacking and preserve visual quality.
- [FlowChef](https://doi.org/10.1109/ICCV51701.2025.01420): Steers rectified-flow models for controlled visual generation.
- [KoDi](https://doi.org/10.1109/ACCESS.2025.3633798): Builds a Korean-English text-to-image diffusion model that targets bilingual prompting and culturally faithful generation.
- [HybridEditDif](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.patcog.2025.112510): Combines text and exemplar guidance for diffusion-based image editing.
- [FFTDiff](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.jvcir.2025.104681): Performs tuning-free image texture transfer with diffusion-model frequency-domain guidance.
- [Gain-Map Decomposed Diffusion](https://doi.org/10.1109/ICCV51701.2025.01629): Generates HDR images by decomposing gain maps within a diffusion-based image-generation pipeline.
- [Edge-Preserving Diffusion Editing](https://doi.org/10.1109/IoTAAI66837.2025.11212919): Adds edge-preserving smoothing to semantic diffusion editing to improve local edit control.
- [LatentPS](https://doi.org/10.1109/WACVW65960.2025.00023): Uses latent diffusion representations for image editing, fitting the model-side image-editing section.
- [The Safety Illusion?](https://doi.org/10.1109/TIP.2025.3620665): Tests limits of concept removal in diffusion models, clarifying when visual-generator safety controls fail to generalize.
- [Culture-based Adversarial Attack on Text-to-Image Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/ICME59968.2025.11209989): Studies culture-conditioned adversarial attacks against text-to-image models, adding a model-side robustness and safety stress test for visual generation.
- [Hidden Space Vector Coding for Image Steganography](https://doi.org/10.1109/TCE.2025.3616171): uses diffusion models for generative image steganography through hidden-space vector coding.
- [Cycle-Consistent Learning for Joint Layout-to-Image Generation and Object Detection](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccv51701.2025.00639): Couples layout-conditioned image generation with object detection through cycle-consistent learning, strengthening structured control in generated images.
- [SVGDreamer++: Advancing Editability and Diversity in Text-Guided SVG Generation](https://doi.org/10.1109/tpami.2025.3547889): Extends text-guided SVG generation with stronger editability and diversity, keeping generated graphics in an editable vector form.
- [Towards Universal AI-Generated Image Detection by Variational Information Bottleneck Network](https://doi.org/10.1109/cvpr52734.2025.02219): Uses a variational information bottleneck network to improve generalization in AI-generated image detection.
- [CLBA: A Cross-Lingual Backdoor Attack against Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/globecom59602.2025.11432651): Studies cross-lingual backdoor attacks against text-to-image diffusion models, adding a generated-image security line where malicious behavior can transfer across language prompts.
- [TrustMark: Robust Watermarking and Watermark Removal for Arbitrary Resolution Images](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccv51701.2025.01731): Studies robust watermark insertion and removal for arbitrary-resolution images, pairing provenance embedding with removal attacks in one evaluation target.
- [Towards provenance-aware diffusion: Key-free watermarking with Gaussian shading](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.eswa.2025.130822): Introduces PPGS, a key-free and fine-tuning-free diffusion watermark that embeds bits through proportion-preserved Gaussian sampling while keeping latent noise statistically Gaussian.
- [Deepfake Detection Leveraging Self-Blended Artifacts Guided by Facial Embedding Discrepancy](https://doi.org/10.1109/tcsvt.2025.3585724): Augments self-blended artifacts with face-recognition embedding discrepancies, letting the detector exploit identity-space inconsistencies as well as low-level forgery cues.
- [Revisiting face forgery detection towards generalization](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.neunet.2025.107310): Surveys generalizable face-forgery detection, organizing robustness to unknown forgeries and damaged low-quality inputs rather than proposing a new model.
- [ROAR: Reducing Inversion Error in Generative Image Watermarking](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccv51701.2025.01836): Reduces latent- and pixel-domain inversion errors in diffusion watermarking with regeneration-based latent optimization and a distortion-adaptive MoE restoration network.
- [CtrlMark: Controllable Watermarking for ControlNet Against Downstream Fine-Tuning](https://doi.org/10.1109/icpads67057.2025.11323086): Watermarks ControlNet plugins with a triggerable benign backdoor and localized latent residual embedding, preserving activation after downstream fine-tuning.
