- [Scalable Dual Fingerprinting for Hierarchical Attribution of Text-to-Image Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccv51701.2025.01394): Uses two FI-LoRA fingerprinting heads so developers can tag service providers and providers can embed consumer-specific provenance during T2I generation.
- [Simpler Diffusion: 1.5 FID on ImageNet512 with pixel-space diffusion](https://doi.org/10.1109/cvpr52734.2025.01683): Scales end-to-end pixel-space diffusion with sigmoid loss weighting, a memory-efficient architecture with fewer skip connections, and high-resolution compute allocation.
- [Diffusion Model is Effectively Its Own Teacher](https://doi.org/10.1109/cvpr52734.2025.01204): Introduces Self Step-Distillation, fusing a diffusion model's current-timestep predictions or activations with preceding timesteps to improve quality without an external teacher.
- [PlugMark: A Plug-In Zero-Watermarking Framework for Diffusion Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccv51701.2025.01610): Protects diffusion-model IP with a plug-in zero-watermarking framework that avoids modifying the sampling process and is designed to survive fine-tuning attacks.
- [Customized Transformer Adapter With Frequency Masking for Deepfake Detection](https://doi.org/10.1109/tifs.2025.3574983): Adds CUTA, a ViT adapter with frequency masking, to prevent deepfake detectors from overfitting dominant forgery regions or a single manipulation pattern.
- [Easy-editable Image Vectorization with Multi-layer Multi-scale Distributed Visual Feature Embedding](https://doi.org/10.1109/cvpr52734.2025.02174): Represents images with hierarchical proxy geometry and multi-scale control points, preserving internal texture structure during vector editing and rendering.
- [Generalization-Preserved Learning: Closing the Backdoor to Catastrophic Forgetting in Continual Deepfake Detection](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccv51701.2025.00362): Addresses continual deepfake detection with generalization-preserving updates so new generator artifacts can be learned without erasing older forgery cues.
- [TTP-AP: Test-time projection of augmented prototypes for generalized deepfake detection](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.knosys.2025.114710): Uses augmented prototypes and test-time projection to adapt deepfake detectors to unseen generators without retraining the full model.
- [A Flexible and Lightweight Watermarking Framework for Stable Diffusion Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/jiot.2025.3647881): Provides a lightweight Stable Diffusion watermarking framework that embeds provenance signals into the generation model with modest deployment overhead.
- [Robust watermarking for diffusion models using error-correcting codes and post-quantum key encapsulation](https://doi.org/10.3389/fphy.2025.1750515): Combines error-correcting codes with post-quantum key encapsulation to make diffusion-model watermarks more resilient to image edits and key-compromise risks.
- [Assessing AI-Generated Image Quality Using a Cross-Modal Hierarchical Perception Network](https://doi.org/10.1109/tbc.2025.3622417): Builds a cross-modal hierarchical perception network for rating AI-generated image quality, linking visual artifacts and semantic alignment in one quality model.
- [Character-Level Adversarial Samples Generation Method Based on Dual-Layer Text Watermark](https://doi.org/10.1109/access.2025.3594727): Generates character-level text adversarial samples through explicit and implicit dual-layer watermarks, making this a text-security item rather than image synthesis.
- [Decentralized Deepfake Detection: Feature-Aligned Federated Learning with Multi-Backbone Fusion](https://doi.org/10.1109/icscss64956.2025.11500806): Combines federated learning with Inception-v3 and Xception feature fusion so clients can train deepfake detectors without centralizing private media.
- [SC-Diffusion: Parameter Generation for Task-Oriented Semantic Communication Systems via Conditional Diffusion Model](https://doi.org/10.1109/tmlcn.2025.3618802): Uses conditional diffusion to generate parameters for task-oriented semantic communication systems, an off-axis wireless-communication application of diffusion.
- [Towards Deepfake Detection for Everyone: A Lightweight Deepfake Detection Algorithm (LiDD)](https://doi.org/10.1109/cai64502.2025.00293): Designs LiDD as a lightweight deepfake detector for modest devices, emphasizing deployable inference cost rather than maximum server-side accuracy.
- [Unveiling authenticity with diffusion-based face retouching reversal](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.asoc.2025.113062): Defines face retouching reversal, builds the 50,000-image deepFRR dataset, and uses FRRffusion with facial-structure restoration and detail generation to recover original facial appearance.
- [HybridNet: Advancing Deepfake Detection Through Residual, SE, and Depthwise Convolutions](https://doi.org/10.1109/access.2025.3640106): Combines residual blocks, depthwise separable convolutions, SE attention, HFSA, uncertainty thresholds, and Grad-CAM for efficient interpretable deepfake detection.
- [Representation Alignment For Deepfake Detection](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccai66501.2025.00109): Aligns detector representations with external semantic features so backbones capture both spatial attributes and frequency cues for cross-manipulation generalization.
- [Constrained Maximum Likelihood Gaussian Score Fusion for Multimodal Deepfake Detection](https://doi.org/10.1109/iwbf63717.2025.11113422): Calibrates multimodal deepfake detector scores with constrained maximum-likelihood Gaussian fusion, targeting trustworthy fused decisions rather than raw accuracy only.
- [Harnessing attention for cropping and fusion in CLIP-based AIGC detection](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.neucom.2025.132482): Repurposes CLIP for AIGC detection with Attention-Guided Saliency Cropping and FFN-only fine-tuning, preserving artifact-rich regions while avoiding attention overfitting.
- [SynTag: Enhancing the Geometric Robustness of Inversion-Based Generative Image Watermarking](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccv51701.2025.01430): Injects synchronization tags into inversion-based generative watermarks, improving robustness to geometric distortions where invariant watermark features are hard to find.
- [Hyperparameter Optimization for Deepfake Detection Improving CNN and Transformer Performance Using Bayesian Search](https://doi.org/10.1109/icmi65310.2025.11141060): Uses Optuna Bayesian search to tune CNN and Transformer deepfake detectors, comparing accuracy, F1, and AUC after dropout, learning-rate, and optimizer selection.
- [Diffusion-based Reinforcement Learning via LogSumExp-weighted Variational Policy Optimization](https://doi.org/10.1109/cac67268.2025.11487801): Introduces LSEVPO for online reinforcement learning with diffusion policies, making it a general RL method rather than a visual-generation entry.
- [FSD 2.0: Improved Fake Song Dataset for Unknown-domain Deepfake Detection](https://doi.org/10.1109/icmew68306.2025.11152048): Releases an expanded fake-song dataset for unknown-domain singing deepfake detection, which is audio-focused rather than image-generation work.
- [Cognitive Inspired Generalization Boosting for Face Forgery Detection](https://doi.org/10.1109/icme59968.2025.11209182): Uses a fast spatial-frequency transformer and a slow momentum memory bank in SFMM, borrowing dual-process cognition to improve face-forgery generalization.
- [CAPMamba: A hybrid vision model for enhancing generalization in deepfake detection](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.eswa.2025.130913): Combines Capsule Networks and MambaVision, using stable-face-region temporal scores to guide attention toward subtle spatio-temporal deepfake artifacts.
- [The Impact of Data Augmentation on Deepfake Detection: A Comparative Study of CNN and Transformer Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/icmi65310.2025.11141228): Compares augmentation choices across CNN and Transformer deepfake detectors on DFDC, showing how rotations, color jitter, noise, and erasing affect robustness metrics.
- [SecureGenAI: A Standardized Framework for Authentication and Provenance in AI-Generated Images using Blockchain-Enhanced Watermarking](https://doi.org/10.1109/ickecs65700.2025.11035329): Embeds DWT watermarks during image generation and anchors verification with blockchain provenance, targeting tamper-resistant authentication of generated images.
- [Omni Contextual Aggregation Networks for High-Fidelity Image Inpainting](https://doi.org/10.1109/tcsvt.2025.3532321): Proposes DOAM, a dynamic omni-attention mechanism that models spatial and channel interactions for high-fidelity image inpainting with lower attention bottlenecks.
- [SFSimNet: An Efficient Spatial-Frequency Multi-Scale Intra-Feature Similarity Measurement Network for Deepfake Detection](https://doi.org/10.1109/tce.2025.3580624): Measures intra-feature similarities across spatial and frequency scales, giving a low-complexity detector for face-synthesis discrepancies.
- [HSFF-Net: Hierarchical spectral-feature fusion network for deepfake detection and localization](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.neunet.2025.107967): Uses HSFF-Net to combine spatial and frequency-domain forgery cues, with spectral detail amplification, hierarchical dual-stream fusion, adaptive feature elevation, and localization guidance for both deepfake detection and tamper localization.
- [Font-Agent: Enhancing Font Understanding with Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/cvpr52734.2025.01832): Builds the 135K-pair Diversity Font Dataset and trains an LLM-based font-quality explainer, evaluating generated graphical fonts rather than generating natural images.
- [Open-Unfairness Adversarial Mitigation for Generalized Deepfake Detection](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccv51701.2025.00073): Addresses fairness bias in deepfake detection without assuming fixed demographic labels, targeting more reliable predictions for legal or identity-security settings.
- [Cascaded Autoregressive Diffusion Models for Remote Sensing Scene Generation](https://doi.org/10.1109/tgrs.2025.3622225): Generates remote-sensing scenes with a cascaded autoregressive diffusion framework that models object-background coupling and object distribution patterns.
- [NoiseCtrl: A Sampling-Algorithm-Agnostic Conditional Generation Method for Diffusion Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/cvpr52734.2025.01686): Steers conditional generation by adjusting the sampling noise term while leaving DDPM, DDIM, or other base samplers unchanged.
- [Solving ILL-posed Regions in High Dynamic Range Reconstruction With Uncertainty-Aware Diffusion Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/tcsvt.2025.3638674): Uses pixel-wise uncertainty maps and a wavelet-domain diffusion module to repair occluded or saturated HDR regions where ghosting artifacts persist.
- [Dual Frequency-Guided Spatiotemporal Feature Learning for Face Forgery Detection](https://doi.org/10.1109/tbiom.2025.3646181): Combines state-space temporal modeling with Fourier and wavelet prompts so video forgery detectors capture local and global frequency artifacts.
- [Visual Relation Diffusion for Human-Object Interaction Detection](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccv51701.2025.02186): Introduces VRDiff, using dense visual relation generation to model human-object interactions beyond pixel-level visual concept learning.
- [Images as Noisy Labels: Unleashing the Potential of the Diffusion Model for Open-Vocabulary Semantic Segmentation](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccv51701.2025.02248): Treats diffusion outputs as denoising supervision for open-vocabulary segmentation, compensating for CLIP's weak spatial dependency modeling.
- [Remote Sensing Image Generation via Object Text Decoupling](https://doi.org/10.1109/tpami.2025.3599520): Decouples object text from global captions in OTD-GAN, giving remote-sensing generation object-level control without fixed layout constraints.
- [Beyond Inserting: Learning Subject Embedding for Semantic-Fidelity Personalized Diffusion Generation](https://doi.org/10.1109/tcsvt.2025.3588882): Learns subject embeddings that preserve personalized subjects while keeping prompt interactions with scenes, actions, and attributes semantically faithful.
- [Autonomous language-image generation loops converge to generic visual motifs](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.patter.2025.101451): Shows SDXL-LLaVA text-image feedback loops collapse into a small set of generic motifs, diagnosing diversity loss in autonomous multimodal generation.
- [Aligning Normal Representations in Diffusion Model for Video Anomaly Detection](https://doi.org/10.1109/tcsvt.2025.3602841): Guides diffusion-based video anomaly detection with normal-specific representations, reducing shortcut correlations in low-level reconstruction.
- [FreqOR: Frequency-guided sampling initialization with attention enhancements for training-free object repositioning](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.cviu.2025.104610): Uses FreqOR for training-free object repositioning, suppressing high-frequency latent priors in vacated regions and adding RA-KV plus query-guided consistency for cleaner edits.
- [Refractory high-entropy alloys for solid-state hydrogen storage: A computational design review](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.ijhydene.2025.153291): Reviews computational design of refractory high-entropy alloys for solid-state hydrogen storage; this is an off-axis materials-design survey for the image-generation page.
- [Unlocking compositional potential: Leveraging specific feedback for text-to-image generation in diffusion models](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.eswa.2025.129443): Fine-tunes T2I diffusion with object-category and quantity feedback, using detector-derived confidence to build a differentiable matching-score reward and a new 11,337-pair compositional benchmark.
- [Enhancing Object-Attribute Alignment in Diffusion Models via Training-Free Contrastive Parallel Denoising](https://doi.org/10.1109/icme59968.2025.11209358): Uses training-free Contrastive Parallel Denoising, splitting prompts into sub-prompts and merging parallel latents with intra- and inter-object attribute losses plus cross-attention maps.
- [DRTNet: Diffusion Reconstruction Texture Network for AI-generated Image Detection](https://doi.org/10.1109/icme59968.2025.11209234): Builds DRTNet for AI-image detection by comparing an input image with Stable Diffusion reconstructions and extracting reconstruction-texture differences through a dual-branch detector.
- [Instance-wise distribution control of text-to-image diffusion models](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.patcog.2025.112614): Controls multi-instance T2I attribute distributions by fine-tuning diffusion models with object-detector and attribute-classifier guidance, matching generated instance proportions to user-defined distributions.
- [ArtisanFlow: Style Customization System for Illustration Generation](https://doi.org/10.1109/icmew68306.2025.11152131): Uses LoRA style customization for illustration generation, keeping compatibility with ControlNet and Layer Diffusion while evaluating style fidelity, semantic similarity, stability, and flexible composition.
- [UNICOM: Unified, foreground-aware, and context-realistic deep image composition with diffusion model](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.neucom.2025.131129): Unifies deep image composition without positional masks, using a background prompt, reference-image space mapping, and self-supervised triplets from segmentation data to adapt foreground pose and appearance to context.
- [A Dual Stream Visual Tokenizer for LLM Image Generation](https://doi.org/10.24963/ijcai.2025/167): Combines semantic tokens and low-level pixel tokens in a dual-stream visual tokenizer, pairing VQ-VAE guidance with diffusion reconstruction so LLM image generation keeps both content and structure.
- [Who Controls the Authorization? Invertible Networks for Copyright Protection in Text-to-Image Synthesis](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccv51701.2025.01469): Uses invertible networks for T2I copyright protection so authorization controls can be embedded and verified while defending against concealed personalized-generation infringement.
- [Reference-Based Super-Resolution via Image-Based Retrieval-Augmented Generation Diffusion](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccv51701.2025.01002): Proposes image-based RAG for reference super-resolution, retrieving real or generated reference patches with trainable hashing and filtering hallucinated diffusion references before restoration.
- [InferEdit: An instruction-based system with a multimodal LLM for complex multi-target image editing](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.visinf.2025.100265): Runs training-free complex image editing through an MLLM planner that decomposes multi-target instructions, localizes targets, and schedules object removal, replacement, or local-edit models via task-specific search trees.
- [PSFD: Proactive Spatial-Frequency Defense against Malicious Exemplar-Guided Image Editing](https://doi.org/10.1109/icme59968.2025.11209369): Defends exemplar-guided image editing with PSFD, adding spatial perturbations against visual encoders and frequency perturbations against high-frequency components to make protected images resist malicious edits.
- [Face forgery detection via identification of evident tampered regions and multi-view analysis](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.neucom.2025.132073): Uses RDNN to score face regions for tampering, trains with latent cue capture loss, and combines multi-view analyses to improve robust face-forgery detection.
- [FMambaIR: A Hybrid State-Space Model and Frequency Domain for Image Restoration](https://doi.org/10.1109/tgrs.2025.3526927): Combines Fourier-domain processing with Mamba state-space blocks in FMambaIR so image restoration models capture global degradation patterns and frequency details more effectively.
- [Diffusion Bridge: Leveraging Diffusion Model to Reduce the Modality Gap Between Text and Vision for Zero-Shot Image Captioning](https://doi.org/10.1109/cvpr52734.2025.00383): Trains a diffusion model over text embeddings to bridge CLIP vision-text modality gaps for zero-shot image captioning, turning paired visual embeddings into text-aligned representations.
- [Enhancing Perception for Autonomous Vehicles: A Multi-Scale Feature Modulation Network for Image Restoration](https://doi.org/10.1109/tits.2025.3538485): Designs MSFM for autonomous-driving image restoration, modulating multi-scale features in frequency and spatial domains to sharpen degraded images under adverse conditions.
- [Positive Enhanced Preference Alignment for Text-to-Image Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/icassp49660.2025.10890823): Revisits DPO for T2I alignment and proposes PEPA, strengthening positive-preference terms so preference tuning improves aesthetics and alignment without suppressing preferred samples.
- [Research on plug-and-play image restoration algorithm based on dual weighted ADMM](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.optlastec.2025.113997): Combines a multi-head self-attention dense U-Net denoiser with dual-weighted ADMM, dynamically balancing data fidelity and prior terms for plug-and-play denoising, deblurring, super-resolution, and deblocking.
- [Spatial-Aware Remote Sensing Image Generation From Spatial Relationship Descriptions](https://doi.org/10.1109/lgrs.2025.3542169): Generates remote-sensing images from spatial-relation descriptions by converting relations into controlled layouts, positional prompts, and layout attention inside an enhanced diffusion pipeline.
- [Learning Dynamic Prompts for All-in-One Image Restoration](https://doi.org/10.1109/tip.2025.3567205): Learns dynamic prompt representations for all-in-one image restoration, letting one restoration model adapt its behavior across degradation types instead of using fixed task prompts.
- [A Unified Framework for Context-Aware and Bias-Resilient Text-to-Image Generation](https://doi.org/10.1109/icoct64433.2025.11118789): Combines Stable Diffusion with CLIP-based context conditioning, toxicity filtering, inclusive prompt generation, and post-generation bias detection for fairer T2I outputs.
- [Personalized text-to-image generation with Large Language and Vision Assistant enhanced training](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.engappai.2025.112116): Introduces LLaVA-Booth for personalized T2I, using LLaVA-enhanced training, identity-binding loss, and prior preservation to reduce overbinding and language drift.
- [Zero-Shot Low-Light Image Enhancement via Joint Frequency Domain Priors Guided Diffusion](https://doi.org/10.1109/lsp.2025.3547269): Guides diffusion-based low-light enhancement with joint frequency-domain priors, targeting zero-shot recovery without task-specific paired retraining.
- [DiffDark: Multi-prior integration driven diffusion model for low-light image enhancement](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.patcog.2025.111814): Integrates multiple priors inside a diffusion model for low-light enhancement, using complementary restoration cues to recover details in dark images.
- [Data Bias Mitigation and Evaluation Framework for Diffusion-Based Generative Face Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccvw69036.2025.00037): Mitigates bias in diffusion face generators by adding KenyanFaceHQ and standardized evaluation procedures for underrepresented Black faces.
- [Aligning Text-to-Image Diffusion Models With Noise-Conditioned Perception](https://doi.org/10.1109/access.2025.3632092): Aligns T2I diffusion with noise-conditioned perceptual feedback, using perception signals at denoising noise levels rather than only final-image text alignment.
- [Semantically invariant conditioning of diffusion models: a unified framework for cross-model positive prompting](https://doi.org/10.21869/2223-1536-2025-15-4-35-49): Develops cross-model positive prompting for diffusion image generation, using semantic and linguistic invariants to improve prompt compliance across models.
- [Enhancing Visual Aesthetics in Stable Diffusion: A Reinforcement Learning Approach](https://doi.org/10.1109/smc58881.2025.11342753): Uses reinforcement learning to improve Stable Diffusion aesthetics, treating an image-aesthetic assessment model as the reward during denoising policy optimization.
- [Debias-DPO: Debiasing Diffusion-based Face Image Generation with Direct Preference Optimization](https://doi.org/10.1109/ijcb65343.2025.11411388): Applies DPO to debias diffusion-based face generation, giving higher preference to underrepresented social-attribute classes during alignment.
- [A Plug-and-Play Approach for Robust Image Editing in Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccvw69036.2025.00454): Uses residual linear interpolation inside self-attention as a plug-and-play inversion stabilizer for robust high-resolution T2I image editing.
- [Continuous alignment of multi-target preferences via instructed diffusion model](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.patcog.2025.112987): Aligns diffusion models to multiple target preferences using instructed preference signals.
- [Iterative Semantic Refinement: A Vision Language Model-Driven Approach to Auto-Regressive Image Editing](https://doi.org/10.1109/isas66241.2025.11101889): Drives auto-regressive image editing with iterative semantic refinement from vision-language models.
- [UDMMColor: A Unified Diffusion Model for Multi-Modal Colorization](https://doi.org/10.1109/tcsvt.2025.3643915): Adds a unified diffusion model for multimodal colorization.
- [Trustworthy Super-Resolution of Multispectral Sentinel-2 Imagery With Latent Diffusion](https://doi.org/10.1109/jstars.2025.3542220): Uses latent diffusion for Sentinel-2 multispectral super-resolution with trustworthy recovery constraints over spectral consistency and spatial detail.
- [Any Fashion Attribute Editing: Dataset and Pretrained Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/tpami.2025.3581793): Releases AFED with 830K sketch and product-domain fashion images for 78 fine-grained attributes, plus Twin-Net GAN inversion for high-fidelity attribute editing while preserving untouched components.
- [ActErase: A Training-Free Paradigm for Precise Concept Erasure via Activation Patching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.00267): Performs training-free concept erasure by locating activation-difference regions and redirecting target-concept activations, avoiding data-heavy fine-tuning of the T2I diffusion model.
- [Fusion-SSAT: Unleashing the Potential of Self-supervised Auxiliary Task by Feature Fusion for Generalized Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.00789): Fuses representations from self-supervised auxiliary tasks with deepfake classification features, improving cross-dataset generalization across DF40, FaceForensics++, Celeb-DF, and related benchmarks.
- [Luminark: Training-free, Probabilistically-Certified Watermarking for General Vision Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.01085): Defines a patch-level luminance-statistics watermark with controllable false-positive probability and injects it through plug-and-play guidance across vision generative models.
- [Mass Concept Erasure in Diffusion Models with Concept Hierarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.03305): Organizes erased concepts into supertype-subtype hierarchies and suppresses groups through shared parent concepts, scaling concept erasure without a separate adapter per target.
- [Detecting AI-Generated Images via Distributional Deviations from Real Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.03586): Analyzes distributional deviations in frozen CLIP-ViT features and turns those high-level real-image clusters into a detector for AI-generated images from unseen generators.
- [LinkD: AutoRegressive Diffusion Model for Mechanical Linkage Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.04054): Uses an autoregressive diffusion model for mechanical linkage synthesis, an off-axis mechanical-design generator rather than image generation or editing.
- [Aligning Text, Code, and Vision: A Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning Framework for Text-to-Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.04582): Optimizes text-to-visualization with multi-objective reinforcement learning over text, code, and vision.
- [On the Holistic Approach for Detecting Human Image Forgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.04715): Builds HuForDet as a holistic human-image forgery detector, covering facial manipulations and full-body synthetic imagery with dual-branch cues.
- [From Easy to Hard++: Promoting Differentially Private Image Synthesis Through Spatial-Frequency Curriculum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.06368): Adds privacy, forensic robustness, or detector-training methods for AI-generated image workflows.
- [Attack-Resistant Watermarking for AIGC Image Forensics via Diffusion-based Semantic Deflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.06639): Implements PAI as a training-free diffusion watermarking framework with semantic deflection, aiming to resist both spoofing and removal attacks while supporting semantic tamper localization.
- [CoF-T2I: Video Models as Pure Visual Reasoners for Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.10061): Uses video-model chain-of-frame reasoning as progressive visual refinement for text-to-image generation, making intermediate visual states part of the generation process.
- [Event Detection with a Context-Aware Encoder and LoRA for Improved Performance on Long-Tailed Classes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.11932): Uses a context-aware encoder and LoRA for long-tailed NLP event detection, which is off-axis for the image-generation model page.
- [S^2F-Net:A Robust Spatial-Spectral Fusion Framework for Cross-Model AIGC Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.12313): Fuses spatial and spectral evidence for AIGC image detection so the detector can generalize across generator families rather than overfitting to one model's artifacts.
- [PhaseMark: A Post-hoc, Optimization-Free Watermarking of AI-generated Images in the Latent Frequency Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.13128): Applies PhaseMark as a single-shot post-hoc watermark, directly modulating VAE latent-frequency phase so generated-image marks avoid iterative inversion or optimization.
- [LuxRemix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.15283): Decomposes and remixes indoor-scene lighting, adding controllable illumination editing for image and scene generation.
- [PF-D2M: A Pose-free Diffusion Model for Universal Dance-to-Music Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.15872): Generates music from dance-video visual features with progressive diffusion training, making it an off-axis video-to-music generation item rather than an image model.
- [Scaling Text-to-Image Diffusion Transformers with Representation Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.16208): Scales text-to-image diffusion Transformers with representation autoencoders, improving the latent space used for generation.
- [ResAgent: Entropy-based Prior Point Discovery and Visual Reasoning for Referring Expression Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.16394): Discovers prior points with entropy-based reasoning for referring-expression segmentation.
- [DeMark: A Query-Free Black-Box Attack on Deepfake Watermarking Defenses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.16473): Removes defensive deepfake watermarks with a query-free black-box attack that exploits encoder-decoder latent vulnerabilities through compressive-sensing sparsification while preserving visual realism.
- [Edge-Aware Image Manipulation via Diffusion Models with a Novel Structure-Preservation Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.16645): Adds a training-free Structure Preservation Loss during latent-diffusion editing, combining edge fidelity, masking, color preservation, and post-processing to keep pixel-level structure intact.
- [Revealing the Truth with ConLLM for Detecting Multi-Modal Deepfakes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.17530): Uses a contrastive LLM-style multimodal detector to reason over cross-modal inconsistency signals in deepfake images and associated content.
- [Adaptive Domain Shift in Diffusion Models for Cross-Modality Image Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.18623): Adapts diffusion trajectories across source and target modalities for image-to-image translation, treating domain shift as a controllable component of the denoising process.
- [SemBind: Binding Diffusion Watermarks to Semantics Against Black-Box Forgery Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.20310): Binds latent diffusion watermarks to image semantics with SemBind, defending provenance signals against black-box forgery attacks that try to copy provider watermarks onto unrelated images.
- [Non-Markov Multi-Round Conversational Image Generation with History-Conditioned MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.20911): Formalizes non-Markov conversational image generation, where edits can refer to earlier images, undo states, and long-range entities, and supplies history-conditioned training and evaluation protocols.
- [PHDME: Physics-Informed Diffusion Models without Explicit Governing Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21234): Uses a port-Hamiltonian diffusion framework for sparse-observation dynamical-system forecasting without explicit governing equations, a scientific-modeling boundary item.
- [SimGraph: A Unified Framework for Scene Graph-Based Image Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21498): Unifies scene-graph-based image generation and editing with SimGraph, using object-relation structure to preserve spatial consistency and semantic coherence.
- [HERS: Hidden-Pattern Expert Learning for Risk-Specific Vehicle Damage Adaptation in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21517): Adapts diffusion generation for vehicle-damage imagery with hidden-pattern expert learning, targeting risk-specific synthetic damage data rather than generic image synthesis.
- [PI-Light: Physics-Inspired Diffusion for Full-Image Relighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22135): Uses a two-stage physics-inspired diffusion framework with batch-aware attention and a physics-guided neural renderer to improve full-scene relighting plausibility and generalization.
- [Pixel Mean Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22158): generates images in one step without a latent bottleneck by using pixel mean flows.
- [DreamVAR: Taming Reinforced Visual Autoregressive Model for High-Fidelity Subject-Driven Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22507): Builds DreamVAR on next-scale visual autoregression for subject-driven image generation, adding multi-scale identity conditioning for high-fidelity personalized synthesis.
- [Particle-Guided Diffusion Models for Partial Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23262): Adds physics-based residual and observation guidance to diffusion sampling within a Sequential Monte Carlo framework, making the generated PDE solutions obey physical constraints.
- [The Illusion of Forgetting: Attack Unlearned Diffusion via Initial Latent Variable Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.00175): Attacks concept-erased text-to-image diffusion models by optimizing initial latents to revive dormant memories, stress-testing the robustness of visual unlearning methods.
- [Diff-PC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.00639): Adds identity-preserving and 3D-aware controls for zero-shot portrait customization with diffusion models.
- [MarkCleaner: High-Fidelity Watermark Removal via Imperceptible Micro-Geometric Perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.01513): Shows semantic watermarks can fail under micro-geometric perturbations and trains a mask-guided encoder plus 2D Gaussian Splatting decoder to remove marks without semantic drift.
- [FlowBypass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.01805): Bypasses parts of rectified-flow trajectories to make training-free image editing faster while retaining fidelity.
- [Fact or Fake? Assessing the Role of Deepfake Detectors in Multimodal Misinformation Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.01854): Tests whether image-only deepfake detectors help verify image-text misinformation claims on MMFakeBench and DGM4, making it closer to benchmark analysis than generator modeling.
- [From Tokens to Numbers: Continuous Number Modeling for SVG Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02820): Models SVG numerical parameters as continuous values rather than token strings, improving vector-graphics generation efficiency and generalization.
- [Unifying Watermarking via Dimension-Aware Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.03373): Unifies deep watermarking as dimension-aware mapping, handling binary payloads, spatial masks, and attribution signals within one encoder-decoder formulation.
- [Light Up Your Face](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.04300): Builds a physically consistent dataset and diffusion model for face fill-light enhancement, adding a lighting-control case to generative image editing.
- [Semantic-level Backdoor Attack against Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.04898): Implants continuous semantic-region triggers by distillation-editing cross-attention key and value projections, making semantically equivalent prompts activate T2I backdoors.
- [SSG: Scaled Spatial Guidance for Multi-Scale Visual Autoregressive Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.05534): Adds training-free scaled spatial guidance for VAR models, emphasizing high-frequency semantic residuals at each scale to reduce coarse-to-fine inference drift.
- [ShapeGaussian: High-Fidelity 4D Human Reconstruction in Monocular Videos via Vision Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.05572): Uses ShapeGaussian for template-free 4D human reconstruction from monocular videos, relying on vision priors instead of SMPL templates.
- [FlexID: Training-Free Flexible Identity Injection via Intent-Aware Modulation for Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.07554): Uses FlexID for tuning-free identity injection, separating semantic identity projection from detail modulation so personalized T2I can balance identity fidelity and prompt adaptability.
- [Effects-Sensitive In-Context Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.08388): Improves geometric image editing by using effects-sensitive in-context inpainting with diffusion transformers.
- [FusionEdit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.08725): Combines semantic fusion with attention modulation for mask-free training-free image editing.
- [Fine-T2I: An Open, Large-Scale, and Diverse Dataset for High-Quality T2I Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.09439): Releases a 6M-pair open T2I fine-tuning dataset spanning task combinations, prompt categories, visual styles, and templates after strict alignment and fidelity filtering.
- [ELROND: Exploring and decomposing intrinsic capabilities of diffusion models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10216): Disentangles intrinsic diffusion-model capabilities in embedding space, giving users direct semantic directions for variation control rather than relying only on random seeds.
- [3DXTalker: Unifying Identity, Lip Sync, Emotion, and Spatial Dynamics in Expressive 3D Talking Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10516): Unifies identity, lip sync, emotion, and spatial dynamics for 3D talking avatars through a controllable audio-driven generation framework.
- [Beyond VLM-Based Rewards: Diffusion-Native Latent Reward Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11146): Introduces diffusion-native latent reward modeling for preference optimization of image generators.
- [Latent Forcing: Reordering the Diffusion Trajectory for Pixel-Space Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11401): Reorders pixel-space diffusion trajectories with latent forcing so key steps better control global structure and fine detail.
- [ImageRAGTurbo: Towards One-step Text-to-Image Generation with Retrieval-Augmented Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12640): Uses retrieval-augmented diffusion to move text-to-image generation toward one-step synthesis.
- [Inject Where It Matters: Training-Free Spatially-Adaptive Identity Preservation for Text-to-Image Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.13994): Uses SpatialID to inject identity features only where needed, avoiding uniform visual injection that contaminates background, lighting, and other non-face regions in personalized T2I.
- [When Test-Time Guidance Is Enough: Fast Image and Video Editing with Diffusion Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14157): Shows that VJP-free test-time diffusion guidance can solve masked image and video editing as inpainting, matching training-based editors without retraining.
- [MarkSweep: A No-box Removal Attack on AI-Generated Image Watermarking via Noise Intensification and Frequency-aware Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15364): Removes invisible AI-image watermarks without model access by amplifying high-frequency watermark noise, then training a frequency-aware denoiser to suppress the traces.
- [Style-Aware Gloss Control for Generative Non-Photorealistic Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.16611): Controls gloss in generative non-photorealistic rendering, separating material appearance from artistic style so generated objects can vary surface sheen.
- [DesignAsCode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.17690): Represents graphic designs as editable code-like structures to balance visual fidelity with fine-grained controllability.
- [Narrating For You: Prompt-guided Audio-visual Narrating Face Generation Employing Multi-entangled Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18618): Generates narrating faces from a static image, voice profile, and target text through a multi-entangled latent space shared by audio and video modalities.
- [ReVision : A Post-Hoc, Vision-Based Technique for Replacing Unacceptable Concepts in Image Generation Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19149): Adds a post-hoc vision-based replacement stage that detects unacceptable generated concepts and substitutes safer visual content inside the image-generation pipeline.
- [Localized Concept Erasure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19631): Erases localized concepts in text-to-image diffusion models through high-level representation misdirection.
- [When Safety Collides: Resolving Multi-Category Harmful Conflicts in Text-to-Image Diffusion via Adaptive Safety Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.20880): Uses adaptive safety guidance to resolve multi-category harmful conflicts in text-to-image diffusion.
- [See and Fix the Flaws: Enabling VLMs and Diffusion Models to Comprehend Visual Artifacts via Agentic Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.20951): Uses agentic data synthesis so VLMs and diffusion models can identify and fix visual artifacts.
- [Sharp Convergence Rates for Masked Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22505): Provides convergence analysis for masked diffusion samplers, comparing Euler-style sampling and first-hitting samplers for discrete diffusion rather than proposing a new image generator.
- [Beyond Detection: Multi-Scale Hidden-Code for Natural Image Deepfake Recovery and Factual Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22759): Embeds multi-scale hidden codes so deepfake images can support recovery and factual retrieval, extending provenance beyond binary generated-image detection.
- [DesignSense: A Human Preference Dataset and Reward Modeling Framework for Graphic Layout Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23438): Provides human preference data and reward modeling for graphic layout generation.
- [All in One: Unifying Deepfake Detection, Tampering Localization, and Source Tracing with a Robust Landmark-Identity Watermark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23523): Unifies proactive deepfake detection, tamper localization, and source tracing with a landmark-identity watermark designed for face manipulation forensics.
- [A Difference-in-Difference Approach to Detecting AI-Generated Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23732): Detects AI-generated images with a difference-in-difference strategy, comparing reconstruction-error changes rather than relying on raw reconstruction error alone.
- [SenCache: Accelerating Diffusion Model Inference via Sensitivity-Aware Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.24208): Caches diffusion features according to layer and timestep sensitivity, accelerating image generation while avoiding quality loss from indiscriminate reuse.
- [Compensation-free Machine Unlearning in Text-to-Image Diffusion Models by Eliminating the Mutual Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00992): Frames T2I concept erasure as eliminating mutual information between target concepts and generations, aiming to preserve unrelated image quality without compensation data.
- [RA-Det: Towards Universal Detection of AI-Generated Images via Robustness Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01544): Detects AI-generated images through robustness asymmetry, exploiting the different response patterns of real and synthetic images under perturbations.
- [Domain-Aware Task Prompt Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01725): Learns task prompts for multi-domain all-in-one image restoration, extending prompt-conditioned restoration models.
- [CAWM-Mamba: A unified model for infrared-visible image fusion and compound adverse weather restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02560): Adds a unified image-fusion and adverse-weather restoration model.
- [Conditioned Activation Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03163): An inference-time T2I safety steering method that learns nonlinear activation-transport maps from contrastive safe and unsafe prompts.
- [On Google's SynthID-Text LLM Watermarking System: Theoretical Analysis and Empirical Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03410): Analyzes SynthID-Text's tournament sampling and Bayesian or mean-score detection, including robustness limits and layer-inflation attacks, so it is off-axis for image-generation models.
- [Linearized Coupling Flow with Shortcut Constraints for One-Step Face Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03648): Uses shortcut-constrained linearized coupling flow to restore faces in one step, targeting fast high-fidelity recovery rather than iterative diffusion sampling.
- [Towards High-resolution and Disentangled Reference-based Sketch Colorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05971): Separates reference appearance from sketch structure so high-resolution colorization can transfer style cues while preserving the input line drawing.
- [HY-WU (Part I): An Extensible Functional Neural Memory Framework and An Instantiation in Text-Guided Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07236): Introduces a functional neural memory framework that stores and retrieves instance-specific parameter functions, instantiated for text-guided image editing under changing user objectives.
- [How Long Can Unified Multimodal Models Generate Images Reliably? Taming Long-Horizon Interleaved Image Generation via Context Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07540): Uses context curation to improve reliability when unified multimodal models generate long interleaved image sequences.
- [CONSTANT: Towards High-Quality One-Shot Handwriting Generation with Patch Contrastive Enhancement and Style-Aware Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07543): Combines patch-contrastive enhancement with style-aware quantization to synthesize handwriting from one reference, preserving writer style under scarce exemplars.
- [LooComp: Leverage Leave-One-Out Strategy to Encoder-only Transformer for Efficient Query-aware Context Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09222): Compresses long query-aware context with a leave-one-out encoder-only transformer; this appears off-axis for visual generation and should be reviewed outside this page.
- [When Detectors Forget Forensics: Blocking Semantic Shortcuts for Generalizable AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09242): Adds privacy, forensic robustness, or detector-training methods for AI-generated image workflows.
- [ShapeMark: Robust and Diversity-Preserving Watermarking for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09454): Embeds robust watermarks in diffusion outputs while preserving sample diversity, aiming to keep provenance signals without collapsing generated-image variation.
- [Towards Unified Multimodal Interleaved Generation via Group Relative Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09538): Applies group relative policy optimization to interleaved multimodal generation, connecting reinforcement learning with image-text generation workflows.
- [OrthoEraser: Coupled-Neuron Orthogonal Projection for Concept Erasure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11493): Uses coupled-neuron orthogonal projection for concept erasure, extending model-side safety and controllability methods for visual generators.
- [Derain-Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11866): Adds a planning network that schedules restoration tools for each rainy image and a strength-modulation mechanism that applies those tools with spatially adaptive intensity to fix residual rain, noise, blur, and color artifacts.
- [Safety-Guided Flow (SGF): A Unified Framework for Negative Guidance in Safe Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13300): Unifies negative guidance through an MMD potential and control-barrier interpretation, steering diffusion or flow samples away from unsafe regions during generation.
- [CtrlAttack: A Unified Attack on World-Model Control in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13435): Studies attacks on diffusion world-model control, exposing how control signals can be manipulated in generative dynamics rather than only in final rendered images.
- [FreeTalk: Emotional Topology-Free 3D Talking Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15512): Generates emotional 3D talking heads without relying on a fixed topology, improving expression control and identity transfer for talking-face synthesis.
- [Latent Proxies for Relighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15555): Learns latent proxies for controllable single-image relighting, adding an efficient lighting-control handle for image editing.
- [PaAgent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17055): Trains a portrait-aware image-restoration agent with subjective-objective reinforcement learning, adding an RL-based restoration model line.
- [EvoGuard: An Extensible Agentic RL-based Framework for Practical and Evolving AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17343): Wraps multiple MLLM and non-MLLM AI-image detectors as tools and uses capability-aware planning, reflection, and RL to adapt detection decisions in evolving environments.
- [Proof-of-Authorship for Diffusion-based AI Generated Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17513): Designs authorship proof for diffusion-generated content, adding provenance verification beyond ordinary image watermark detection.
- [Rel-Zero: Harnessing Patch-Pair Invariance for Robust Zero-Watermarking Against AI Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17531): Uses patch-pair invariance for zero-watermarking so attribution survives AI editing without embedding a visible or model-internal payload.
- [DSS-GAN: Directional State Space GAN with Mamba backbone for Class-Conditional Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17637): Builds a class-conditional image generator with a directional state-space GAN and Mamba backbone, offering an alternative to diffusion-heavy synthesis architectures.
- [TINA: Text-Free Inversion Attack for Unlearned Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17828): Probes unlearned text-to-image diffusion models with text-free inversion attacks, exposing residual unsafe concepts that remain reachable outside ordinary prompt space.
- [The Unreasonable Effectiveness of Text Embedding Interpolation for Continuous Image Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17998): Shows that interpolating text embeddings can provide continuous steering controls for generated images without retraining the diffusion model.
- [One-to-More: High-Fidelity Training-Free Anomaly Generation with Attention Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18093): Uses attention control to turn one normal sample into multiple high-fidelity anomaly variants without training a new generator, supporting synthetic defect data creation.
- [Recolour What Matters: Region-Aware Colour Editing via Token-Level Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18466): Performs region-aware color edits through token-level diffusion control, changing target areas while preserving unrelated image content.
- [CRAFT: Aligning Diffusion Models with Fine-Tuning Is Easier Than You Think](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18991): Fine-tunes diffusion models for alignment with a simplified recipe, focusing on practical reward or preference steering rather than new backbone design.
- [Under One Sun](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19226): Models materials and illumination jointly across multiple objects, improving physically grounded visual editing and rendering control.
- [Cubic Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19232): Generates high-dimensional visual representation tokens with discrete diffusion, bridging visual understanding features and generation.
- [DiffGraph: An Automated Agent-driven Model Merging Framework for In-the-Wild Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20470): Automates text-to-image model merging as a model-composition method for improving in-the-wild generation behavior.
- [SLURP-TN : Resource for Tunisian Dialect Spoken Language Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21940): Releases a Tunisian dialect spoken-language-understanding resource; this is off-axis for image generation and should be removed or moved during taxonomy cleanup.
- [Group Editing: Edit Multiple Images in One Go](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22883): Builds explicit VGGT geometric correspondences and implicit pseudo-video temporal priors so related images can receive semantically aligned edits in one operation.
- [AgentFoX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23115): uses an LLM-agent-guided fusion and explanation workflow for AI-generated image detection, linking provenance detection with agentic reasoning.
- [High-Fidelity Face Content Recovery via Tamper-Resilient Versatile Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23940): Designs a tamper-resilient face watermark that can recover high-fidelity identity content after manipulations, supporting provenance and repair for generated or edited faces.
- [SemLayer: Semantic-aware Generative Segmentation and Layer Construction for Abstract Icons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24039): Generates semantic layers for abstract icons by combining segmentation and layer construction, supporting editable vector-like decomposition of generated graphics.
- [ScrollScape: Unlocking 32K Image Generation With Video Diffusion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24270): Reformulates extreme-aspect-ratio 32K image synthesis as continuous video generation, using video-diffusion temporal priors as long-range spatial constraints.
- [Self-Corrected Image Generation with Explainable Latent Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24965): Uses explainable latent rewards to guide self-correction in image generation models.
- [PAD-Hand: Physics-Aware Diffusion for Hand Motion Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26068): Adds physics constraints to diffusion-based hand motion recovery, improving plausible articulated hand animation from visual or motion cues.
- [TaxaAdapter: Vision Taxonomy Models are Key to Fine-grained Image Generation over the Tree of Life](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26128): Adapts fine-grained biological taxonomy knowledge for tree-of-life image generation, using taxonomy models to control species-level visual details.
- [LightMover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27209): Controls generated light movement, color, and intensity, adding an editable lighting-control primitive for visual generation.
- [Understanding Semantic Perturbations on In-Processing Generative Image Watermarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27513): Analyzes how semantic perturbations affect watermarks embedded during generation, clarifying the robustness limits of in-processing watermark mechanisms.
- [Diversity Matters: Dataset Diversification and Dual-Branch Network for Generalized AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27800): Combines dataset diversification with a dual-branch network for generalized AI-generated image detection.
- [Rethinking Structure Preservation in Text-Guided Image Editing with Visual Autoregressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28367): Studies structure preservation for text-guided image editing with visual autoregressive models.
- [GazeCLIP: Gaze-Guided CLIP with Adaptive-Enhanced Fine-Grained Language Prompt for Deepfake Attribution and Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29295): Guides CLIP-style deepfake attribution with gaze cues and adaptive fine-grained language prompts, targeting localized manipulation evidence.
- [SafeRoPE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01826): Applies risk-specific head-wise embedding rotations to steer rectified-flow transformers away from unsafe image generation while preserving quality.
- [IPSL-AID: Generative Diffusion Models for Climate Downscaling from Global to Regional Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03275): Applies generative diffusion to climate downscaling from global to regional simulations; it is a scientific surrogate-model item rather than visual image generation.
- [VicoEdit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04487): Performs training-free image editing by integrating visual context and concept alignment.
- [MAR-GRPO: Stabilized GRPO for AR-diffusion Hybrid Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06966): Stabilizes GRPO for masked autoregressive and diffusion hybrid image generators by reducing noisy diffusion-head optimization signals.
- [MegaStyle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08364): constructs a large style dataset and trains style encoders or transfer models through consistent text-to-image style mapping.
- [What Matters in Virtual Try-Off? Dual-UNet Diffusion Model For Garment Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08716): Uses a dual-UNet diffusion model to reconstruct garments for virtual try-off, separating clothing recovery from the person image.
- [BlendFusion -- Scalable Synthetic Data Generation for Diffusion Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09022): Scales synthetic-data generation for diffusion training, blending generated sources to improve downstream visual generator training coverage.
- [Diffusion Inpainting MIMO-OFDM Channels with Limited Noisy Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09039): Uses diffusion inpainting for wireless MIMO-OFDM channel recovery, an off-axis signal-processing use of diffusion rather than image editing.
- [Detecting Diffusion-generated Images via Dynamic Assembly Forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09106): Uses dynamic assembly forests to classify diffusion-generated images, aggregating multiple artifact cues instead of relying on a single fixed detector head.
- [EGLOCE: Training-Free Energy-Guided Latent Optimization for Concept Erasure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09405): Erases concepts with training-free energy-guided latent optimization, steering diffusion latents away from unwanted semantics at inference time.
- [Training-Free Object-Background Compositional T2I via Dynamic Spatial Guidance and Multi-Path Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09850): Improves object-background compositional text-to-image generation with dynamic spatial guidance and multi-path pruning.
- [AIM-Bench: Benchmarking and Improving Affective Image Manipulation via Fine-Grained Hierarchical Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10454): Combines an affective-image-manipulation benchmark with fine-grained hierarchical control methods; the benchmark component should be checked for Bench placement.
- [TimeMark: A Trustworthy Time Watermarking Framework for Exact Generation-Time Recovery from AIGC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12216): Encodes generation-time information in AIGC watermarks so provenance checks can recover when an image was generated, not just whether it is synthetic.
- [Fragile Reconstruction: Adversarial Vulnerability of Reconstruction-Based Detectors for Diffusion-Generated Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12781): Studies adversarial weaknesses in reconstruction-based diffusion-image detectors, showing when detector reliance on reconstruction error can be broken.
- [PostureObjectstitch: Anomaly Image Generation Considering Assembly Relationships in Industrial Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13863): Generates industrial anomaly images while respecting assembly relationships, making synthetic defects more physically plausible for inspection data.
- [Beyond Text Prompts: Precise Concept Erasure through Text-Image Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15829): Uses TICoE's text-image collaborative erasing, continuous convex concept manifolds, and hierarchical visual representations for precise concept removal with less over-erasure.
- [Unveiling Deepfakes: A Frequency-Aware Triple Branch Network for Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17477): Detects deepfakes with a frequency-aware triple-branch network that separates complementary artifact cues across spectral views.
- [Edit Fidelity Field: Semantics-Aware Region Isolation for Training-Free Scene Text Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17500): Isolates editable text regions with a semantics-aware fidelity field, enabling training-free scene-text edits that protect surrounding image content.
- [UniGeo: Unifying Geometric Guidance for Camera-Controllable Image Editing via Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17565): Uses video-model geometric guidance for camera-controllable image editing.
- [AdaGScale: Viewpoint-Adaptive Gaussian Scaling in 3D Gaussian Splatting to Reduce Gaussian-Tile Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18980): Scales 3D Gaussians by viewpoint to reduce Gaussian-tile pairs, improving rendering efficiency rather than adding a 2D image-editing method.
- [Discrete Preference Learning for Personalized Multimodal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20434): Learns discrete preferences for personalized multimodal generation, turning user-specific choices into controllable generation signals.
- [GeoEdit: Local Frames for Fast, Training-Free On-Manifold Editing in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24238): Uses local manifold frames for fast training-free diffusion editing, keeping edits on the learned image manifold while avoiding fine-tuning.
- [TextGround4M: A Prompt-Aligned Dataset for Layout-Aware Text Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24459): Provides 4M prompt-image pairs with span-level text boxes and trains autoregressive T2I models with layout-aware span tokens for grounded scene-text rendering.
- [Learning Illumination Control in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24877): Learns explicit illumination controls for diffusion models so generated or edited images can vary lighting direction and intensity.
- [The Forensic Cost of Watermark Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25491): Measures forensic traces left after watermark removal, showing that erasing visible attribution does not necessarily erase detector evidence.
- [ACPO: Anchor-Constrained Perceptual Optimization for Diffusion Models with No-Reference Quality Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26348): Optimizes diffusion outputs with no-reference quality guidance while constraining updates around anchors to avoid perceptual drift.
- [Delta Score Matters! Spatial Adaptive Multi Guidance in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26503): Replaces globally uniform classifier-free guidance with spatially adaptive multi-guidance, using delta scores to vary guidance strength across image regions and reduce artifacts.
- [Frequency-Aware Semantic Fusion with Gated Injection for AI-generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27875): Adds privacy, forensic robustness, or detector-training methods for AI-generated image workflows.
- [STARE: Step-wise Temporal Alignment and Red-teaming Engine for Multi-modal Toxicity Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00699): A model-side contribution for image generation and editing models; core idea: Step-wise Temporal Alignment and Red-teaming Engine for Multi-modal Toxicity Attack.
- [PhysEdit: Physically-Consistent Region-Aware Image Editing via Adaptive Spatio-Temporal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00707): Performs region-aware edits with adaptive spatial and temporal reasoning so image modifications remain physically consistent with scene structure.
- [Watch Your Step: Information Injection in Diffusion Models via Shadow Timestep Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00935): Injects information through shadow timestep embeddings, adding a timestep-level control channel inside diffusion denoising.
- [ScribbleEdit: Synthetic Data for Image Editing with Scribbles and Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01135): Generates synthetic edit data pairing scribbles and text instructions, targeting controllable image-editing training signals.
- [SpecEdit: Training-Free Acceleration for Diffusion based Image Editing via Semantic Locking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02152): Accelerates diffusion editing without training by semantically locking unchanged content, reducing unnecessary denoising updates outside target regions.
- [FEAT: Fashion Editing and Try-On from Any Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02393): Edits fashion images and virtual try-on outputs from arbitrary design inputs, transferring garment design cues into person images.
- [Stylistic Attribute Control in Latent Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02583): Controls stylistic attributes in latent diffusion by exposing style-specific directions or conditioning signals rather than relying only on prompt wording.
- [LEGO: LoRA-Enabled Generator-Oriented Framework for Synthetic Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04445): Adds privacy, forensic robustness, or detector-training methods for AI-generated image workflows.
- [Arena as Offline Reward: Efficient Fine-Grained Preference Optimization for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06070): Converts arena-style preference data into offline fine-grained rewards for diffusion preference optimization, avoiding repeated online human comparison during training.
- [Consistency Regularised Gradient Flows for Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07907): Uses vision-language latent diffusion priors and prompt optimization in a gradient-flow framework for inverse-problem solving.
- [Improving Generative Adversarial Networks with Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08577): Uses self-distillation to improve GAN training, transferring stronger generator behavior into later training stages without changing the task setup.
- [FlashClear: Ultra-Fast Image Content Removal via Efficient Step Distillation and Feature Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09003): Distills image content removal into fewer denoising steps and reuses cached features, targeting low-latency object removal with diffusion-quality edits.
- [Removing the Watermark Is Not Enough: Forensic Stealth in Generative-AI Watermark Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09203): Studies watermark removal that also hides residual forensic traces, distinguishing simple watermark erasure from stealth against generated-image detectors.
- [Geometric 4D Stitching for Grounded 4D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09984): Stitches geometry across time for grounded 4D generation, improving spatial and temporal consistency in generated dynamic scenes.
- [ZeroIDIR: Zero-Reference Illumination Degradation Image Restoration with Perturbed Consistency Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11435): Restores illumination-degraded images without references by perturbing consistency diffusion models, targeting low-light or illumination-corrupted inputs.
- [Is Monotonic Sampling Necessary in Diffusion Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11773): Questions monotonic timestep ordering in diffusion sampling and studies whether non-monotonic schedules can improve efficiency or quality.
- [SenseNova-U1: Unifying Multimodal Understanding and Generation with NEO-unify Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12500): Unifies multimodal understanding and generation with the NEO-unify architecture, addressing fragmented VLM generation and comprehension pipelines.
- [Seg-Agent: Test-Time Multimodal Reasoning for Training-Free Language-Guided Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12953): Uses test-time multimodal reasoning for training-free language-guided segmentation.
- [Engram in AR Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13179): tests whether Engram-style associative memory transfers to autoregressive image generation, clarifying memory-module effects in visual generators.
- [Covariance-aware sampling for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13910): Incorporates covariance information into diffusion sampling, refining denoising trajectories beyond scalar noise schedules.
- [CoReDiT: Spatial Coherence-Guided Token Pruning and Reconstruction for Efficient Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14191): Prunes spatially redundant tokens in diffusion transformers and reconstructs skipped attention outputs with coherence-guided aggregation, reducing attention FLOPs for image and video generation backbones.
- [Watermarking Game-Playing Agents in Perfect-Information Extensive-Form Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14283): Watermarks game-playing agents in extensive-form games; it is an agent/game-theory provenance item rather than image generation or editing.
- [ClickRemoval: An Interactive Open-Source Tool for Object Removal in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14461): Provides a click-driven diffusion editing interface for object removal, combining user-selected regions with model-side inpainting behavior.
- [VAGS: Velocity Adaptive Guidance Scale for Image Editing and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15661): Adjusts diffusion guidance scale according to denoising velocity, improving the balance between edit strength and image fidelity.
- [Partial-Sketch Diffusion Editing](https://doi.org/10.1109/TIP.2026.3685834): Conditions diffusion-based image manipulation on partial sketches, adding a controllable editing method for sketch-guided visual changes.
- [SegEdit](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.eswa.2026.131222): Combines semantic mask segmentation with shape injection inside diffusion models for controlled image editing.
- [HierLoRA](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.neucom.2026.132927): Uses hierarchical enhanced LoRA to learn multiple personalized concepts in image diffusion models.
- [Attribute-Conditioned Attention Scaling](https://doi.org/10.14569/ijacsa.2026.0170472): Controls text-to-image diffusion outputs by scaling attention according to desired visual attributes.
- [TrojanEdit: Multimodal backdoor attack against image editing model](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.neucom.2026.133346): Studies multimodal backdoor attacks against image editing models, adding a security and robustness line for instruction-guided visual editing systems.
- [Training-Free Layered Framework for Geometry-Aware Multilingual Text Editing](https://doi.org/10.1109/icassp55912.2026.11464805): Provides a training-free layered framework for multilingual text editing with geometric constraints, adding an image-editing route for text-rich visual content.
- [TraceMark-LDM: Authenticatable watermarking for latent diffusion models via binary-guided rearrangement](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.eswa.2026.131831): Rearranges latent diffusion representations under binary guidance to create authenticatable watermarks for generated images.
- [Component-Specific Prompt Tuning for Deepfake Detection](https://doi.org/10.1109/tifs.2026.3678021): Tunes component-specific prompts for deepfake detection, using prompt specialization to expose manipulation artifacts in different facial or image regions.
- [Focus on Finding Deepfakes: A Robust Proactive Detection Method Based on Orthogonal Moment Watermarking](https://doi.org/10.1109/tip.2026.3678069): Uses orthogonal-moment watermarking as a proactive signal for robust deepfake detection and attribution.
- [RWP: a robust watermarking plugin for attribution and protection in stable diffusion models](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.neunet.2026.108626): Adds a plug-in watermarking module to Stable Diffusion for attribution and protection while preserving the base generator workflow.
- [M 2 EA: Multi-VAE Manifold Envelope Alignment for Challenging AI-Generated Image Detection](https://doi.org/10.1109/icassp55912.2026.11463215): Aligns multi-VAE manifold envelopes to detect challenging AI-generated images whose artifacts are not captured by a single representation.
- [RI-Mark: Robust and Imperceptible Watermarking for Diffusion Models](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.eswa.2026.132903): Designs robust imperceptible watermarks for diffusion models, targeting provenance signals that survive common image transformations.
- [KBNet: A Knowledge Bridging Network for Generalizable Deepfake Detection](https://doi.org/10.1109/icassp55912.2026.11463462): Bridges external or learned knowledge with visual features to improve deepfake detector generalization across manipulation sources.
- [FALCON-Net: Feature Aggregation of Local Patterns for AI-Generated Image Detection](https://doi.org/10.1109/tifs.2026.3683286): Aggregates local-pattern features for AI-generated image detection, emphasizing fine-grained artifacts rather than whole-image semantics alone.
- [NeurErase: Selective Deactivation of Neurons for Erasing Concepts in Diffusion Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/icassp55912.2026.11464730): Erases concepts by selectively deactivating neurons in diffusion models, providing a targeted safety mechanism without full retraining.
- [MAP-Mamba: Multi-Artifacts Perception Mamba for Generalizable Face Forgery Detection](https://doi.org/10.1109/tifs.2026.3651948): Uses a Mamba-based multi-artifact perception network to detect face forgeries across heterogeneous manipulation traces.
- [Refining Forgery-Aware Prompts for Deepfake Detection with Pattern Blended Samples](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.patcog.2026.113659): Refines forgery-aware prompts using pattern-blended samples so deepfake detectors learn more transferable manipulation cues.
- [Diffusion-Driven Forgery Detection: Distilling Latent Features for Generalized Image Forensics](https://doi.org/10.1109/lsp.2026.3687792): Distills latent features from diffusion-driven reconstruction for generalized image forensics, turning generative priors into detector signals.
- [RADI: A Retrieval-Augmented Dynamic In-Context Learning Framework for AIGC Image Detection](https://doi.org/10.1109/icassp55912.2026.11462412): Uses retrieval-augmented dynamic in-context learning for AIGC image detection, adapting evidence examples at inference time.
- [AdaParse: Personalized Fingerprinting for Visual Generative Model Reverse Engineering](https://doi.org/10.1109/tifs.2026.3671095): Builds personalized fingerprints for reverse-engineering visual generative models, linking outputs back to likely source models.
- [AICE: Three domain conversion network applied to all-in-one image inpainting and color enhancement task](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.eswa.2026.131558): Uses a three-domain conversion network for joint image inpainting and color enhancement, combining restoration and appearance correction in one model.
- [Dynamic Frequency Domain Curriculum Learning: A Novel Framework for Adaptive Image Forgery Detection](https://doi.org/10.1109/icassp55912.2026.11464347): Trains image-forgery detectors with a dynamic frequency-domain curriculum so the model sees artifact difficulty in a controlled progression.
- [Keypoint-enhanced image watermarking with spatial-frequency mapping and perceptual optimization](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.ins.2026.123500): Combines keypoint guidance, spatial-frequency mapping, and perceptual optimization for robust image watermarking.
- [FuLLaMa: Training-free Diffusion-based Object Removal with Context Preservation](https://doi.org/10.1109/wacv61042.2026.00826): Removes objects with a training-free diffusion procedure that preserves surrounding context rather than regenerating the whole scene.
- [Towards Data-Constrained Defect Inspection in Advanced Manufacturing: A Lightweight Diffusion Model Adapter for High-Fidelity Data Generation](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.eswa.2026.132452): Adds a lightweight diffusion adapter to generate high-fidelity defect data under limited manufacturing examples.
- [Toward Accurate Image Generation via Dynamic Generative Image Transformer](https://doi.org/10.1109/tpami.2026.3653620): Proposes a dynamic generative image transformer to improve image-generation accuracy through adaptive token or structure modeling.
- [Conversational Image Generation: Towards Multi-Round Personalized Generation with Multi-Modal Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/wacv61042.2026.00799): Uses multimodal language models to support multi-round personalized image generation, carrying user preferences across conversation turns.
- [A Few-Shot Learning Framework for Time-Varying Scientific Data Generation via Conditional Diffusion Model](https://doi.org/10.1109/tvcg.2026.3656934): Generates time-varying scientific data with conditional diffusion under few-shot constraints; this is visualization/scientific-data generation rather than ordinary image editing.
- [FreeMD: Training-free multi-domain text-to-image generation with any control.](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.neunet.2026.108823): Provides training-free multi-domain text-to-image generation with arbitrary control inputs, reducing reliance on domain-specific fine-tuning.
- [OPa-Ma: Text guided Mamba for 360-degree image out-painting](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.patcog.2026.113957): Applies a text-guided Mamba architecture to 360-degree image outpainting, extending scenes beyond the original panorama boundary.
- [VideoSketcher: A Training-Free Approach for Coherent Video Sketch Transfer](https://doi.org/10.1109/wacv61042.2026.00708): Transfers sketch style across video frames without training while maintaining temporal coherence.
- [S2I-DiT: Unlocking the semantic-to-image transferability by fine-tuning large diffusion transformer models](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.patcog.2026.113158): Fine-tunes large diffusion transformers to improve semantic-to-image transfer, aligning abstract semantic inputs with generated visual content.
- [Linear probing enables Ship-Radiated Noise recognition with pretrained audio embeddings](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.ecoinf.2026.103709): Uses pretrained audio embeddings for ship-radiated noise recognition; this is off-axis audio classification and should leave the image-generation page.
- [Dynamic Trajectory Diffusion Model for All-in-one Image Restoration](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.eswa.2026.132767): Models restoration as a dynamic diffusion trajectory for all-in-one image restoration across multiple degradation types.
- [Frequency-Enhanced and Conflict-Adaptive ODE Framework for Training-Free Consistent Video Editing](https://doi.org/10.1109/icassp55912.2026.11460542): Uses frequency enhancement and conflict-adaptive ODE updates for training-free video editing with stronger temporal consistency.
- [Multimodal structure-guided diffusion model for Magnetic Particle Imaging reconstruction](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.media.2026.104086): Applies multimodal structure-guided diffusion to magnetic particle imaging reconstruction; this is biomedical imaging reconstruction and should be reviewed outside this topic.
- [FreeAnimate: Training-Free Human Image Animation with Preview-Guided Denoising](https://doi.org/10.1109/icassp55912.2026.11462600): Animates human images without training by using preview-guided denoising to steer motion while preserving identity and appearance.
- [Towards Few-Shot Deepfake Detection with an Enhanced CLIP Model](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.neunet.2026.109027): Enhances CLIP for few-shot deepfake detection, adapting vision-language representations when only limited fake samples are available.
- [Beyond Chunk-level RAG: Hierarchical Document-aware Retrieval for Indian Legal Judgments](https://doi.org/10.1109/icdsaai69492.2026.11505138): Proposes hierarchical document-aware retrieval for Indian legal judgments; it is off-axis legal RAG rather than visual generation.
- [Line Art Colorization with Offset Prior-based Diffusion Model](https://doi.org/10.1109/wacv61042.2026.00540): Uses an offset-prior diffusion model for line-art colorization, improving color placement while respecting drawn contours.
- [Visual Keys to Symphonies: Latent Diffusion for Multi-Scene Video-to-Music Generation](https://doi.org/10.1109/icassp55912.2026.11462058): Uses latent diffusion to generate music from multi-scene video cues; this is cross-modal media generation but not image generation or editing.
- [ParaEdit: Unifying Parallel Transport and Generative Flows for High-Fidelity Image Editing](https://doi.org/10.1109/icassp55912.2026.11462013): Unifies parallel transport and generative flows to perform high-fidelity image edits along smoother latent trajectories.
- [Advanced Classification of AI-Generated Images Through Transformers](https://doi.org/10.1109/icict68280.2026.11510816): Applies transformer classifiers to AI-generated image detection, focusing on classification of synthetic visual artifacts.
- [Diff-AgentDOA: Diffusion-agent Model for Synthetic Data Generation in Shallow-water Target Direction of Arrival Estimation](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.inffus.2026.104371): Generates synthetic data for shallow-water direction-of-arrival estimation with a diffusion-agent model; this is off-axis signal-processing data generation.
- [Towards alleviating hallucination in text-to-image retrieval for CLIP in zero-shot learning](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.neucom.2026.132640): Mitigates hallucination in CLIP-based text-to-image retrieval for zero-shot learning, an adjacent retrieval topic rather than a generator or editor.
