- [TAIS-Net: Time adaptive implicit sampling diffusion model for arbitrary-scale UAV video super-resolution](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.isprsjprs.2026.04.060): Uses time-adaptive implicit sampling diffusion for arbitrary-scale UAV video super-resolution.
- [DoTA: Latent Distribution Conditioned Data Attribution for Diffusion Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/wacv61042.2026.00201): Conditions data attribution on latent distributions to identify which training data most influences diffusion-model outputs.
- [NeuroPatch: Lightweight diffusion model repair for backdoor attack mitigation based on neuron-level patching](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.neucom.2026.133283): Repairs backdoored diffusion models with neuron-level patching, relevant to image-generation model safety.
- [MASA: Query-Free Black-Box Adversarial Attack on Text-to-Image Generation Via Multi-Modal Adaptive Semantic Optimization](https://doi.org/10.1109/icassp55912.2026.11461721): Attacks text-to-image generation with query-free multimodal adaptive semantic optimization.
- [Panoptic Scene Graph Grounded Editing](https://doi.org/10.1049/cvi2.70069): Uses panoptic scene graphs and mutually exclusive attention manipulation for training-free image editing, fitting the image-editing Model section rather than embodied VLA.
- [Content-Style Decoupled Latent Diffusion](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41598-026-36407-3): Decouples content and style in latent diffusion for multi-style image generation.
- [Text-Conditioned Diffusion Image Generation](https://doi.org/10.1007/s11042-025-20990-0): Studies a diffusion pipeline conditioned directly on textual descriptions, focusing on how prompt encodings steer the denoising process for image synthesis.
- [PT-T2I/V](https://openreview.net/forum?id=lTrrnNdkOX): Uses proxy-tokenized diffusion transformers to share an efficient architecture across text-to-image and text-to-video generation.
- [AdaDiff: Adaptive Step Selection for Fast Diffusion Models](https://doi.org/10.1609/aaai.v39i9.33075): Learns when to skip or retain diffusion sampling steps, reducing inference cost while preserving generation quality.
- [Self-Corrected Flow Distillation for Consistent One-Step and Few-Step Image Generation](https://doi.org/10.1609/aaai.v39i3.32269): Corrects flow-distillation errors during training so one-step and few-step samplers produce more consistent images.
- [DesignEdit: Unify Spatial-Aware Image Editing via Training-free Inpainting with a Multi-Layered Latent Diffusion Framework](https://doi.org/10.1609/aaai.v39i4.32414): Decomposes an image into latent layers and uses training-free inpainting to localize spatial edits without retraining the base diffusion model.
- [Revealing Gender Bias from Prompt to Image in Stable Diffusion](https://doi.org/10.3390/jimaging11020035): Traces how gendered prompt terms change Stable Diffusion outputs, giving a model-behavior audit for bias in generated images.
- [ViPCap: Retrieval Text-Based Visual Prompts for Lightweight Image Captioning](https://doi.org/10.1609/aaai.v39i4.32454): Uses retrieved textual prompts as lightweight visual guidance for captioning; this is adjacent to generation and should be considered for a captioning or VLM file.
- [FaceSigns: Semi-fragile Watermarks for Media Authentication](https://doi.org/10.1145/3640466): Designs semi-fragile face watermarks that survive benign transformations but expose tampering, supporting authentication of generated or edited media.
- [Optimize & Reduce: A Top-Down Approach for Image Vectorization](https://doi.org/10.1609/aaai.v38i3.27987): Vectorizes raster images by iteratively optimizing and reducing primitives, connecting image synthesis with editable vector-graphics generation.
- [Multimodal Large Language Models Make Text-to-Image Generative Models Align Better](https://doi.org/10.52202/079017-2584): Uses multimodal LLM feedback to improve text-to-image alignment, replacing purely text-side prompt scoring with image-aware supervision.
- [Generative emulation of weather forecast ensembles with diffusion models](https://doi.org/10.1126/sciadv.adk4489): Applies diffusion models to weather-ensemble emulation rather than visual media generation, making it an off-axis diffusion-model candidate for scientific modeling.
- [From Outline to Detail: An Hierarchical End-to-end Framework for Coherent and Consistent Visual Novel Generation and Assembly](https://doi.org/10.1145/3746027.3755541): Generates visual novels in stages from outline to detailed scenes, coupling image generation with assembly constraints for coherent story pages.
- [PD-CBDM: Training Class-Balancing Diffusion Models with Perceptual Distinguish Loss](https://doi.org/10.3390/math14101576): Uses a perceptual distinguish loss in class-balancing diffusion training so minority-class samples remain separable and visually faithful.
- [Exposing the Guardrails: Reverse-Engineering and Jailbreaking Safety Filters in DALL·E Text-to-Image Pipelines](https://www.usenix.org/conference/usenixsecurity25/presentation/villa): Reverse-engineers DALL-E safety filters and jailbreak paths, documenting where prompt and image guardrails fail in text-to-image pipelines.
- [Robust deepfake detector against deep image watermarking](https://doi.org/10.1371/journal.pone.0338778): Evaluates deepfake detection under deep image watermarking, focusing on detector robustness when provenance marks alter forensic traces.
- [Toward Robust Deepfake Detection: A Proactive Method Based on Watermarking and Knowledge Distillation](https://doi.org/10.1145/3746027.3755621): Combines proactive watermarking with knowledge distillation to train deepfake detectors that remain effective after generated-media transformations.
- [Removing watermark from diffusion models via Low-Rank Adaptation](https://doi.org/10.3788/tfsr20250147): Studies LoRA-based removal of diffusion-model watermarks, treating fine-tuning itself as an attack surface for provenance systems.
- [Patch-based localized artifact enhancement for deepfake detection](https://doi.org/10.1117/12.3096373): Enhances localized artifact patches before classification so deepfake detectors can attend to small generation defects instead of global semantics.
- [Bi-mark: A copyright marking framework for AI-generative-text](https://doi.org/10.1145/3774949.3774970): Proposes copyright marking for AI-generated text, which is off-axis for this image-generation file and should be considered for text provenance coverage.
- [Efficient DeepFake Image Classification Using Lightweight MobileNetV4-Small Architecture](https://doi.org/10.5220/0013613400004664): Uses a compact MobileNetV4-Small classifier for deepfake image detection, emphasizing deployable generated-image forensics.
- [DiffBIR: Toward Blind Image Restoration with Generative Diffusion Prior](https://doi.org/10.1007/978-3-031-73202-7_25): Uses a generative diffusion prior for blind image restoration, separating degradation removal from high-fidelity content reconstruction.
- [Conditional neural field latent diffusion model for generating spatiotemporal turbulence](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41467-024-54712-1): Combines conditional neural fields with latent diffusion for turbulence simulation, an off-axis scientific generative model rather than image editing.
- [DreamIdentity: Enhanced Editability for Efficient Face-Identity Preserved Image Generation](https://doi.org/10.1609/aaai.v38i2.27891): Balances face identity preservation with prompt editability so personalized portrait generation can change attributes without identity drift.
- [Generative Diffusion for Regional Surrogate Models From Sea‐Ice Simulations](https://doi.org/10.1029/2024ms004395): Builds diffusion surrogate models for regional sea-ice simulations, making this a scientific-modeling diffusion entry rather than visual generation.
- [ColJailBreak: Collaborative Generation and Editing for Jailbreaking Text-to-Image Deep Generation](https://doi.org/10.52202/079017-1929): Uses collaborative generation and editing to find jailbreak prompts or images against text-to-image systems, highlighting safety risks in iterative visual creation.
- [AdaptiveSliders: User-aligned Semantic Slider-based Editing of Text-to-Image Model Output](https://doi.org/10.1145/3706598.3714292): Learns semantic sliders aligned to user preferences, giving controllable continuous edits over text-to-image outputs.
- [Frequency-Domain Masking and Spatial Interaction for Generalizable Deepfake Detection](https://doi.org/10.3390/electronics14071302): Combines frequency-domain masking with spatial interactions to improve deepfake detection across generators and manipulations.
- [Opportunities and challenges of diffusion models for generative AI](https://doi.org/10.1093/nsr/nwae348): Reviews diffusion-model strengths and limitations across generative AI, including controllability, efficiency, safety, and deployment challenges.
- [DeepFloyd-IF via diffusion and U-Net based cross-model attention for semantic coherence](https://doi.org/10.11591/eei.v15i2.9927): Places cross-model attention over a U-Net diffusion backbone to improve semantic coherence in DeepFloyd-IF style generation.
- [Rethinking Open-World DeepFake Attribution with Multi-perspective Sensory Learning](https://doi.org/10.1007/s11263-024-02184-7): Uses multi-perspective sensory features for open-world attribution, aiming to identify unseen deepfake sources rather than only closed-set generators.
- [Real Appearance Modeling for More General Deepfake Detection](https://doi.org/10.1007/978-3-031-72943-0_23): Models real-image appearance distributions so detectors learn what authentic content looks like before flagging synthetic deviations.
- [TreeReward: Improve Diffusion Model via Tree-Structured Feedback Learning](https://doi.org/10.1145/3664647.3680610): Organizes feedback in a tree structure for diffusion-model reward learning, allowing image quality and alignment signals to be propagated across related generations.
- [A novel flexible identity-net with diffusion models for painting-style generation](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41598-025-12434-4): Attaches an identity-aware network to diffusion-based painting-style generation so stylization can preserve subject identity.
- [Diffusion Models for Recommender Systems: From Content Distribution To Content Creation](https://doi.org/10.1145/3711896.3736554): Discusses diffusion models in recommender systems, including content creation, but is broader than image-generation model work.
- [FAST: Flexibly Controllable Arbitrary Style Transfer via Latent Diffusion Models](https://doi.org/10.1145/3748655): Uses latent diffusion for arbitrary style transfer with flexible controls over style strength and target content preservation.
- [VectorWeaver: Transformers-Based Diffusion Model for Vector Graphics Generation](https://doi.org/10.5220/0013185100003912): Generates vector graphics with a Transformer-based diffusion model, shifting synthesis from pixels to editable graphical primitives.
- [AlignTex: Pixel-Precise Texture Generation from Multi-view Artwork](https://doi.org/10.1145/3731158): Generates textures from multi-view artwork with pixel-level alignment, targeting consistent texture synthesis across views.
- [EditMaster: Bridging Text instruction and Visual Example for Multimodal guided Image Editing](https://doi.org/10.1145/3746027.3754926): Combines text instructions with visual examples so image edits can follow both linguistic commands and reference appearance.
- [LoCo: Training-Free Layout-to-Image Synthesis with Localized Constraints](https://doi.org/10.1145/3746027.3754905): Enforces localized layout constraints at inference time, enabling layout-to-image synthesis without training a new control model.
- [ENGDM: Enhanced Non-Isotropic Gaussian Diffusion Model for Progressive Image Editing](https://doi.org/10.3390/s25102970): Uses non-isotropic Gaussian diffusion to progressively edit images, shaping the noise process toward directional or region-specific changes.
- [U3D: Unified Landmark-Displacement Framework for Real-Time Multi-Modal Emotion-Controllable 3D Facial Animation](https://doi.org/10.5220/0014427100004084): Controls 3D facial animation through landmark displacement and multimodal emotion inputs; this is closer to face animation than still-image generation.
- [4DVD: Cascaded Dense-view Video Diffusion Model for High-quality 4D Content Generation](https://doi.org/10.1007/s11263-026-02811-5): Cascades dense-view video diffusion stages to synthesize high-quality 4D content with view and temporal consistency.
- [High-Fidelity Human Image Animation: Preserving Identity and Pose Consistency](https://doi.org/10.1007/s11760-026-05195-8): Animates human images while constraining identity and pose consistency, sitting at the image-animation boundary.
- [Identity and structure preserving face editing via diffusion models](https://doi.org/10.1007/s00530-025-02183-9): Edits faces with diffusion while preserving identity and structural geometry, reducing common drift in local facial changes.
- [UPD-Diff: a unified precipitation downscaling method based on multi-stream elucidating diffusion model](https://doi.org/10.1088/2632-2153/ae4da6): Uses multi-stream elucidated diffusion for precipitation downscaling, an off-axis geoscience diffusion application.
- [Mhdaunet: enhancing semantic consistency in diffusion models via dual-path noise alignment](https://doi.org/10.1007/s00138-026-01798-w): Aligns noise through dual paths to improve semantic consistency in diffusion outputs.
- [A Dual-UNet Diffusion Framework for Personalized Panoramic Generation](https://doi.org/10.3390/jimaging12010040): Uses a dual-UNet diffusion architecture to generate personalized panoramas while preserving subject or scene cues across the wide field of view.
- [Accelerating Multi-Elemental Catalyst Discovery with Interpretable Machine Learning and Automated Experimentation](https://doi.org/10.1021/acsnano.5c20552): Applies interpretable machine learning and automated experiments to catalyst discovery, not image generation or editing.
- [Modeling and Robust Control for Power Insurance Claim Collaborative Robots Using 3D Gaussian Splash Reconstruction](https://doi.org/10.1088/1742-6596/3062/1/012005): Uses 3D Gaussian reconstruction for robot modeling and control in insurance-claim scenarios, making it off-axis for this still-image model file.
- [Can AI Influence How We Remember? Revisiting Memory Through AI-Generated Visuals](https://doi.org/10.1145/3786995.3787032): Studies how AI-generated visuals affect human memory, providing human-subject evidence around generated images rather than a new generator.
- [Degradation-Aware One-Step Diffusion Model for Content-Sensitive Super-Resolution in the Dark](https://doi.org/10.1145/3746027.3755853): Conditions one-step diffusion super-resolution on degradation and content cues to restore dark images with low latency.
- [Fine-tuning Bias Neurons for Fair Text-to-Image Generation](https://doi.org/10.1145/3746027.3755047): Identifies and fine-tunes bias-sensitive neurons in text-to-image models to reduce unfair demographic associations.
- [SRConvNet: A Transformer-Style ConvNet for Lightweight Image Super-Resolution](https://doi.org/10.1007/s11263-024-02147-y): Builds a lightweight ConvNet with Transformer-style blocks for efficient single-image super-resolution.
- [Single image super-resolution with denoising diffusion GANS](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41598-024-52370-3): Combines denoising diffusion with GAN training for single-image super-resolution, aiming for sharper reconstructions than regression-only restorers.
- [EvilEdit: Backdooring Text-to-Image Diffusion Models in One Second](https://doi.org/10.1145/3664647.3680689): Injects fast backdoors into text-to-image diffusion models, showing how model editing can create malicious trigger-driven generations.
- [MuST: Robust Image Watermarking for Multi-Source Tracing](https://doi.org/10.1609/aaai.v38i6.28344): Embeds robust watermarks that distinguish multiple image sources, supporting attribution when generated or edited images are redistributed.
- [SDGAN: Disentangling Semantic Manipulation for Facial Attribute Editing](https://doi.org/10.1609/aaai.v38i3.28012): Disentangles facial semantics in a GAN so attribute edits can change target traits while preserving unrelated identity information.
- [PSC diffusion: patch-based simplified conditional diffusion model for low-light image enhancement](https://doi.org/10.1007/s00530-024-01391-z): Uses patch-based conditional diffusion for low-light enhancement, improving local illumination restoration without full-image overcorrection.
- [Camera Settings as Tokens: Modeling Photography on Latent Diffusion Models](https://doi.org/10.1145/3680528.3687635): Represents camera settings as tokens in latent diffusion, enabling generation controlled by exposure, focal length, and other photographic parameters.
- [Few-Shot Diffusion Models Escape the Curse of Dimensionality](https://doi.org/10.52202/079017-2189): Provides theory and experiments showing how few-shot diffusion estimation can avoid dimension-dependent sample complexity under structural assumptions.
- [L-DiffER: Single Image Reflection Removal with Language-Based Diffusion Model](https://doi.org/10.1007/978-3-031-72661-3_4): Uses language-guided diffusion to remove reflections from a single image, linking restoration targets to textual degradation descriptions.
- [Menstrual cycle inspired latent diffusion model for image augmentation in energy production](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41598-025-99088-4): Applies a biologically inspired latent diffusion augmentation scheme to energy-production data, making it a domain-specific diffusion use rather than visual generation.
- [A Comparative Analysis of Compression and Transfer Learning Techniques in DeepFake Detection Models](https://doi.org/10.3390/math13050887): Compares compression and transfer-learning choices for deepfake detectors, clarifying which compact models retain forensic performance.
- [Theory of Consistency Diffusion Models: Distribution Estimation Meets Fast Sampling](https://proceedings.mlr.press/v235/dou24a.html): Analyzes consistency diffusion models as distribution estimators, connecting their fast sampling behavior to statistical guarantees.
- [EvilPromptFuzzer: generating inappropriate content based on text-to-image models](https://doi.org/10.1186/s42400-024-00279-9): Fuzzes text prompts to elicit inappropriate text-to-image outputs, providing an automated safety-testing method for visual generators.
- [EGGen: Image Generation with Multi-entity Prior Learning through Entity Guidance](https://doi.org/10.1145/3664647.3680898): Learns multi-entity priors and entity guidance so text-to-image generation handles several objects and their relations more reliably.
- [DRIP: Unleashing Diffusion Priors for Joint Foreground and Alpha Prediction in Image Matting](https://doi.org/10.52202/079017-2537): Uses diffusion priors to jointly estimate foreground appearance and alpha mattes, improving image matting as a generative restoration task.
- [Improving Image Editing Models with Generative Data Refinement](https://openreview.net/forum?id=q5UrA58oyY): Refines generated editing data before training image editors, targeting cleaner instruction-image pairs for downstream edit fidelity.
- [Trajectory anomaly detection via spatial–temporal complementary reconstruction diffusion model](https://doi.org/10.1007/s10707-026-00574-2): Applies diffusion reconstruction to trajectory anomaly detection, which is outside image generation despite the diffusion terminology.
- [Shaping scientific papers diffusion structure: academia and public in social media networks](https://doi.org/10.1007/s11192-025-05511-y): Studies diffusion of scientific papers in social networks, not diffusion generative models; it should be removed from this model page in a taxonomy pass.
- [AI Tool for Room Decoration: Harnessing Diffusion Model for Interior Design](https://doi.org/10.1145/3731763.3731792): Applies diffusion generation to room-decoration concepts, turning interior-design constraints into synthesized visual layout proposals.
- [Spatial transcriptomics data imputation based on diffusion models](https://doi.org/10.1117/12.3095397): Uses diffusion models for spatial-transcriptomics imputation, an off-axis biomedical data reconstruction entry.
- [Stylized image generation based on multi-attribute decomposition](https://doi.org/10.1007/s10044-025-01577-9): Decomposes multiple style attributes before generation, enabling finer control over stylized image synthesis.
- [SCOL: Style Code Orchestration in Latent Space for Proactive Face-Swapping Defense](https://doi.org/10.1145/3746027.3755344): Orchestrates latent style codes to proactively defend against face swapping, framing face-swap robustness as controllable representation editing.
- [Expressive Image Generation and Editing with Rich Text](https://doi.org/10.1007/s11263-025-02361-2): Conditions generation and editing on rich text formatting, letting typography-like cues and detailed language control visual appearance.
- [SAWNA: Space-Aware Text to Image Generation](https://doi.org/10.1145/3721250.3743023): Injects spatial awareness into text-to-image generation so object placement and scene layout follow prompt relations more faithfully.
- [Mitigating Stereotypes in Text-to-Image Generation: A Novel Perspective of Selective Neural Suppression](https://doi.org/10.1145/3746027.3755293): Suppresses selected neural directions associated with stereotypes, reducing biased visual associations in text-to-image outputs.
- [MPPR: Memory-Prior-based Prompt Refinement in Continuous Space for Advanced Text-to-Image Generation](https://doi.org/10.1145/3746027.3755280): Refines prompts in continuous embedding space with memory priors, improving text-to-image generation without relying only on discrete prompt rewriting.
- [PolyArt: Customizable Multilingual Movie Poster Generation via Diffusion Transformer](https://doi.org/10.1145/3757374.3771431): Uses a diffusion transformer for multilingual movie-poster generation, combining text rendering, layout, and style control.
- [Large-scale traceable and robust watermarking for diffusion models with precise affine coupling flow encoding](https://doi.org/10.1007/s10489-026-07217-x): Encodes traceable watermarks through affine-coupling flows inside diffusion models, targeting robustness and source attribution at large scale.
- [HiDream-I1: An Open-Source High-Efficient Image Generative Foundation Model](https://doi.org/10.1145/3746027.3756870): Introduces HiDream-I1 as an open efficient image-generation foundation model balancing released weights, image quality, and inference cost.
- [SSAIM: Not All Self-Attentions Contain Effective Spatial Structure in Diffusion Models for Text-to-Image Editing](https://doi.org/10.1145/3746027.3754904): Introduces an image generation or editing method; core idea: improve controllability, safety, fidelity, or model-side training for diffusion-style visual generation.
- [DSPO: Direct Score Preference Optimization for Diffusion Model Alignment](https://openreview.net/forum?id=xyfb9HHvMe): Distills the score function of human-preferred image distributions into T2I diffusion models, aligning fine-tuning with score matching instead of directly porting LLM-style DPO.
- [DiffKGW: Stealthy and Robust Diffusion Model Watermarking](https://www.semanticscholar.org/paper/04fc3d110c1621a42215791e4dd01c02ae1d2a3c): DiffKGW adds an image-generation or image-editing model around visual synthesis, watermarking, concept erasure, or generated-media detection.
